# PATH: README.md
# SIZE: 2.76 KB
# MODIFIED: 08/14/2026 23:28:31
################################################################

# TTEA

Personal job-market aggregation and matching prototype.

## V0.1 scope

The first dry run deliberately uses only a few small, low-friction collectors:

- Jobicy public remote-jobs API
- Remotive public remote-jobs API
- Arbeitnow public job-board API
- Keejob public HTML category pages, only when robots.txt allows the request

The pipeline:

1. fetches a maximum of 100 raw listings;
2. stores raw snapshots under `data/raw/`;
3. normalizes each source into one `JobRecord` model;
4. scores technical, eligibility, location and experience fit separately;
5. deduplicates canonical jobs while preserving every source URL;
6. stores all jobs in SQLite, including poor matches;
7. marks postings older than 45 days as stale when a publication date exists;
8. exports ranked live jobs to `data/exports/top_matches.csv`.

No API keys, paid AI model, account logins, CAPTCHA bypass, Playwright, or automated job applications are used in this first version.

## Run again

Open PowerShell in the project folder:

```powershell
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m src.main dry-run --limit 100
```

Normal runs respect each source's configured minimum poll interval.

## Tests

```powershell
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest -q
```

## Database

SQLite database: `data/jobs.db`

Main tables:
- `jobs`
- `job_sources`

Every job begins with `application_status = discovered`.

## Next development phase

After this smoke test is stable, add source families separately:
- Tunisia: TanitJobs, Fabskill, company career sites and additional local boards.
- ATS: Greenhouse, Lever, Ashby, SmartRecruiters, Workable, Teamtailor, Workday.
- Discovery: search-engine discovery and company-domain enumeration.
- JavaScript pages: Playwright, only where needed and without CAPTCHA/access-control bypass.
- UI: local/desktop dashboard first, remotely accessible dashboard after the data layer is stable.
## Visual dashboard

V0.2 adds a local FastAPI dashboard backed directly by `data/jobs.db`.

Launch it like a desktop app:

```powershell
.\scripts\start_dashboard_app.ps1
```

Or keep it in a normal browser:

```powershell
.\scripts\start_dashboard.ps1
```

Local URL: `http://127.0.0.1:8765`

The dashboard provides:
- live market metrics and top matches;
- searchable/filterable job inventory;
- job detail view with technical/eligibility/location/experience score breakdown;
- application status, notes and CV-version editing;
- application board;
- collector/source coverage and cooldown status;
- UI-launched collection runs with source-by-source progress and live logs;
- run history;
- read-only profile and ranking configuration overview.

`start_dashboard_lan.ps1` exposes the same dashboard on the local network. It has no authentication, so it must not be port-forwarded or exposed directly to the public internet.

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
from PIL import Image

PROJECT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
BRAND = PROJECT / "assets" / "branding"
UI = PROJECT / "ui"

source = BRAND / "ttea-logo-master.png"
app_logo = BRAND / "ttea-logo.png"
icon = BRAND / "ttea.ico"
ui_logo = UI / "ttea-logo.png"
ui_icon = UI / "favicon.ico"
touch = UI / "apple-touch-icon.png"

img = Image.open(source).convert("RGBA")

# Preserve the whole artwork. If a future logo is non-square, pad it instead
# of cropping it so no branding is lost.
side = max(img.size)
canvas = Image.new("RGBA", (side, side), (0, 0, 0, 0))
x = (side - img.width) // 2
y = (side - img.height) // 2
canvas.alpha_composite(img, (x, y))

def resized(size: int) -> Image.Image:
    return canvas.resize((size, size), Image.Resampling.LANCZOS)

resized(512).save(app_logo, "PNG", optimize=True)
resized(512).save(ui_logo, "PNG", optimize=True)
resized(180).save(touch, "PNG", optimize=True)

# Pillow writes the complete Windows multi-resolution ICO when sizes are
# supplied. This is used by the Desktop shortcut and is also copied as favicon.
base = resized(256)
base.save(
    icon,
    format="ICO",
    sizes=[
        (16, 16),
        (24, 24),
        (32, 32),
        (48, 48),
        (64, 64),
        (128, 128),
        (256, 256),
    ],
)
ui_icon.write_bytes(icon.read_bytes())

print(f"Master source : {source}")
print(f"App PNG       : {app_logo}")
print(f"Windows ICO   : {icon}")
print(f"UI logo       : {ui_logo}")
print(f"Favicon       : {ui_icon}")
print(f"Touch icon    : {touch}")

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import json
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path

from playwright.async_api import async_playwright, TimeoutError as PlaywrightTimeoutError

PROJECT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
DASHBOARD = "http://127.0.0.1:8765"

desktop = Path.home() / "OneDrive" / "Desktop"
if not desktop.exists():
    desktop = Path.home() / "Desktop"

stamp = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
OUTPUT = desktop / f"TTEA_UI_Audit_{stamp}"
OUTPUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

VIEWPORT = {"width": 1600, "height": 1000}


async def settle(page, delay=650):
    # networkidle can remain noisy because the Runs screen may poll.
    try:
        await page.wait_for_load_state("domcontentloaded")
    except Exception:
        pass
    await page.wait_for_timeout(delay)


async def screenshot(page, filename: str):
    path = OUTPUT / filename
    await settle(page)
    await page.screenshot(
        path=str(path),
        full_page=True,
        animations="disabled",
    )
    print(f"CAPTURED  {path.name}")


async def click_view(page, view: str, expected_id: str):
    await page.locator(f'.nav-item[data-view="{view}"]').click()
    await page.locator(expected_id).wait_for(state="visible", timeout=8000)
    await settle(page, 750)


async def launch_browser(p):
    # Prefer the user's installed Microsoft Edge so no browser binary download
    # is required. Fall back to common executable paths if channel discovery
    # is unavailable.
    try:
        return await p.chromium.launch(channel="msedge", headless=True)
    except Exception as first:
        candidates = [
            Path(r"C:\Program Files (x86)\Microsoft\Edge\Application\msedge.exe"),
            Path(r"C:\Program Files\Microsoft\Edge\Application\msedge.exe"),
            Path(r"C:\Program Files\Google\Chrome\Application\chrome.exe"),
            Path(r"C:\Program Files (x86)\Google\Chrome\Application\chrome.exe"),
        ]
        for candidate in candidates:
            if candidate.exists():
                return await p.chromium.launch(
                    executable_path=str(candidate),
                    headless=True,
                )
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Could not launch Microsoft Edge or Chrome for screenshot capture."
        ) from first


async def main():
    async with async_playwright() as p:
        browser = await launch_browser(p)
        context = await browser.new_context(
            viewport=VIEWPORT,
            device_scale_factor=1,
            color_scheme="light",
        )
        page = await context.new_page()

        console_errors = []
        page.on(
            "console",
            lambda msg: console_errors.append(msg.text)
            if msg.type == "error"
            else None,
        )

        await page.goto(f"{DASHBOARD}/?audit={stamp}", wait_until="domcontentloaded")
        await page.locator("#connectionText").wait_for(timeout=10000)
        await settle(page, 1000)

        # Disable transitions after the first render to make captures stable.
        await page.add_style_tag(
            content="""
                *, *::before, *::after {
                    animation-duration: 0s !important;
                    transition-duration: 0s !important;
                    caret-color: transparent !important;
                }
            """
        )

        # 01 - Overview
        await click_view(page, "overview", "#overviewSnapshot")
        await screenshot(page, "01_overview.png")

        # 02 - Jobs register
        await click_view(page, "jobs", "#jobsTable")
        await screenshot(page, "02_jobs.png")

        # 03 - First real job detail state
        first_job = page.locator('#jobsTable [data-job-id]').first
        if await first_job.count():
            await first_job.click()
            await page.locator("#jobModal:not(.hidden)").wait_for(
                state="visible",
                timeout=8000,
            )
            await settle(page, 650)
            await screenshot(page, "03_job_detail.png")
            await page.locator("#closeJobModal").click()
            await settle(page, 250)
        else:
            print("SKIPPED   03_job_detail.png (no job row found)")

        # 04 - Application workflow
        await click_view(page, "applications", "#applicationBoard")
        await screenshot(page, "04_applications.png")

        # 05 - Source register
        await click_view(page, "sources", "#sourcesTable")
        await screenshot(page, "05_sources.png")

        # 06 - Current run / collector control
        await click_view(page, "pipeline", "#runLogs")
        await screenshot(page, "06_runs.png")

        # 07 - Profile / scoring rules
        await click_view(page, "profile", "#profileSummary")
        await screenshot(page, "07_profile.png")

        # Return to Overview and capture only the fixed frame/top-level shell.
        await click_view(page, "overview", "#overviewSnapshot")

        manifest = {
            "captured_at": datetime.now().isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
            "dashboard": DASHBOARD,
            "viewport": VIEWPORT,
            "captures": [
                "01_overview.png",
                "02_jobs.png",
                "03_job_detail.png",
                "04_applications.png",
                "05_sources.png",
                "06_runs.png",
                "07_profile.png",
            ],
            "review_order": [
                "1. Functional purpose / question answered",
                "2. Missing actions or information",
                "3. Redundant or misplaced information",
                "4. Interaction / workflow problems",
                "5. Visual hierarchy",
                "6. Vintage / analog visual refinement",
            ],
            "browser_console_errors": console_errors,
        }
        (OUTPUT / "audit_manifest.json").write_text(
            json.dumps(manifest, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False),
            encoding="utf-8",
        )

        await browser.close()

    print()
    print(f"AUDIT_FOLDER={OUTPUT}")
    print(f"CAPTURE_COUNT={len(list(OUTPUT.glob('*.png')))}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    asyncio.run(main())

# FILE 11 / 68

# PATH: scripts\rescore_existing.py

# MODIFIED: 08/14/2026 23:16:55

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
if str(ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

import yaml
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, selectinload

from src.database import JobORM, get_engine
from src.main import export_top_matches, show_top_jobs
from src.matcher import score_job
from src.models import JobRecord


def main() -> int:
    profile = json.loads((ROOT / "config" / "profile.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    settings = yaml.safe_load((ROOT / "config" / "settings.yaml").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    db_path = ROOT / settings.get("storage", {}).get("sqlite_path", "data/jobs.db")
    engine = get_engine(str(db_path))

    changed = 0
    with Session(engine) as session:
        jobs = session.scalars(select(JobORM).options(selectinload(JobORM.sources))).all()
        for job in jobs:
            source = job.sources[0] if job.sources else None
            record = JobRecord(
                title=job.title,
                company=job.company,
                location=job.location,
                description=job.description,
                employment_type=job.employment_type,
                remote=job.remote,
                remote_region=job.remote_region,
                seniority=job.seniority,
                min_years=job.min_years,
                max_years=job.max_years,
                salary_min=job.salary_min,
                salary_max=job.salary_max,
                salary_currency=job.salary_currency,
                salary_period=job.salary_period,
                posted_at=job.posted_at,
                source_name=source.source_name if source else "stored",
                source_url=source.source_url if source else (job.canonical_url or f"stored:{job.id}"),
            )
            scores = score_job(record, profile, settings)
            job.seniority = record.seniority
            job.employment_type = record.employment_type
            job.min_years = record.min_years
            job.max_years = record.max_years
            job.technical_score = scores.technical
            job.eligibility_score = scores.eligibility
            job.location_score = scores.location
            job.experience_score = scores.experience
            job.overall_score = scores.overall
            job.score_explanation_json = json.dumps(scores.explanations, ensure_ascii=False)
            changed += 1
        session.commit()

    export_path = ROOT / settings.get("storage", {}).get("export_top_matches_csv", "data/exports/top_matches.csv")
    exported = export_top_matches(engine, export_path)
    print(f"Rescored {changed} stored canonical jobs.")
    print(f"Exported {exported} live jobs to {export_path}.")
    show_top_jobs(engine)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

if (-not (Test-Path $Python)) {
    throw "Virtual environment not found at $Python"
}
Push-Location $Root
try {
    & $Python -m src.main dry-run --limit 100
    exit $LASTEXITCODE
}
finally {
    Pop-Location
}

from __future__ import annotations

import sys
from pathlib import Path

# When Python directly executes a file inside scripts/, sys.path[0] points to
# scripts/ rather than the project root. Explicitly insert the project root
# so `import src...` works no matter where PowerShell was launched from.
PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
project_root_text = str(PROJECT_ROOT)
if project_root_text not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, project_root_text)

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from src.ui_server import app

client = TestClient(app)

checks = [
    ("/", 200),
    ("/assets/styles.css", 200),
    ("/assets/app.js", 200),
    ("/api/health", 200),
    ("/api/overview", 200),
    ("/api/jobs?page_size=5", 200),
    ("/api/sources", 200),
    ("/api/profile", 200),
    ("/api/run/status", 200),
    ("/api/run/history", 200),
]

for path, expected in checks:
    response = client.get(path)
    if response.status_code != expected:
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"{path}: expected HTTP {expected}, got "
            f"{response.status_code}: {response.text[:500]}"
        )
    print(f"OK  {response.status_code}  {path}")

overview_response = client.get("/api/overview")
overview_response.raise_for_status()
overview = overview_response.json()
metrics = overview.get("metrics", {})

print()
print(
    "API OK | "
    f"total={metrics.get('total_jobs', 0)} "
    f"live={metrics.get('live_jobs', 0)} "
    f"strong={metrics.get('strong_matches', 0)} "
    f"active_pipeline={metrics.get('active_pipeline', 0)}"
)

from __future__ import annotations

import sys
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
if str(ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from src.ui_server import app

c = TestClient(app)

checks = [
    "/api/health",
    "/api/overview",
    "/api/jobs?page_size=100&freshness=all",
    "/api/jobs?page_size=20&review=unreviewed",
    "/api/jobs?page_size=20&verdict=blocked",
    "/api/applications",
    "/api/sources",
    "/api/profile",
    "/api/run/status",
    "/api/run/history",
]

payloads = {}
for path in checks:
    r = c.get(path)
    if r.status_code != 200:
        raise RuntimeError(f"{path} -> {r.status_code}: {r.text[:500]}")
    payloads[path] = r.json()
    print(f"OK  {r.status_code}  {path}")

health = payloads["/api/health"]
assert health["version"] == "0.4.1", health

sources = payloads["/api/sources"]["items"]
assert len(sources) >= 20
assert any(x.get("state") == "active" for x in sources)
assert any(x.get("state") == "planned" for x in sources)

jobs = payloads["/api/jobs?page_size=100&freshness=all"]["items"]

for job in jobs:
    location = (job.get("location") or "").lower()
    company = (job.get("company") or "").lower()

    if "sousse" in location or "zaghouan" in location:
        assert job["verdict"] == "blocked", job
        assert job["scores"]["location"] == 0, job

    assert company not in {
        "informatique / tÃƒÂ©lÃƒÂ©coms",
        "informatique / telecoms",
        "banque / finance / assurances",
    }, job

print()
print(
    f"V0.4.1 API OK | version={health['version']} "
    f"sources={len(sources)} jobs_checked={len(jobs)}"
)

from __future__ import annotations

import sys
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
if str(ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from src.ui_server import app

client = TestClient(app)

health = client.get("/api/health")
health.raise_for_status()
assert health.json()["version"] == "0.4.3"

review = client.get(
    "/api/jobs?page_size=100&review=unreviewed&verdict=reviewable&freshness=live"
)
review.raise_for_status()
review_jobs = review.json()["items"]
assert all(job["verdict"] != "blocked" for job in review_jobs)

all_jobs = client.get("/api/jobs?page_size=100&freshness=all")
all_jobs.raise_for_status()
jobs = all_jobs.json()["items"]

now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
for job in jobs:
    posted = job.get("posted_at")
    if posted:
        dt = datetime.fromisoformat(posted.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
        assert dt <= now + timedelta(days=1), job

    if job["title"] == "Technicien Maintenance Smartphone":
        assert job["company"] == "FLASH PHONE", job
        assert (job.get("posted_at") or "").startswith("2026-08-11"), job

    if job["title"] == "Ing\u00e9nieur infrastructure":
        assert job["company"] == "TUNISIE LEASING ET FACTORING", job
        assert (job.get("posted_at") or "").startswith("2026-08-13"), job

sources = client.get("/api/sources")
sources.raise_for_status()
assert all("priority" in row for row in sources.json()["items"])

print(
    f"V0.4.3 API OK | review_queue={len(review_jobs)} "
    f"jobs={len(jobs)} sources={len(sources.json()['items'])}"
)

from __future__ import annotations

import sys
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
if str(ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from src.ui_server import app


def as_aware_utc(value: str) -> datetime:
    dt = datetime.fromisoformat(value.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
    if dt.tzinfo is None:
        return dt.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    return dt.astimezone(timezone.utc)


client = TestClient(app)

paths = [
    "/api/health",
    "/api/overview",
    "/api/jobs?page_size=100&freshness=all",
    "/api/jobs?page_size=100&review=unreviewed&verdict=reviewable&freshness=live",
    "/api/applications",
    "/api/sources",
    "/api/profile",
    "/api/run/status",
    "/api/run/history",
]

payloads = {}

for path in paths:
    response = client.get(path)
    if response.status_code != 200:
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"{path} -> HTTP {response.status_code}: {response.text[:500]}"
        )
    payloads[path] = response.json()
    print(f"OK  {response.status_code}  {path}")

health = payloads["/api/health"]
assert health["version"] == "0.4.3", health

all_jobs = payloads["/api/jobs?page_size=100&freshness=all"]["items"]
reviewable = payloads[
    "/api/jobs?page_size=100&review=unreviewed&verdict=reviewable&freshness=live"
]["items"]

assert all(job["verdict"] != "blocked" for job in reviewable), reviewable

now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
future_limit = now + timedelta(days=1)

flash_phone = None
leasing_job = None

for job in all_jobs:
    posted_at = job.get("posted_at")
    if posted_at:
        assert as_aware_utc(posted_at) <= future_limit, job

    if job.get("company") == "FLASH PHONE":
        flash_phone = job

    if job.get("company") == "TUNISIE LEASING ET FACTORING":
        leasing_job = job

assert flash_phone is not None, "FLASH PHONE job not found"
assert (flash_phone.get("posted_at") or "").startswith("2026-08-11"), flash_phone

assert leasing_job is not None, "TUNISIE LEASING ET FACTORING job not found"
assert (leasing_job.get("posted_at") or "").startswith("2026-08-13"), leasing_job
assert leasing_job["verdict"] == "actionable", leasing_job

sources = payloads["/api/sources"]["items"]
assert len(sources) >= 20
assert all("priority" in source for source in sources)
assert any(source.get("state") == "active" for source in sources)
assert any(source.get("state") == "planned" for source in sources)

print()
print(
    "V0.4.3 FINAL SMOKE OK | "
    f"jobs={len(all_jobs)} "
    f"review_queue={len(reviewable)} "
    f"sources={len(sources)}"
)

for job in jobs:

    None,
)

    "/api/jobs?page_size=100&review=unreviewed&verdict=reviewable&freshness=live"
)

items = review.json()["items"]

titles = {item["title"] for item in items}
for blocked_title in [
    "Customer Support Team Leader - Bulgarian Language",
    "Customer Support Team Leader â€“ Bulgarian Language",
    "Inside Sales Contractor",
    "Business Development Representative",
]:
    assert blocked_title not in titles, (blocked_title, titles)

all_jobs.raise_for_status()
all_items = all_jobs.json()["items"]

def find_contains(needle):
    return next(
        (
            item for item in all_items
            if needle.lower() in item["title"].lower()
        ),
        None,
    )

bulgarian = find_contains("Bulgarian Language")
if bulgarian:
    assert bulgarian["verdict"] == "blocked", bulgarian

inside_sales = find_contains("Inside Sales Contractor")
if inside_sales:
    assert inside_sales["verdict"] == "blocked", inside_sales

bdr = find_contains("Business Development Representative")
if bdr:
    assert bdr["verdict"] == "blocked", bdr

    "V0.4.5 FINAL MATCHING SMOKE OK | "
    f"review_queue={len(items)}"

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

$ProjectPath  = Split-Path -Parent $PSScriptRoot
$ServerScript = Join-Path $ProjectPath "scripts\start_dashboard.ps1"
$Url = "http://127.0.0.1:8765"

Set-Location $ProjectPath

$AlreadyRunning = $false
try {
    $r = Invoke-WebRequest -UseBasicParsing -Uri "$Url/api/health" -TimeoutSec 1
    $AlreadyRunning = ($r.StatusCode -eq 200)
} catch {}

if (-not $AlreadyRunning) {
    Start-Process powershell.exe -WorkingDirectory $ProjectPath -ArgumentList @(
        "-ExecutionPolicy", "Bypass",
        "-NoExit",
        "-File", $ServerScript
    )

    for ($i = 0; $i -lt 40; $i++) {
        try {
            $r = Invoke-WebRequest -UseBasicParsing -Uri "$Url/api/health" -TimeoutSec 1
            if ($r.StatusCode -eq 200) {
                $AlreadyRunning = $true
                break
            }
        } catch {}
        Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 350
    }
}

if (-not $AlreadyRunning) {
    throw "The dashboard server did not become ready at $Url."
}

$EdgeCandidates = @(
    "$env:ProgramFiles(x86)\Microsoft\Edge\Application\msedge.exe",
    "$env:ProgramFiles\Microsoft\Edge\Application\msedge.exe"
)

$ChromeCandidates = @(
    "$env:ProgramFiles\Google\Chrome\Application\chrome.exe",
    "$env:ProgramFiles(x86)\Google\Chrome\Application\chrome.exe"
)

$BrowserExe = (
    $EdgeCandidates + $ChromeCandidates |
    Where-Object { Test-Path $_ } |
    Select-Object -First 1
)

if ($BrowserExe) {
    Start-Process $BrowserExe -ArgumentList "--app=$Url"
} else {
    Start-Process $Url
}

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path

import yaml

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
settings_path = ROOT / 'config' / 'settings.yaml'
profile_path = ROOT / 'config' / 'profile.json'

settings = yaml.safe_load(settings_path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')) or {}
settings.setdefault('project', {})['version'] = '0.4.0'
matching = settings.setdefault('matching', {})
matching.setdefault('overall_weights', {'technical': 0.45, 'eligibility': 0.20, 'location': 0.20, 'experience': 0.15})
matching['ranking_caps'] = {
    'non_target_title': 25,
    'international_onsite': 15,
    'remote_geo_incompatible': 30,
    'hard_eligibility': 15,
    'senior_title': 30,
    'three_years': 40,
    'four_plus_years': 25,
    'weak_technical': 30,
}
settings_path.write_text(yaml.safe_dump(settings, allow_unicode=True, sort_keys=False), encoding='utf-8')

profile = json.loads(profile_path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
tracker = profile.setdefault('application_tracker', {})
tracker['review_statuses'] = ['unreviewed', 'shortlisted', 'dismissed']
tracker['statuses'] = [
    'not_started', 'application_preparing', 'ready_to_apply', 'applied', 'interview',
    'technical_test', 'hr_interview', 'offer', 'rejected', 'withdrawn', 'expired'
]
profile['language_policy'] = {
    'mandatory_unknown_language': 'exclude',
    'optional_unknown_language': 'keep',
}
profile_path.write_text(json.dumps(profile, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
print('Configured V0.4 matching caps, workflow statuses and language policy.')

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
import time
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
if str(ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

import yaml
from sqlalchemy import func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, selectinload

from src.collectors.keejob import KeejobCollector
from src.database import JobORM, JobSourceORM, get_engine, init_db
from src.main import export_top_matches, show_top_jobs
from src.matcher import score_job
from src.models import JobRecord
from src.normalize import norm_text


def load_json(path: Path, default):
    try:
        return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except Exception:
        return default


def save_json(path: Path, data):
    path.write_text(json.dumps(data, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False, default=str), encoding="utf-8")


def bootstrap_legacy_history(engine) -> bool:
    history_path = ROOT / "data" / "ui_run_history.json"
    history = load_json(history_path, [])
    if history:
        return False

    with Session(engine) as session:
        total = int(session.scalar(select(func.count(JobORM.id))) or 0)
        source_rows = int(session.scalar(select(func.count(JobSourceORM.id))) or 0)
        stale = int(session.scalar(select(func.count(JobORM.id)).where(JobORM.stale.is_(True))) or 0)
        live = total - stale
        earliest = session.scalar(select(func.min(JobORM.first_seen_at)))
        source_names = [row[0] for row in session.execute(select(JobSourceORM.source_name).distinct()).all()]

    if total == 0:
        return False

    started = earliest.isoformat() if hasattr(earliest, "isoformat") else datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
    sources = {name: {"status": "success"} for name in source_names}
    history = [{
        "id": "legacy_initial_dry_run",
        "status": "success",
        "started_at": started,
        "finished_at": started,
        "limit": source_rows,
        "summary": {
            "raw_jobs": source_rows,
            "new_jobs": total,
            "duplicates": max(0, source_rows - total),
            "seen_again": 0,
            "stale": stale,
            "collector_failures": 0,
            "live_exported": live,
        },
        "sources": sources,
    }]
    save_json(history_path, history)
    return True


def update_from_record(job: JobORM, source: JobSourceORM, record: JobRecord) -> None:
    job.title = record.title or job.title
    if record.company and norm_text(record.company) not in {"unknown", ""}:
        job.company = record.company
    if record.location:
        job.location = record.location
    if record.description:
        job.description = record.description
    if record.employment_type:
        job.employment_type = record.employment_type
    job.remote = record.remote
    job.remote_region = record.remote_region
    job.min_years = record.min_years
    job.max_years = record.max_years
    job.salary_min = record.salary_min
    job.salary_max = record.salary_max
    job.salary_currency = record.salary_currency
    job.salary_period = record.salary_period
    if record.posted_at:
        job.posted_at = record.posted_at
    job.norm_title = norm_text(job.title)
    job.norm_company = norm_text(job.company)
    job.norm_location = norm_text(job.location)
    job.updated_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    source.raw_payload_json = json.dumps(record.raw_payload, ensure_ascii=False, default=str)


def enrich_keejob(engine, settings: dict) -> tuple[int, int]:
    source_cfg = yaml.safe_load((ROOT / "config" / "sources.yaml").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["sources"]["keejob"]
    collector = KeejobCollector(source_cfg, settings)
    ok = 0
    failed = 0
    try:
        with Session(engine) as session:
            rows = session.execute(
                select(JobORM, JobSourceORM)
                .join(JobSourceORM, JobSourceORM.job_id == JobORM.id)
                .where(JobSourceORM.source_name == "keejob")
            ).all()
            for job, source in rows:
                try:
                    record = collector.fetch_detail(source.source_url, job.title, job.description)
                    update_from_record(job, source, record)
                    ok += 1
                    print(f"[KEEJOB-DETAIL] OK   {job.id:>3}  {job.company} | {job.title}")
                except Exception as exc:
                    failed += 1
                    print(f"[KEEJOB-DETAIL] FAIL {job.id:>3}  {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
                time.sleep(0.12)
            session.commit()
    finally:
        collector.close()
    return ok, failed


def rescore(engine, profile: dict, settings: dict) -> int:
    changed = 0
    with Session(engine) as session:
        jobs = session.scalars(select(JobORM).options(selectinload(JobORM.sources))).all()
        for job in jobs:
            source = job.sources[0] if job.sources else None
            record = JobRecord(
                title=job.title,
                company=job.company,
                location=job.location,
                description=job.description,
                employment_type=job.employment_type,
                remote=job.remote,
                remote_region=job.remote_region,
                seniority=job.seniority,
                min_years=job.min_years,
                max_years=job.max_years,
                salary_min=job.salary_min,
                salary_max=job.salary_max,
                salary_currency=job.salary_currency,
                salary_period=job.salary_period,
                posted_at=job.posted_at,
                source_name=source.source_name if source else "stored",
                source_url=source.source_url if source else (job.canonical_url or f"stored:{job.id}"),
            )
            scores = score_job(record, profile, settings)
            job.seniority = record.seniority
            job.employment_type = record.employment_type
            job.min_years = record.min_years
            job.max_years = record.max_years
            job.technical_score = scores.technical
            job.eligibility_score = scores.eligibility
            job.location_score = scores.location
            job.experience_score = scores.experience
            job.overall_score = scores.overall
            job.verdict = scores.verdict
            job.score_explanation_json = json.dumps(scores.explanations, ensure_ascii=False)
            job.requirements_json = json.dumps(scores.requirements, ensure_ascii=False, default=str)
            job.required_languages_json = json.dumps(scores.requirements.get("languages", []), ensure_ascii=False, default=str)
            job.extraction_confidence_json = json.dumps(scores.confidence, ensure_ascii=False, default=str)
            changed += 1
        session.commit()
    return changed


def main() -> int:
    settings = yaml.safe_load((ROOT / "config" / "settings.yaml").read_text(encoding="utf-8")) or {}
    profile = json.loads((ROOT / "config" / "profile.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    db_path = ROOT / settings.get("storage", {}).get("sqlite_path", "data/jobs.db")
    engine = get_engine(str(db_path))
    init_db(engine)

    imported = bootstrap_legacy_history(engine)
    print(f"Legacy run ledger imported: {'YES' if imported else 'already present / not needed'}")

    print("\nRe-enriching existing Keejob records from their detail pages...")
    ok, failed = enrich_keejob(engine, settings)
    print(f"Keejob detail enrichment: {ok} succeeded, {failed} failed")

    changed = rescore(engine, profile, settings)
    print(f"Rescored {changed} canonical jobs with V0.4 rules.")

    export_path = ROOT / settings.get("storage", {}).get("export_top_matches_csv", "data/exports/top_matches.csv")
    exported = export_top_matches(engine, export_path)
    print(f"Exported {exported} live jobs to {export_path}.")
    show_top_jobs(engine)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
if str(ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

import yaml
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, selectinload

from src.database import JobORM, get_engine
from src.main import export_top_matches, show_top_jobs
from src.matcher import score_job
from src.models import JobRecord
from src.normalize import norm_text


SECTOR_SIGNALS = {
    "informatique", "telecoms", "telecom", "banque", "finance", "assurances",
    "industrie", "production", "commerce", "distribution", "marketing",
    "communication", "sante", "medical", "pharmaceutique", "transport",
    "logistique", "tourisme", "hotellerie", "restauration",
}


def looks_like_sector_label(value: str) -> bool:
    if "/" not in (value or ""):
        return False
    return bool(set(norm_text(value).split()).intersection(SECTOR_SIGNALS))


def main() -> int:
    settings_path = ROOT / "config" / "settings.yaml"
    profile_path = ROOT / "config" / "profile.json"

    settings = yaml.safe_load(settings_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) or {}
    profile = json.loads(profile_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))

    settings.setdefault("project", {})["version"] = "0.4.1"
    settings.setdefault("matching", {}).setdefault("ranking_caps", {})[
        "local_outside_allowed"
    ] = 15

    settings_path.write_text(
        yaml.safe_dump(
            settings,
            sort_keys=False,
            allow_unicode=True,
            width=120,
        ),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    db_path = ROOT / settings.get("storage", {}).get("sqlite_path", "data/jobs.db")
    engine = get_engine(str(db_path))

    sanitised = 0
    rescored = 0

    with Session(engine) as session:
        jobs = session.scalars(
            select(JobORM).options(selectinload(JobORM.sources))
        ).all()

        for job in jobs:
            # Some Keejob pages hide the employer and expose a sector link in
            # the same visual position. Do not present that sector as a company.
            if looks_like_sector_label(job.company):
                job.company = "Confidential employer"
                job.norm_company = norm_text(job.company)
                sanitised += 1

            source = job.sources[0] if job.sources else None
            record = JobRecord(
                title=job.title,
                company=job.company,
                location=job.location,
                description=job.description,
                employment_type=job.employment_type,
                remote=job.remote,
                remote_region=job.remote_region,
                seniority=job.seniority,
                min_years=job.min_years,
                max_years=job.max_years,
                salary_min=job.salary_min,
                salary_max=job.salary_max,
                salary_currency=job.salary_currency,
                salary_period=job.salary_period,
                posted_at=job.posted_at,
                source_name=source.source_name if source else "stored",
                source_url=source.source_url if source else (
                    job.canonical_url or f"stored:{job.id}"
                ),
            )

            scores = score_job(record, profile, settings)

            job.seniority = record.seniority
            job.employment_type = record.employment_type
            job.min_years = record.min_years
            job.max_years = record.max_years

            job.technical_score = scores.technical
            job.eligibility_score = scores.eligibility
            job.location_score = scores.location
            job.experience_score = scores.experience
            job.overall_score = scores.overall
            job.verdict = scores.verdict
            job.score_explanation_json = json.dumps(
                scores.explanations, ensure_ascii=False
            )
            job.requirements_json = json.dumps(
                scores.requirements, ensure_ascii=False, default=str
            )
            job.required_languages_json = json.dumps(
                scores.requirements.get("languages", []),
                ensure_ascii=False,
                default=str,
            )
            job.extraction_confidence_json = json.dumps(
                scores.confidence, ensure_ascii=False, default=str
            )
            rescored += 1

        session.commit()

    export_path = ROOT / settings.get("storage", {}).get(
        "export_top_matches_csv",
        "data/exports/top_matches.csv",
    )
    exported = export_top_matches(engine, export_path)

    print(f"Sanitised sector-as-company values: {sanitised}")
    print(f"Re-scored canonical jobs: {rescored}")
    print(f"Exported live jobs: {exported}")
    print()
    show_top_jobs(engine)

    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

from __future__ import annotations

import json

from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
if str(ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

import yaml
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, selectinload

from src.collectors.keejob import KeejobCollector
from src.database import JobORM, JobSourceORM, get_engine
from src.main import export_top_matches, show_top_jobs
from src.matcher import score_job
from src.models import JobRecord
from src.normalize import norm_text

settings_path = ROOT / "config" / "settings.yaml"
profile_path = ROOT / "config" / "profile.json"

settings = yaml.safe_load(settings_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) or {}
settings.setdefault("project", {})["version"] = "0.4.3"
settings_path.write_text(
    yaml.safe_dump(settings, sort_keys=False, allow_unicode=True, width=120),
    encoding="utf-8",
)
profile = json.loads(profile_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))

db_path = ROOT / settings.get("storage", {}).get("sqlite_path", "data/jobs.db")

source_cfg = yaml.safe_load(
    (ROOT / "config" / "sources.yaml").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
)["sources"]["keejob"]
collector = KeejobCollector(source_cfg, settings)

now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
ok = 0
failed = 0
cleared_future = 0

print("Refreshing existing Keejob records with Unicode-safe parser...")
try:
    with Session(engine) as session:
        rows = session.execute(
            select(JobORM, JobSourceORM)
            .join(JobSourceORM, JobSourceORM.job_id == JobORM.id)
            .where(JobSourceORM.source_name == "keejob")
        ).all()

        for job, source in rows:
            # Never preserve an impossible future publication date.
            if job.posted_at:
                posted = job.posted_at
                if posted.tzinfo is None:
                    posted = posted.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
                if posted > now + timedelta(days=1):
                    job.posted_at = None
                    cleared_future += 1

            try:
                record = collector.fetch_detail(
                    source.source_url,
                    job.title,
                    job.description,
                )
                job.title = record.title or job.title
                job.company = record.company or job.company
                job.location = record.location or job.location
                job.description = record.description or job.description
                job.employment_type = record.employment_type or job.employment_type
                job.remote = record.remote
                job.remote_region = record.remote_region
                job.min_years = record.min_years
                job.max_years = record.max_years
                job.salary_min = record.salary_min
                job.salary_max = record.salary_max
                job.salary_currency = record.salary_currency
                job.salary_period = record.salary_period
                job.posted_at = record.posted_at
                job.norm_title = norm_text(job.title)
                job.norm_company = norm_text(job.company)
                job.norm_location = norm_text(job.location)
                source.raw_payload_json = json.dumps(
                    record.raw_payload,
                    ensure_ascii=False,
                    default=str,
                )
                ok += 1
                date_text = (
                    record.posted_at.date().isoformat()
                    if record.posted_at
                    else "DATE UNKNOWN"
                )
                print(
                    f"[KEEJOB-V043] OK {job.id:>3} | "
                    f"{job.company} | {date_text}"
                )
            except Exception as exc:
                failed += 1
                print(
                    f"[KEEJOB-V043] FAIL {job.id:>3} | "
                    f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
                )

            time.sleep(0.12)

        session.commit()
finally:
    try:
        collector.close()
    except Exception:
        pass

print(
    f"Keejob refresh: {ok} succeeded, {failed} failed; "
    f"cleared {cleared_future} impossible future date(s)"
)

print("Re-scoring all canonical jobs...")
with Session(engine) as session:
    jobs = session.scalars(
        select(JobORM).options(selectinload(JobORM.sources))
    ).all()

    for job in jobs:
        source = job.sources[0] if job.sources else None
        record = JobRecord(
            title=job.title,
            company=job.company,
            location=job.location,
            description=job.description,
            employment_type=job.employment_type,

            salary_min=job.salary_min,
            salary_max=job.salary_max,
            salary_currency=job.salary_currency,
            salary_period=job.salary_period,
            posted_at=job.posted_at,
            source_name=source.source_name if source else "stored",
            source_url=(
                source.source_url
                if source
                else (job.canonical_url or f"stored:{job.id}")
            ),
        )

        scores = score_job(record, profile, settings)
        job.seniority = record.seniority
        job.employment_type = record.employment_type
        job.min_years = record.min_years
        job.max_years = record.max_years
        job.technical_score = scores.technical
        job.eligibility_score = scores.eligibility
        job.location_score = scores.location
        job.experience_score = scores.experience
        job.overall_score = scores.overall
        job.verdict = scores.verdict
        job.score_explanation_json = json.dumps(

        )
        job.requirements_json = json.dumps(
            scores.requirements, ensure_ascii=False, default=str
        )

            scores.requirements.get("languages", []),
            ensure_ascii=False,
            default=str,
        )
        job.extraction_confidence_json = json.dumps(
            scores.confidence, ensure_ascii=False, default=str
        )

    session.commit()

export_path = ROOT / settings.get("storage", {}).get(
    "export_top_matches_csv",
    "data/exports/top_matches.csv",

# SIZE: 8.27 KB
# MODIFIED: 08/16/2026 17:16:49

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import re
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
if str(ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

import yaml
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, selectinload

from src.database import JobORM, get_engine
from src.main import export_top_matches, show_top_jobs
from src.matcher import score_job
from src.models import JobRecord
from src.normalize import (
    canonical_job_type,
    detect_seniority,
    norm_text,
    parse_date,
)


DATE_KEYS_BY_SOURCE = {
    "remotive": [
        "publicationdate",
        "publishedat",
        "dateposted",
        "postedat",
        "createdat",
    ],
    "jobicy": [
        "pubdate",
        "dateposted",
        "publishedat",
        "publicationdate",
        "createdat",
    ],
    "arbeitnow": [
        "createdat",
        "publishedat",
        "publicationdate",
        "dateposted",
        "postedat",
    ],
}

GENERIC_DATE_KEYS = [
    "publicationdate",
    "pubdate",
    "publishedat",
    "dateposted",
    "postedat",
    "createdat",
]


def key_id(value: str) -> str:
    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]", "", str(value).lower())


def iter_key_values(value: Any):
    if isinstance(value, dict):
        for key, child in value.items():
            yield key_id(key), child
            yield from iter_key_values(child)
    elif isinstance(value, list):
        for child in value:
            yield from iter_key_values(child)


def raw_posted_at(source_name: str, raw_json: str):
    try:
        payload = json.loads(raw_json or "{}")
    except Exception:
        return None, None

    collected = list(iter_key_values(payload))
    priorities = DATE_KEYS_BY_SOURCE.get(source_name, []) + GENERIC_DATE_KEYS

    seen = set()
    for wanted in priorities:
        if wanted in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(wanted)

        for key, value in collected:
            if key != wanted or value in (None, ""):
                continue

            parsed = parse_date(value)
            if parsed is None:
                continue

            return parsed, value

    return None, None


def aware(value):
    if value is None:
        return None
    return value if value.tzinfo else value.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)


settings_path = ROOT / "config" / "settings.yaml"
profile_path = ROOT / "config" / "profile.json"

settings = yaml.safe_load(settings_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) or {}
settings.setdefault("project", {})["version"] = "0.4.4"
settings_path.write_text(
    yaml.safe_dump(
        settings,
        sort_keys=False,
        allow_unicode=True,
        width=120,
    ),
    encoding="utf-8",
)

profile = json.loads(profile_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
db_path = ROOT / settings.get("storage", {}).get("sqlite_path", "data/jobs.db")
engine = get_engine(str(db_path))

now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
future_limit = now + timedelta(days=1)

dates_repaired = 0
future_cleared = 0
seniority_repaired = 0
types_repaired = 0
date_examples = []

with Session(engine) as session:
    jobs = session.scalars(
        select(JobORM).options(selectinload(JobORM.sources))
    ).all()

    for job in jobs:
        # Keejob was already refreshed directly from detail pages in V0.4.3.
        # Repair API-source dates from their preserved original raw payload.
        source_candidates = [
            source for source in job.sources
            if source.source_name != "keejob"
        ]

        repaired_date = None
        repaired_raw = None
        repaired_source = None

        for source in source_candidates:
            candidate, raw_value = raw_posted_at(
                source.source_name,
                source.raw_payload_json,
            )
            candidate = aware(candidate)
            if candidate is None or candidate > future_limit:
                continue

            repaired_date = candidate
            repaired_raw = raw_value
            repaired_source = source.source_name
            break

        current = aware(job.posted_at)

        if repaired_date is not None:
            if current is None or abs((current - repaired_date).total_seconds()) > 1:
                old_text = current.isoformat() if current else "None"
                job.posted_at = repaired_date
                dates_repaired += 1
                if len(date_examples) < 12:
                    date_examples.append(
                        (
                            job.id,
                            repaired_source,
                            old_text,
                            repaired_date.isoformat(),
                            str(repaired_raw)[:80],
                        )
                    )
        elif current is not None and current > future_limit:
            job.posted_at = None
            future_cleared += 1

        # Recompute high-impact derived fields instead of retaining stale
        # inference values from old collectors.
        title_seniority = detect_seniority(job.title or "")
        if (job.seniority or "") != title_seniority:
            job.seniority = title_seniority
            seniority_repaired += 1

        canonical_type = canonical_job_type(job.employment_type, job.remote)
        if canonical_type and canonical_type != (job.employment_type or ""):
            job.employment_type = canonical_type
            types_repaired += 1

    session.commit()

print(f"Source dates repaired: {dates_repaired}")
print(f"Impossible future dates cleared: {future_cleared}")
print(f"Derived seniority values repaired: {seniority_repaired}")
print(f"Employment-type spellings canonicalized: {types_repaired}")

for job_id, source, old, new, raw in date_examples:
    print(
        f"[DATE-REPAIR] job={job_id} source={source} "
        f"{old} -> {new} | raw={raw}"
    )

print()
print("Re-scoring all jobs with corrected derived fields...")

with Session(engine) as session:
    jobs = session.scalars(
        select(JobORM).options(selectinload(JobORM.sources))
    ).all()

    for job in jobs:
        source = job.sources[0] if job.sources else None

        record = JobRecord(
            title=job.title,
            company=job.company,
            location=job.location,
            description=job.description,
            employment_type=job.employment_type,
            remote=job.remote,
            remote_region=job.remote_region,
            seniority=job.seniority,
            min_years=job.min_years,
            max_years=job.max_years,
            salary_min=job.salary_min,
            salary_max=job.salary_max,
            salary_currency=job.salary_currency,
            salary_period=job.salary_period,
            posted_at=job.posted_at,
            source_name=source.source_name if source else "stored",
            source_url=source.source_url if source else (
                job.canonical_url or f"stored:{job.id}"
            ),
        )

        scores = score_job(record, profile, settings)

        job.seniority = record.seniority
        job.employment_type = record.employment_type
        job.min_years = record.min_years
        job.max_years = record.max_years
        job.technical_score = scores.technical
        job.eligibility_score = scores.eligibility
        job.location_score = scores.location
        job.experience_score = scores.experience
        job.overall_score = scores.overall
        job.verdict = scores.verdict
        job.score_explanation_json = json.dumps(
            scores.explanations,
            ensure_ascii=False,
        )
        job.requirements_json = json.dumps(
            scores.requirements,
            ensure_ascii=False,
            default=str,
        )
        job.required_languages_json = json.dumps(
            scores.requirements.get("languages", []),
            ensure_ascii=False,
            default=str,
        )
        job.extraction_confidence_json = json.dumps(
            scores.confidence,
            ensure_ascii=False,
            default=str,
        )

    session.commit()

export_path = ROOT / settings.get("storage", {}).get(
    "export_top_matches_csv",
    "data/exports/top_matches.csv",
)
exported = export_top_matches(engine, export_path)

print(f"Re-scored {len(jobs)} canonical jobs.")
print(f"Exported {exported} live jobs.")
print()
show_top_jobs(engine)

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
if str(ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

import yaml
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, selectinload

from src.database import JobORM, get_engine
from src.main import export_top_matches, show_top_jobs
from src.matcher import score_job
from src.models import JobRecord

settings_path = ROOT / "config" / "settings.yaml"
profile_path = ROOT / "config" / "profile.json"

settings = yaml.safe_load(settings_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) or {}
settings.setdefault("project", {})["version"] = "0.4.4.2"
settings_path.write_text(
    yaml.safe_dump(
        settings,
        sort_keys=False,
        allow_unicode=True,
        width=120,
    ),
    encoding="utf-8",
)

profile = json.loads(profile_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
removed = profile.pop("salary_preferences", None)
profile_path.write_text(
    json.dumps(profile, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n",
    encoding="utf-8",
)

print(
    "Salary preference removed from profile."
    if removed is not None
    else "No salary preference remained in profile."
)

db_path = ROOT / settings.get("storage", {}).get(
    "sqlite_path",
    "data/jobs.db",
)
engine = get_engine(str(db_path))

with Session(engine) as session:
    jobs = session.scalars(
        select(JobORM).options(selectinload(JobORM.sources))
    ).all()

    for job in jobs:
        source = job.sources[0] if job.sources else None

        record = JobRecord(
            title=job.title,
            company=job.company,
            location=job.location,
            description=job.description,
            employment_type=job.employment_type,
            remote=job.remote,
            remote_region=job.remote_region,
            seniority=job.seniority,
            min_years=job.min_years,
            max_years=job.max_years,
            salary_min=job.salary_min,
            salary_max=job.salary_max,
            salary_currency=job.salary_currency,
            salary_period=job.salary_period,
            posted_at=job.posted_at,
            source_name=source.source_name if source else "stored",
            source_url=source.source_url if source else (
                job.canonical_url or f"stored:{job.id}"
            ),
        )

        scores = score_job(record, profile, settings)

        job.seniority = record.seniority
        job.employment_type = record.employment_type
        job.min_years = record.min_years
        job.max_years = record.max_years
        job.technical_score = scores.technical
        job.eligibility_score = scores.eligibility
        job.location_score = scores.location
        job.experience_score = scores.experience
        job.overall_score = scores.overall
        job.verdict = scores.verdict
        job.score_explanation_json = json.dumps(
            scores.explanations,
            ensure_ascii=False,
        )
        job.requirements_json = json.dumps(
            scores.requirements,
            ensure_ascii=False,
            default=str,
        )
        job.required_languages_json = json.dumps(
            scores.requirements.get("languages", []),
            ensure_ascii=False,
            default=str,
        )
        job.extraction_confidence_json = json.dumps(
            scores.confidence,
            ensure_ascii=False,
            default=str,
        )

    session.commit()

export_path = ROOT / settings.get("storage", {}).get(
    "export_top_matches_csv",
    "data/exports/top_matches.csv",
)
exported = export_top_matches(engine, export_path)

print(f"Re-scored {len(jobs)} canonical jobs without salary filtering.")
print(f"Exported {exported} live jobs.")
print()
show_top_jobs(engine)

from __future__ import annotations
from pathlib import Path
from PIL import Image, ImageEnhance, ImageFilter

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
BRANDING = ROOT / "assets" / "branding"
UI = ROOT / "ui"

candidates = [
    BRANDING / "ttea-logo-master.png",
    BRANDING / "ttea-logo.png",
    UI / "ttea-logo.png",
]
source = next((p for p in candidates if p.exists()), None)
if source is None:
    raise SystemExit("No TTEA source logo was found.")

img = Image.open(source).convert("RGBA")
bbox = img.getchannel("A").getbbox()
if bbox:
    img = img.crop(bbox)

w, h = img.size
side = max(w, h)
margin = max(12, round(side * 0.035))
canvas_side = side + margin * 2
canvas = Image.new("RGBA", (canvas_side, canvas_side), (0,0,0,0))
canvas.alpha_composite(img, ((canvas_side-w)//2, (canvas_side-h)//2))

hd = canvas.resize((1024,1024), Image.Resampling.LANCZOS)
hd = hd.filter(ImageFilter.UnsharpMask(radius=0.7, percent=115, threshold=2))

BRANDING.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
UI.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
hd.save(BRANDING / "ttea-logo-hd.png", optimize=True)
hd.save(UI / "ttea-logo-hd.png", optimize=True)

icon256 = canvas.resize((256,256), Image.Resampling.LANCZOS)
icon256 = ImageEnhance.Contrast(icon256).enhance(1.04)
icon256 = icon256.filter(ImageFilter.UnsharpMask(radius=0.65, percent=150, threshold=1))

icon64 = icon256.resize((64,64), Image.Resampling.LANCZOS)
icon64 = icon64.filter(ImageFilter.UnsharpMask(radius=0.45, percent=165, threshold=1))
icon64.save(UI / "ttea-icon-64.png", optimize=True)

ico_brand = BRANDING / "ttea-v0451.ico"
ico_ui = UI / "ttea-v0451.ico"
icon256.save(
    ico_brand,
    format="ICO",
    sizes=[(16,16),(20,20),(24,24),(32,32),(40,40),(48,48),(64,64),(128,128),(256,256)]
)
ico_ui.write_bytes(ico_brand.read_bytes())
(UI / "favicon.ico").write_bytes(ico_brand.read_bytes())
(BRANDING / "ttea.ico").write_bytes(ico_brand.read_bytes())

print(f"Logo source : {source}")
print(f"Source size : {Image.open(source).size}")
print("HD UI logo  : 1024 x 1024")
print("ICO frames  : 16,20,24,32,40,48,64,128,256")

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]

async def main():
    from playwright.async_api import async_playwright

    errors = []

    async with async_playwright() as p:
        try:
            browser = await p.chromium.launch(channel="msedge", headless=True)
        except Exception:
            candidates = [
                Path(r"C:\Program Files (x86)\Microsoft\Edge\Application\msedge.exe"),
                Path(r"C:\Program Files\Microsoft\Edge\Application\msedge.exe"),
            ]
            edge = next((x for x in candidates if x.exists()), None)
            if edge is None:
                raise
            browser = await p.chromium.launch(executable_path=str(edge), headless=True)

        context = await browser.new_context(
            viewport={"width":1296,"height":987},
            color_scheme="light"
        )
        page = await context.new_page()

        page.on("pageerror", lambda exc: errors.append(f"PAGE: {exc}"))
        page.on(
            "console",
            lambda msg: errors.append(f"CONSOLE: {msg.text}") if msg.type == "error" else None
        )

        await page.goto(
            "http://127.0.0.1:8765/?runtime_smoke=0451",
            wait_until="domcontentloaded"
        )

        await page.wait_for_function(
            """() => document.getElementById('connectionText')?.textContent?.includes('ONLINE')""",
            timeout=12000
        )

        await page.locator('.nav-item[data-view="jobs"]').click()

        await page.wait_for_function(
            """() => {
                const count = document.getElementById('jobsCount')?.textContent || '';
                const table = document.getElementById('jobsTable');
                return !count.includes('LOADING') &&
                       table &&
                       (table.querySelector('.job-data-row') ||
                        table.textContent.includes('NO JOBS'));
            }""",
            timeout=12000
        )

        logo = page.locator(".brand-logo").first
        await logo.wait_for(state="visible", timeout=5000)

        metrics = await logo.evaluate(
            """img => ({
                naturalWidth: img.naturalWidth,
                naturalHeight: img.naturalHeight,
                displayedWidth: img.getBoundingClientRect().width,
                imageRendering: getComputedStyle(img).imageRendering,
                src: img.currentSrc || img.src
            })"""
        )

        (ROOT / "logs").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        await page.screenshot(
            path=str(ROOT / "logs" / "v0451_frontend_smoke.png"),
            full_page=True,
            animations="disabled"
        )
        await browser.close()

    if metrics["naturalWidth"] < 512:
        raise RuntimeError(f"HD logo is too small: {metrics}")
    if "pixel" in (metrics["imageRendering"] or "").lower():
        raise RuntimeError(f"Logo still uses pixelated scaling: {metrics}")
    if errors:
        raise RuntimeError("Front-end errors detected:\n" + "\n".join(errors))

    print("V0.4.5.1 FRONT-END SMOKE OK")
    print(f"Logo natural size : {metrics['naturalWidth']} x {metrics['naturalHeight']}")
    print(f"Logo display width: {metrics['displayedWidth']:.1f}px")
    print(f"Image rendering   : {metrics['imageRendering']}")
    print(f"Asset             : {metrics['src']}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    asyncio.run(main())

from __future__ import annotations
import re
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
UI = ROOT / "ui"

app_path = UI / "app.js"
html_path = UI / "index.html"
css_path = UI / "styles.css"

app = app_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
html = html_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
css = css_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")

marker = "/* V0.4.5.1 RUNTIME NULL-SAFETY */"
if marker not in app:
    helper = r'''
/* V0.4.5.1 RUNTIME NULL-SAFETY */
function byId(id){return document.getElementById(id);}
function fieldValue(id,fallback=''){const e=byId(id);return e&&('value' in e)?e.value:fallback;}
'''
    anchor = "function scoreClass(v){ return Number(v)>=65?'score-high':Number(v)>=45?'score-mid':'score-low'; }"
    if anchor not in app:
        raise RuntimeError("Could not locate scoreClass() insertion point")
    app = app.replace(anchor, anchor + helper, 1)

replacements = {
    "document.getElementById('jobsSearch').value": "fieldValue('jobsSearch','')",
    "document.getElementById('jobsMinScore').value": "fieldValue('jobsMinScore','0')",
    "document.getElementById('jobsReview').value": "fieldValue('jobsReview','unreviewed')",
    "document.getElementById('jobsVerdict').value": "fieldValue('jobsVerdict','reviewable')",
    "document.getElementById('jobsSource').value": "fieldValue('jobsSource','all')",
    "document.getElementById('jobsLocation').value": "fieldValue('jobsLocation','all')",
    "document.getElementById('jobsSort').value": "fieldValue('jobsSort','verdict')",
    "document.getElementById('runScope').value": "fieldValue('runScope','all')",
    "document.getElementById('runLimit').value": "fieldValue('runLimit','100')",
}
for old, new in replacements.items():
    app = app.replace(old, new)

pattern = re.compile(r"function populateSourceFilter\(\)\{.*?\}\n", re.S)
match = pattern.search(app)
if not match:
    raise RuntimeError("populateSourceFilter() was not found")

safe_populate = r'''function populateSourceFilter(){
  const active=state.sources.filter(s=>s.state==='active');
  const jobsSource=byId('jobsSource');
  if(jobsSource){
    const current=jobsSource.value;
    jobsSource.innerHTML='<option value="all">ALL ACTIVE SOURCES</option>'+
      active.map(s=>`<option value="${esc(s.name)}">${esc(s.name.toUpperCase())}</option>`).join('');
    if([...jobsSource.options].some(o=>o.value===current))jobsSource.value=current;
  }
  const runScope=byId('runScope');
  if(runScope){
    const currentScope=runScope.value;
    runScope.innerHTML='<option value="all">ALL READY SOURCES</option>'+
      active.filter(s=>s.enabled).map(s=>`<option value="${esc(s.name)}">${esc(s.name.toUpperCase())}</option>`).join('');
    if([...runScope.options].some(o=>o.value===currentScope))runScope.value=currentScope;
  }
}
'''
app = app[:match.start()] + safe_populate + app[match.end():]

app = app.replace(
    "document.getElementById('jobsSearch').oninput=scheduleJobs;",
    "if(byId('jobsSearch'))byId('jobsSearch').oninput=scheduleJobs;"
)
app = app.replace(
    "['jobsVerdict','jobsReview','jobsLocation','jobsSource','jobsMinScore','jobsSort'].forEach(id=>document.getElementById(id).onchange=scheduleJobs);",
    "['jobsVerdict','jobsReview','jobsLocation','jobsSource','jobsMinScore','jobsSort'].forEach(id=>{const e=byId(id);if(e)e.onchange=scheduleJobs;});"
)
app = app.replace(
    "document.getElementById('runScope').onchange=updateRunEligibility;",
    "if(byId('runScope'))byId('runScope').onchange=updateRunEligibility;"
)

if "bootstrapRetries" not in app:
    app = app.replace(
        "jobsMode: 'review', runTimer: null, currentJobId: null,",
        "jobsMode: 'review', runTimer: null, currentJobId: null, bootstrapRetries: 0,",
        1
    )

old_bootstrap = re.search(r"async function bootstrap\(\)\{.*?\}\n", app, re.S)
if old_bootstrap:
    safe_bootstrap = r'''async function bootstrap(){
  try{
    const [h,p,s]=await Promise.all([api('/api/health'),api('/api/profile'),api('/api/sources')]);
    state.bootstrapRetries=0;
    const dot=byId('connectionDot');
    const connection=byId('connectionText');
    const version=byId('appVersion');
    if(dot){dot.classList.remove('offline');dot.classList.add('online');}
    if(connection)connection.textContent='LOCAL API / ONLINE';
    if(version)version.textContent=`VERSION ${h.version}`;
    state.profile=p.profile;
    state.settings=p.settings;
    state.reviewStatuses=p.review_statuses||[];
    state.applicationStatuses=p.application_statuses||[];
    state.sources=s.items||[];
    populateSourceFilter();
    await loadOverview();
    const r=await api('/api/run/status');
    renderRun(r);
    if(r.status==='running')beginRunPolling();
  }catch(e){
    const dot=byId('connectionDot');
    const connection=byId('connectionText');
    if(dot){dot.classList.remove('online');dot.classList.add('offline');}
    if(connection)connection.textContent='LOCAL API / OFFLINE';
    state.bootstrapRetries=(state.bootstrapRetries||0)+1;
    if(state.bootstrapRetries<=12){setTimeout(()=>bootstrap(),500);}
    else{handleError(e);}
  }
}
'''
    app = app[:old_bootstrap.start()] + safe_bootstrap + app[old_bootstrap.end():]

version = "0451"
html = re.sub(r'(/assets/styles\.css)(?:\?[^"\']*)?', rf'\1?v={version}', html)
html = re.sub(r'(/assets/app\.js)(?:\?[^"\']*)?', rf'\1?v={version}', html)
html = re.sub(
    r'/assets/ttea-logo(?:-hd)?\.png(?:\?[^"\']*)?',
    f'/assets/ttea-logo-hd.png?v={version}',
    html
)
html = re.sub(
    r'/assets/(?:favicon|ttea-v\d+)\.ico(?:\?[^"\']*)?',
    f'/assets/ttea-v0451.ico?v={version}',
    html
)
if "ttea-icon-64.png" not in html:
    html = html.replace(
        "</head>",
        '  <link rel="icon" type="image/png" sizes="64x64" href="/assets/ttea-icon-64.png?v=0451" />\n</head>',
        1
    )

if "V0.4.5.1 HD BRANDING" not in css:
    css += r'''

/* ==========================================================
   V0.4.5.1 HD BRANDING
   ========================================================== */
.brand-logo,
.brand-logo-block img {
  image-rendering: auto !important;
  object-fit: contain !important;
  max-width: 100%;
  height: auto;
  filter: none !important;
  transform: translateZ(0);
}
.brand-logo {
  width: min(168px, 88%);
}
'''

app_path.write_text(app, encoding="utf-8")
html_path.write_text(html, encoding="utf-8")
css_path.write_text(css, encoding="utf-8")
print("Front-end runtime + cache-busting patch: OK")




################################################################
# FILE 35 / 68

# SIZE: 5.98 KB
# MODIFIED: 08/18/2026 08:37:08

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

import yaml
from sqlalchemy import inspect, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, selectinload

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
if str(ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

from src.database import JobORM, get_engine, init_db
from src.main import export_top_matches
from src.matcher import score_job
from src.models import JobRecord


def jf(value: str | None, default):
    try:
        return json.loads(value or "")
    except Exception:
        return default


settings_path = ROOT / "config" / "settings.yaml"
profile_path = ROOT / "config" / "profile.json"
settings = yaml.safe_load(settings_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) or {}
profile = json.loads(profile_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))

settings.setdefault("project", {})["version"] = "0.5.0"
settings_path.write_text(
    yaml.safe_dump(settings, sort_keys=False, allow_unicode=True, width=120),
    encoding="utf-8",
)

db_path = ROOT / settings.get("storage", {}).get("sqlite_path", "data/jobs.db")
engine = get_engine(str(db_path))
init_db(engine)

required_columns = {
    "opportunity_type", "country", "deadline", "is_open", "education_score",
    "domain_score", "visa_score", "salary_score", "language_score", "priority",
    "eligibility_status", "funding_status", "tuition_covered", "cover_letter_version",
}
actual = {c["name"] for c in inspect(engine).get_columns("jobs")}
missing = sorted(required_columns - actual)
if missing:
    raise RuntimeError("V0.5 schema migration missing columns: " + ", ".join(missing))

changed = 0
priority_counts: dict[str, int] = {}
eligibility_counts: dict[str, int] = {}
with Session(engine) as session:
    jobs = session.scalars(select(JobORM).options(selectinload(JobORM.sources))).all()
    for job in jobs:
        source = job.sources[0] if job.sources else None
        record = JobRecord(
            opportunity_type=job.opportunity_type or "job",
            title=job.title,
            company=job.company,
            location=job.location,
            country=job.country,
            city=job.city,
            description=job.description,
            employment_type=job.employment_type,
            work_mode=job.work_mode,
            remote=job.remote,
            remote_region=job.remote_region,
            seniority=job.seniority,
            min_years=job.min_years,
            max_years=job.max_years,
            salary_min=job.salary_min,
            salary_max=job.salary_max,
            salary_currency=job.salary_currency,
            salary_period=job.salary_period,
            posted_at=job.posted_at,
            deadline=job.deadline,
            last_verified_at=job.last_verified_at,
            is_open=job.is_open,
            education_requirement=job.education_requirement,
            visa_status=job.visa_status or "unknown",
            visa_sponsorship=job.visa_sponsorship,
            international_candidates=job.international_candidates,
            relocation_support=job.relocation_support,
            funding_status=job.funding_status,
            tuition_covered=job.tuition_covered,
            housing_support=job.housing_support,
            insurance_support=job.insurance_support,
            travel_support=job.travel_support,
            benefits=jf(job.benefits_json, {}),
            source_name=source.source_name if source else "stored",
            source_url=source.source_url if source else (job.canonical_url or f"stored:{job.id}"),
        )
        scores = score_job(record, profile, settings)

        job.opportunity_type = record.opportunity_type
        if not job.country:
            job.country = str(scores.requirements.get("country") or "")
        if not job.work_mode:
            job.work_mode = "remote" if job.remote else "onsite_or_hybrid"
        if job.last_verified_at is None:
            job.last_verified_at = job.last_seen_at or datetime.now(timezone.utc)

        job.seniority = record.seniority
        job.employment_type = record.employment_type
        job.min_years = record.min_years
        job.max_years = record.max_years
        job.technical_score = scores.technical
        job.eligibility_score = scores.eligibility
        job.location_score = scores.location
        job.experience_score = scores.experience
        job.education_score = scores.education
        job.domain_score = scores.domain
        job.visa_score = scores.visa
        job.salary_score = scores.salary
        job.language_score = scores.language
        job.overall_score = scores.overall
        job.priority = scores.priority
        job.eligibility_status = scores.eligibility_status
        job.verdict = scores.verdict
        job.score_explanation_json = json.dumps(scores.explanations, ensure_ascii=False)
        job.requirements_json = json.dumps(scores.requirements, ensure_ascii=False, default=str)
        job.required_languages_json = json.dumps(scores.requirements.get("languages", []), ensure_ascii=False, default=str)
        job.extraction_confidence_json = json.dumps(scores.confidence, ensure_ascii=False, default=str)
        job.updated_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        changed += 1
        priority_counts[scores.priority] = priority_counts.get(scores.priority, 0) + 1
        eligibility_counts[scores.eligibility_status] = eligibility_counts.get(scores.eligibility_status, 0) + 1
    session.commit()

export_path = ROOT / settings.get("storage", {}).get("export_top_matches_csv", "data/exports/top_matches.csv")
exported = export_top_matches(engine, export_path)

print("========================================================")
print(" TTEA V0.5.0 - Global Core migration complete")
print("========================================================")
print(f"Database:            {db_path}")
print(f"Canonical rows:      {changed}")
print(f"Live export rows:    {exported}")
print(f"Priority counts:     {priority_counts}")
print(f"Eligibility counts:  {eligibility_counts}")
print(f"CSV:                 {export_path}")

from __future__ import annotations

from .base import BaseCollector
from ..models import JobRecord
from ..normalize import clean_text, parse_date


class ArbeitnowCollector(BaseCollector):
    name = "arbeitnow"

    def fetch(self, limit: int) -> list[JobRecord]:
        payload = self.get_json(self.config["base_url"])
        out: list[JobRecord] = []
        for raw in payload.get("data", [])[:limit]:
            tags = raw.get("tags") or []
            job_types = raw.get("job_types") or []
            description = clean_text(raw.get("description"))
            if tags:
                description = description + " Tags: " + ", ".join(map(str, tags))
            out.append(
                JobRecord(
                    title=raw.get("title") or "Untitled job",
                    company=raw.get("company_name") or "Unknown",
                    location=raw.get("location") or "",
                    description=description,
                    employment_type=", ".join(map(str, job_types)),
                    remote=bool(raw.get("remote")),
                    remote_region=raw.get("location") if raw.get("remote") else "",
                    posted_at=parse_date(raw.get("created_at")),
                    source_name=self.name,
                    source_url=raw.get("url") or "",
                    external_id=str(raw.get("slug") or ""),
                    raw_payload=raw,
                )
            )
        return [x for x in out if x.source_url]

from __future__ import annotations

from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from typing import Any

import httpx

from ..models import JobRecord


class BaseCollector(ABC):
    name = "base"

    def __init__(self, config: dict, settings: dict):
        self.config = config
        self.settings = settings
        timeout = settings.get("collection", {}).get("request_timeout_seconds", 25)
        ua = settings.get("collection", {}).get(
            "user_agent",
            "TTEA/0.1 (personal job-search research)"
        )
        self.client = httpx.Client(
            timeout=timeout,
            follow_redirects=True,
            headers={
                "User-Agent": ua,
                "Accept": "application/json,text/html;q=0.9,*/*;q=0.8",
            },
        )

    def get_json(self, url: str, params: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> dict:
        response = self.client.get(url, params=params)
        response.raise_for_status()
        return response.json()

    def get_text(self, url: str) -> str:
        response = self.client.get(url)
        response.raise_for_status()
        return response.text

    def close(self) -> None:
        self.client.close()

    @abstractmethod
    def fetch(self, limit: int) -> list[JobRecord]:
        raise NotImplementedError
