from __future__ import annotations

from .base import BaseCollector
from ..models import JobRecord
from ..normalize import clean_text, parse_date


class JobicyCollector(BaseCollector):
    name = "jobicy"

    def fetch(self, limit: int) -> list[JobRecord]:
        url = self.config["base_url"]
        payload = self.get_json(url, params={"count": min(limit, 100)})
        out: list[JobRecord] = []

        for raw in payload.get("jobs", [])[:limit]:
            salary_min = raw.get("salaryMin")
            salary_max = raw.get("salaryMax")
            out.append(
                JobRecord(
                    title=raw.get("jobTitle") or "Untitled job",
                    company=raw.get("companyName") or "Unknown",
                    location=raw.get("jobGeo") or "",
                    description=clean_text(raw.get("jobDescription") or raw.get("jobExcerpt")),
                    employment_type=", ".join(raw.get("jobType") or []),
                    remote=True,
                    remote_region=raw.get("jobGeo") or "",
                    seniority=raw.get("jobLevel") or "",
                    salary_min=float(salary_min) if salary_min not in (None, "") else None,
                    salary_max=float(salary_max) if salary_max not in (None, "") else None,
                    salary_currency=raw.get("salaryCurrency") or None,
                    salary_period=raw.get("salaryPeriod") or None,
                    posted_at=parse_date(raw.get("pubDate")),
                    source_name=self.name,
                    source_url=raw.get("url") or f"https://jobicy.com/jobs/{raw.get('id')}",
                    external_id=str(raw.get("id") or ""),
                    raw_payload=raw,
                )
            )
        return out

from __future__ import annotations

import re
import urllib.robotparser
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from urllib.parse import urljoin, urlparse

from bs4 import BeautifulSoup

from .base import BaseCollector
from ..models import JobRecord
from ..normalize import clean_text, detect_job_type, extract_years, first_match, norm_text, parse_date, parse_salary_text

TUNISIA_LOCATIONS = [
    "Tunis", "Ariana", "Ben Arous", "Manouba", "Mghira", "Charguia", "Ghazala", "El Ghazala",
    "Les Berges du Lac", "Lac 1", "Lac 2", "MÃ©grine", "Megrine", "RadÃ¨s", "Rades", "Sousse",
    "Sfax", "Nabeul", "Monastir", "Bizerte", "Kairouan", "Mahdia", "GabÃ¨s", "Gabes", "Kalaa El Kebira",
]

DETAIL_LABELS = [
    "RÃ©fÃ©rence", "Date de publication", "Type de contrat", "Lieu de travail", "ExpÃ©rience requise",
    "Niveau d'Ã©tudes", "Salaire proposÃ©", "DisponibilitÃ©", "MobilitÃ©",
]


SECTOR_LABEL_SIGNALS = {
    "informatique", "telecoms", "telecom", "banque", "finance", "assurances",
    "industrie", "production", "commerce", "distribution", "marketing",
    "communication", "sante", "medical", "pharmaceutique", "transport",
    "logistique", "tourisme", "hotellerie", "restauration",
}


class KeejobCollector(BaseCollector):
    name = "keejob"

    def __init__(self, config: dict, settings: dict):
        super().__init__(config, settings)
        self._robots_cache: dict[str, urllib.robotparser.RobotFileParser] = {}

    def _robots_allows(self, url: str) -> bool:
        parsed = urlparse(url)
        root = f"{parsed.scheme}://{parsed.netloc}"
        parser = self._robots_cache.get(root)
        if parser is None:
            parser = urllib.robotparser.RobotFileParser()
            parser.set_url(urljoin(root, "/robots.txt"))
            try:
                parser.read()
            except Exception:
                # Fail closed for the HTML collector if robots could not be read.
                return False
            self._robots_cache[root] = parser
        user_agent = self.settings.get("collection", {}).get("user_agent", "TTEA")
        return parser.can_fetch(user_agent, url)

    @staticmethod
    def _best_card(anchor):
        node = anchor
        for _ in range(7):
            if node is None:
                break
            text = clean_text(node.get_text(" ", strip=True))
            if len(text) >= 80 and len(text) <= 2500:
                return node
            node = getattr(node, "parent", None)
        return getattr(anchor, "parent", None)

    @staticmethod
    def _value_after_label(lines: list[str], label: str) -> str:
        target = norm_text(label)
        labels = {norm_text(x) for x in DETAIL_LABELS}
        for i, line in enumerate(lines):
            if norm_text(line) != target:
                continue
            for candidate in lines[i + 1: i + 5]:
                n = norm_text(candidate)
                if not n or n in labels:
                    continue
                return candidate
        return ""

    @staticmethod
    def _description_from_lines(lines: list[str]) -> str:
        start = next((i for i, line in enumerate(lines) if norm_text(line) == norm_text("Description de l'annonce")), None)
        if start is None:
            return ""
        stop_tokens = {
            norm_text("Partager"), norm_text("Imprimer"), norm_text("Ã€ propos de Keejob"),
            norm_text("Postuler maintenant"), norm_text("Offres similaires"),
        }
        selected = []
        for line in lines[start + 1:]:
            if norm_text(line) in stop_tokens:
                break
            if line:
                selected.append(line)
        return clean_text(" ".join(selected))

    @staticmethod
    def _looks_like_sector_label(value: str) -> bool:
        normalised = norm_text(value)
        tokens = set(normalised.split())
        # Keejob sometimes exposes the sector/category near the title in the
        # same position where an employer link would otherwise appear.
        return (
            "/" in value
            and bool(tokens.intersection(SECTOR_LABEL_SIGNALS))
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _company_from_page(soup: BeautifulSoup, title: str) -> str:
        # The employer section on Keejob is structurally much safer than
        # nearby category/sector links.
        enterprise_heading = next(
            (
                node for node in soup.find_all(["h2", "h3", "h4"])
                if norm_text(node.get_text(" ", strip=True)) == "entreprise"
            ),
            None,
        )
        if enterprise_heading:
            for node in enterprise_heading.find_all_next(["h2", "h3", "h4", "a"], limit=20):
                value = clean_text(node.get_text(" ", strip=True))
                if not value or value == title:
                    continue
                if norm_text(value).startswith("secteur"):
                    break
                if KeejobCollector._looks_like_sector_label(value):
                    continue
                if norm_text(value) in {"entreprise", "postuler maintenant"}:
                    continue
                if len(value) <= 140:
                    return value

        # Fallback to canonical company links.
        for link in soup.find_all("a", href=True):
            href = link.get("href", "")
            if "/companies/" not in href:
                continue
            value = clean_text(link.get_text(" ", strip=True))
            if value and value != title and not KeejobCollector._looks_like_sector_label(value):
                return value

        return "Confidential employer"

    @staticmethod
    def _publication_from_page(soup: BeautifulSoup, lines: list[str]) -> tuple[str, object]:
        # Normalize accents first so this parser is fully ASCII-safe:
        # "PubliÃ©e le 11 aoÃ»t 2026" -> "publiee le 11 aout 2026".
        page_norm = norm_text(soup.get_text(" ", strip=True))
        match = re.search(
            r"\bpubliee?\s+le\s+([0-3]?\d)\s+([a-z]+)\s+(\d{4})\b",
            page_norm,
        )
        raw = ""
        parsed = None

        if match:
            day, month, year = match.groups()
            raw = f"{day} {month} {year}"
            parsed = parse_date(raw)

        if parsed is None:
            raw = KeejobCollector._value_after_label(lines, "Date de publication")
            parsed = parse_date(raw)

        if parsed is None:
            return raw, None

        if parsed > datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(days=1):
            return raw, None

        return raw, parsed

    def fetch_detail(self, url: str, listing_title: str = "", listing_text: str = "") -> JobRecord:
        if not self._robots_allows(url):
            raise RuntimeError(f"robots.txt does not allow detail URL: {url}")

        html = self.get_text(url)
        soup = BeautifulSoup(html, "html.parser")
        lines = [clean_text(x) for x in soup.stripped_strings]
        lines = [x for x in lines if x]

        h1 = soup.find("h1")
        title = clean_text(h1.get_text(" ", strip=True)) if h1 else listing_title or "Untitled job"
        company = self._company_from_page(soup, title)
        location = self._value_after_label(lines, "Lieu de travail") or first_match(listing_text, TUNISIA_LOCATIONS) or "Tunisia"
        contract = self._value_after_label(lines, "Type de contrat")
        experience = self._value_after_label(lines, "ExpÃ©rience requise")
        education = self._value_after_label(lines, "Niveau d'Ã©tudes")
        salary_text = self._value_after_label(lines, "Salaire proposÃ©")
        availability = self._value_after_label(lines, "DisponibilitÃ©")
        publication, publication_dt = self._publication_from_page(soup, lines)
        description = self._description_from_lines(lines) or listing_text

        salary_min, salary_max, currency, period = parse_salary_text(
            f"Salaire proposÃ© {salary_text}" if salary_text else ""
        )
        min_years, max_years = extract_years(experience or description)
        full_context = " ".join([title, contract, availability, description])
        remote = any(term in norm_text(full_context) for term in ["remote", "teletravail", "tÃ©lÃ©travail", "a distance", "Ã  distance"])

        external_match = re.search(r"/offres-emploi/(\d+)/", url)
        external_id = external_match.group(1) if external_match else None

        return JobRecord(
            title=title,
            company=company,
            location=location,
            description=description,
            employment_type=detect_job_type(f"{contract} {availability} {description}"),
            remote=remote,
            seniority="",
            min_years=min_years,
            max_years=max_years,
            salary_min=salary_min,
            salary_max=salary_max,
            salary_currency=currency,
            salary_period=period,
            posted_at=publication_dt,
            source_name=self.name,
            source_url=url,
            external_id=external_id,
            raw_payload={
                "detail_page": True,
                "reference": self._value_after_label(lines, "RÃ©fÃ©rence"),
                "company": company,
                "location": location,
                "contract": contract,
                "experience": experience,
                "education": education,
                "salary": salary_text,
                "availability": availability,
                "publication": publication,
                "listing_text": listing_text,
            },
        )

    def fetch(self, limit: int) -> list[JobRecord]:
        pages = self.config.get("pages") or []
        found: dict[str, dict] = {}

        for page_url in pages:
            if len(found) >= limit:
                break
            if not self._robots_allows(page_url):
                continue

            html = self.get_text(page_url)
            soup = BeautifulSoup(html, "html.parser")
            for anchor in soup.find_all("a", href=True):
                href = anchor.get("href", "")
                if not re.search(r"/offres-emploi/\d+/", href):
                    continue
                absolute = urljoin(page_url, href)
                text = clean_text(anchor.get_text(" ", strip=True))
                if not text or norm_text(text) in {"voir l offre", "voir offre", "postuler"}:
                    continue
                card = self._best_card(anchor)
                card_text = clean_text(card.get_text(" ", strip=True)) if card else text
                if absolute not in found or len(text) > len(found[absolute]["title"]):
                    found[absolute] = {"title": text, "card_text": card_text}
                if len(found) >= limit:
                    break

        out: list[JobRecord] = []
        for url, item in list(found.items())[:limit]:
            try:
                out.append(self.fetch_detail(url, item["title"], item["card_text"]))
            except Exception:
                # Detail enrichment should not make the whole collector brittle.
                title = item["title"]
                card_text = item["card_text"]
                location = first_match(card_text, TUNISIA_LOCATIONS) or "Tunisia"
                salary_min, salary_max, currency, period = parse_salary_text(card_text)
                out.append(JobRecord(
                    title=title,
                    company="Unknown",
                    location=location,
                    description=card_text,
                    employment_type=detect_job_type(card_text),
                    remote=any(term in norm_text(card_text) for term in ["remote", "teletravail", "a distance"]),
                    salary_min=salary_min,
                    salary_max=salary_max,
                    salary_currency=currency,
                    salary_period=period,
                    posted_at=None,
                    source_name=self.name,
                    source_url=url,
                    external_id=(re.search(r"/offres-emploi/(\d+)/", url).group(1) if re.search(r"/offres-emploi/(\d+)/", url) else None),
                    raw_payload={"detail_page": False, "listing_text": card_text},
                ))
        return out

from __future__ import annotations

from .base import BaseCollector
from ..models import JobRecord
from ..normalize import clean_text, parse_date, parse_salary_text


class RemotiveCollector(BaseCollector):
    name = "remotive"

    def fetch(self, limit: int) -> list[JobRecord]:
        payload = self.get_json(self.config["base_url"])
        out: list[JobRecord] = []

        for raw in payload.get("jobs", [])[:limit]:
            smin, smax, curr, period = parse_salary_text(raw.get("salary") or "")
            tags = raw.get("tags") or []
            description = clean_text(raw.get("description"))
            if tags:
                description = description + " Skills/tags: " + ", ".join(map(str, tags))
            out.append(
                JobRecord(
                    title=raw.get("title") or "Untitled job",
                    company=raw.get("company_name") or "Unknown",
                    location=raw.get("candidate_required_location") or "",
                    description=description,
                    employment_type=raw.get("job_type") or "",
                    remote=True,
                    remote_region=raw.get("candidate_required_location") or "",
                    salary_min=smin,
                    salary_max=smax,
                    salary_currency=curr,
                    salary_period=period,
                    posted_at=parse_date(raw.get("publication_date")),
                    source_name=self.name,
                    source_url=raw.get("url") or "",
                    external_id=str(raw.get("id") or ""),
                    raw_payload=raw,
                )
            )
        return [x for x in out if x.source_url]

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

from rapidfuzz import fuzz
from sqlalchemy import (
    Boolean,
    DateTime,
    Float,
    ForeignKey,
    Integer,
    String,
    Text,
    create_engine,
    inspect,
    select,
    text,
)
from sqlalchemy.orm import DeclarativeBase, Mapped, Session, mapped_column, relationship

from .models import JobRecord, ScoreResult
from .normalize import norm_text


class Base(DeclarativeBase):
    pass


class JobORM(Base):
    __tablename__ = "jobs"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    title: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(500))
    company: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(300), default="Unknown")
    location: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(300), default="")
    description: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")
    employment_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), default="")
    remote: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False)
    remote_region: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(200), default="")
    seniority: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), default="")
    min_years: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True)
    max_years: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True)
    salary_min: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)
    salary_max: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)
    salary_currency: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(20), nullable=True)
    salary_period: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(30), nullable=True)
    posted_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True)
    stale: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False)

    # V0.5 Global Opportunity Core. Historical rows remain type=job.
    opportunity_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20), default="job")
    country: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120), default="")
    city: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(160), default="")
    work_mode: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), default="")
    deadline: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True)
    last_verified_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True)
    is_open: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=True)

    education_requirement: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")
    visa_status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(60), default="unknown")
    visa_sponsorship: Mapped[bool | None] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=True)
    international_candidates: Mapped[bool | None] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=True)
    relocation_support: Mapped[bool | None] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=True)

    funding_status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), default="")
    tuition_covered: Mapped[bool | None] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=True)
    housing_support: Mapped[bool | None] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=True)
    insurance_support: Mapped[bool | None] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=True)
    travel_support: Mapped[bool | None] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=True)
    benefits_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="{}")

    technical_score: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    eligibility_score: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    location_score: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    experience_score: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    education_score: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    domain_score: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    visa_score: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    salary_score: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    language_score: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    overall_score: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    priority: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20), default="discard")
    eligibility_status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), default="needs_verification")
    score_explanation_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="[]")

    # V0.4 separates market-review decisions from application workflow.
    review_status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), default="unreviewed")
    verdict: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), default="needs_verification")
    required_languages_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="[]")
    requirements_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="{}")
    extraction_confidence_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="{}")

    application_status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), default="not_started")
    application_date: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True)
    cv_version: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(200), nullable=True)
    cover_letter_version: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(200), nullable=True)
    response: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=True)
    interview_stage: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(120), nullable=True)
    notes: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=True)

    canonical_url: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")
    norm_title: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(500), index=True)
    norm_company: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(300), index=True)
    norm_location: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(300), index=True)

    first_seen_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc))
    last_seen_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc))
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc))
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc))

    sources: Mapped[list["JobSourceORM"]] = relationship(back_populates="job", cascade="all, delete-orphan")


class JobSourceORM(Base):
    __tablename__ = "job_sources"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    job_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("jobs.id"), index=True)
    source_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(100), index=True)
    source_url: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, unique=True)
    external_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(300), nullable=True)
    raw_payload_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="{}")
    first_seen_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc))
    last_seen_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc))

    job: Mapped[JobORM] = relationship(back_populates="sources")


def get_engine(db_path: str):
    path = Path(db_path)
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    return create_engine(f"sqlite:///{path.as_posix()}", future=True)


def _ensure_v04_columns(engine) -> None:
    """Idempotent schema evolution through V0.5.0.

    The historical function name is retained because older scripts import or
    conceptually depend on it, but it now owns all additive migrations.
    """
    inspector = inspect(engine)
    if "jobs" not in inspector.get_table_names():
        return
    existing = {column["name"] for column in inspector.get_columns("jobs")}
    additions = {
        # V0.4 columns
        "review_status": "VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'unreviewed'",
        "verdict": "VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'needs_verification'",
        "required_languages_json": "TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'",
        "requirements_json": "TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'",
        "extraction_confidence_json": "TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'",
        # V0.5 opportunity identity / geography / freshness
        "opportunity_type": "VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'job'",
        "country": "VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL DEFAULT ''",
        "city": "VARCHAR(160) NOT NULL DEFAULT ''",
        "work_mode": "VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL DEFAULT ''",
        "deadline": "DATETIME",
        "last_verified_at": "DATETIME",
        "is_open": "BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT 1",
        # V0.5 eligibility / funding metadata
        "education_requirement": "TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''",
        "visa_status": "VARCHAR(60) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'unknown'",
        "visa_sponsorship": "BOOLEAN",
        "international_candidates": "BOOLEAN",
        "relocation_support": "BOOLEAN",
        "funding_status": "VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL DEFAULT ''",
        "tuition_covered": "BOOLEAN",
        "housing_support": "BOOLEAN",
        "insurance_support": "BOOLEAN",
        "travel_support": "BOOLEAN",
        "benefits_json": "TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'",
        # V0.5 TarekFit V2 components
        "education_score": "INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0",
        "domain_score": "INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0",
        "visa_score": "INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0",
        "salary_score": "INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0",
        "language_score": "INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0",
        "priority": "VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'discard'",
        "eligibility_status": "VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'needs_verification'",
        # richer application tracking
        "cover_letter_version": "VARCHAR(200)",
        "response": "TEXT",
        "interview_stage": "VARCHAR(120)",
    }
    with engine.begin() as conn:
        for name, ddl in additions.items():
            if name not in existing:
                conn.execute(text(f"ALTER TABLE jobs ADD COLUMN {name} {ddl}"))

        # Migrate old overloaded statuses once. The operation is idempotent.
        conn.execute(text("UPDATE jobs SET review_status='shortlisted', application_status='not_started' WHERE application_status='shortlisted'"))
        conn.execute(text("UPDATE jobs SET review_status='dismissed', application_status='not_started' WHERE application_status IN ('rejected_by_you','rejected_by_user')"))
        conn.execute(text("UPDATE jobs SET application_status='not_started' WHERE application_status='discovered'"))
        conn.execute(text("UPDATE jobs SET review_status='unreviewed' WHERE review_status IS NULL OR review_status=''"))
        conn.execute(text("UPDATE jobs SET verdict='needs_verification' WHERE verdict IS NULL OR verdict=''"))
        conn.execute(text("UPDATE jobs SET opportunity_type='job' WHERE opportunity_type IS NULL OR opportunity_type=''"))
        conn.execute(text("UPDATE jobs SET priority='discard' WHERE priority IS NULL OR priority=''"))
        conn.execute(text("UPDATE jobs SET eligibility_status='needs_verification' WHERE eligibility_status IS NULL OR eligibility_status=''"))
        conn.execute(text("UPDATE jobs SET visa_status='unknown' WHERE visa_status IS NULL OR visa_status=''"))
        conn.execute(text("UPDATE jobs SET is_open=1 WHERE is_open IS NULL"))

        # Remove the concrete false-positive observed in V0.3: a year parsed as salary.
        conn.execute(text("""
            UPDATE jobs
            SET salary_min=NULL, salary_max=NULL, salary_currency=NULL, salary_period=NULL
            WHERE salary_min BETWEEN 1900 AND 2100
              AND salary_max BETWEEN 1900 AND 2100
              AND (salary_currency IS NULL OR salary_currency='')
        """))


def init_db(engine) -> None:
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    _ensure_v04_columns(engine)


def _same_remote_family(existing: JobORM, record: JobRecord) -> bool:
    if existing.remote and record.remote:
        return True
    a = norm_text(existing.location)
    b = norm_text(record.location)
    return bool(a and b and (a == b or a in b or b in a))


def _find_duplicate(session: Session, record: JobRecord) -> JobORM | None:
    normalized_company = norm_text(record.company)
    normalized_title = norm_text(record.title)
    normalized_location = norm_text(record.location)

    exact = session.scalar(
        select(JobORM).where(
            JobORM.opportunity_type == (record.opportunity_type or "job").lower(),
            JobORM.norm_company == normalized_company,
            JobORM.norm_title == normalized_title,
            JobORM.norm_location == normalized_location,
        )
    )
    if exact:
        return exact

    if not normalized_company or normalized_company == "unknown":
        return None

    candidates = session.scalars(
        select(JobORM).where(
            JobORM.norm_company == normalized_company,
            JobORM.opportunity_type == (record.opportunity_type or "job").lower(),
        )
    ).all()
    for candidate in candidates:
        title_score = fuzz.token_set_ratio(candidate.norm_title, normalized_title)
        company_score = fuzz.token_set_ratio(candidate.norm_company, normalized_company)
        if title_score >= 92 and company_score >= 95 and _same_remote_family(candidate, record):
            return candidate
    return None


def _valid_company(value: str | None) -> bool:
    n = norm_text(value)
    if not n or n in {"unknown", "entreprise anonyme"}:
        return False
    # Common Keejob sector labels that V0.1 accidentally stored as companies.
    bad = [
        "informatique telecoms", "recrutement ressources humaines", "banque finance assurances",
        "architecture immobilier btp", "sante paramedical optique", "call center televente",
        "agriculture agro alimentaire environnement",
    ]
    return n not in bad


def _apply_scores(job: JobORM, scores: ScoreResult) -> None:
    job.technical_score = scores.technical
    job.eligibility_score = scores.eligibility
    job.location_score = scores.location
    job.experience_score = scores.experience
    job.education_score = scores.education
    job.domain_score = scores.domain
    job.visa_score = scores.visa
    job.salary_score = scores.salary
    job.language_score = scores.language
    job.overall_score = scores.overall
    job.priority = scores.priority
    job.eligibility_status = scores.eligibility_status
    job.verdict = scores.verdict
    job.score_explanation_json = json.dumps(scores.explanations, ensure_ascii=False)
    job.requirements_json = json.dumps(scores.requirements, ensure_ascii=False, default=str)
    job.required_languages_json = json.dumps(scores.requirements.get("languages", []), ensure_ascii=False, default=str)
    job.extraction_confidence_json = json.dumps(scores.confidence, ensure_ascii=False, default=str)
    if not job.country:
        job.country = str(scores.requirements.get("country") or "")
    if not job.work_mode:
        job.work_mode = "remote" if job.remote else "onsite_or_hybrid"


def _apply_record_metadata(job: JobORM, record: JobRecord) -> None:
    if record.title and (not job.title or len(record.title) >= len(job.title)):
        job.title = record.title
        job.norm_title = norm_text(record.title)
    if _valid_company(record.company) and (not _valid_company(job.company) or norm_text(record.company) != norm_text(job.company)):
        job.company = record.company
        job.norm_company = norm_text(record.company)
    if record.location:
        job.location = record.location
        job.norm_location = norm_text(record.location)
    job.opportunity_type = (record.opportunity_type or job.opportunity_type or "job").lower()
    if record.country:
        job.country = record.country
    if record.city:
        job.city = record.city
    if record.work_mode:
        job.work_mode = record.work_mode
    if record.deadline is not None:
        job.deadline = record.deadline
    if record.last_verified_at is not None:
        job.last_verified_at = record.last_verified_at
    job.is_open = bool(record.is_open)
    if record.education_requirement:
        job.education_requirement = record.education_requirement
    if record.visa_status and record.visa_status != "unknown":
        job.visa_status = record.visa_status
    if record.visa_sponsorship is not None:
        job.visa_sponsorship = record.visa_sponsorship
    if record.international_candidates is not None:
        job.international_candidates = record.international_candidates
    if record.relocation_support is not None:
        job.relocation_support = record.relocation_support
    if record.funding_status:
        job.funding_status = record.funding_status
    if record.tuition_covered is not None:
        job.tuition_covered = record.tuition_covered
    if record.housing_support is not None:
        job.housing_support = record.housing_support
    if record.insurance_support is not None:
        job.insurance_support = record.insurance_support
    if record.travel_support is not None:
        job.travel_support = record.travel_support
    if record.benefits:
        job.benefits_json = json.dumps(record.benefits, ensure_ascii=False, default=str)
    if len(record.description or "") > len(job.description or ""):
        job.description = record.description
    if record.employment_type:
        job.employment_type = record.employment_type
    if record.seniority:
        job.seniority = record.seniority
    job.min_years = record.min_years
    job.max_years = record.max_years
    if record.posted_at is not None:
        job.posted_at = record.posted_at
    if record.salary_min is not None:
        job.salary_min = record.salary_min
        job.salary_max = record.salary_max
        job.salary_currency = record.salary_currency
        job.salary_period = record.salary_period
    elif job.salary_min is not None and 1900 <= job.salary_min <= 2100 and not job.salary_currency:
        job.salary_min = None
        job.salary_max = None
        job.salary_period = None
    job.remote = bool(record.remote)
    if record.remote_region:
        job.remote_region = record.remote_region


def upsert_job(session: Session, record: JobRecord, scores: ScoreResult, stale: bool) -> tuple[JobORM, str]:
    existing_source = session.scalar(select(JobSourceORM).where(JobSourceORM.source_url == record.source_url))
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

    if existing_source:
        job = existing_source.job
        _apply_record_metadata(job, record)
        _apply_scores(job, scores)
        job.stale = stale
        job.last_seen_at = now
        job.last_verified_at = record.last_verified_at or now
        job.updated_at = now
        existing_source.last_seen_at = now
        existing_source.raw_payload_json = json.dumps(record.raw_payload, ensure_ascii=False, default=str)
        return job, "seen_again"

    job = _find_duplicate(session, record)
    action = "merged" if job else "inserted"

    if not job:
        job = JobORM(
            title=record.title,
            company=record.company or "Unknown",
            location=record.location,
            description=record.description,
            employment_type=record.employment_type,
            remote=record.remote,
            remote_region=record.remote_region,
            seniority=record.seniority,
            min_years=record.min_years,
            max_years=record.max_years,
            salary_min=record.salary_min,
            salary_max=record.salary_max,
            salary_currency=record.salary_currency,
            salary_period=record.salary_period,
            posted_at=record.posted_at,
            stale=stale,
            opportunity_type=(record.opportunity_type or "job").lower(),
            country=record.country,
            city=record.city,
            work_mode=record.work_mode,
            deadline=record.deadline,
            last_verified_at=record.last_verified_at or now,
            is_open=record.is_open,
            education_requirement=record.education_requirement,
            visa_status=record.visa_status or "unknown",
            visa_sponsorship=record.visa_sponsorship,
            international_candidates=record.international_candidates,
            relocation_support=record.relocation_support,
            funding_status=record.funding_status,
            tuition_covered=record.tuition_covered,
            housing_support=record.housing_support,
            insurance_support=record.insurance_support,
            travel_support=record.travel_support,
            benefits_json=json.dumps(record.benefits, ensure_ascii=False, default=str),
            review_status="unreviewed",
            application_status="not_started",
            canonical_url=record.source_url,
            norm_title=norm_text(record.title),
            norm_company=norm_text(record.company),
            norm_location=norm_text(record.location),
            first_seen_at=now,
            last_seen_at=now,
            created_at=now,
            updated_at=now,
        )
        _apply_scores(job, scores)
        session.add(job)
        session.flush()
    else:
        _apply_record_metadata(job, record)
        _apply_scores(job, scores)
        job.stale = job.stale and stale
        job.last_seen_at = now
        job.last_verified_at = record.last_verified_at or now
        job.updated_at = now

    source = JobSourceORM(
        job_id=job.id,
        source_name=record.source_name,
        source_url=record.source_url,
        external_id=record.external_id,
        raw_payload_json=json.dumps(record.raw_payload, ensure_ascii=False, default=str),
        first_seen_at=now,
        last_seen_at=now,
    )
    session.add(source)
    return job, action

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import json
import logging
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

import yaml
from rich.console import Console
from rich.table import Table
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, selectinload

from .collectors.arbeitnow import ArbeitnowCollector
from .collectors.jobicy import JobicyCollector
from .collectors.keejob import KeejobCollector
from .collectors.remotive import RemotiveCollector
from .database import JobORM, get_engine, init_db, upsert_job
from .matcher import is_stale, score_job

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
console = Console()

COLLECTORS = {
    "jobicy": JobicyCollector,
    "remotive": RemotiveCollector,
    "arbeitnow": ArbeitnowCollector,
    "keejob": KeejobCollector,
}


def load_yaml(path: Path) -> dict:
    with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        return yaml.safe_load(f) or {}


def load_json(path: Path) -> dict:
    with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        return json.load(f)


def setup_logging(settings: dict) -> Path:
    log_dir = ROOT / settings.get("logging", {}).get("directory", "logs")
    log_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    stamp = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
    log_path = log_dir / f"dry_run_{stamp}.log"
    level = getattr(logging, settings.get("logging", {}).get("level", "INFO").upper(), logging.INFO)
    logging.basicConfig(
        level=level,
        format="%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(message)s",
        handlers=[logging.FileHandler(log_path, encoding="utf-8"), logging.StreamHandler()],
        force=True,
    )
    return log_path


def _poll_state_path() -> Path:
    return ROOT / "data" / "source_poll_state.json"


def load_poll_state() -> dict:
    path = _poll_state_path()
    if not path.exists():
        return {}
    try:
        return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except Exception:
        return {}


def save_poll_state(state: dict) -> None:
    path = _poll_state_path()
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path.write_text(json.dumps(state, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")


def poll_allowed(source_name: str, source_cfg: dict, state: dict) -> bool:
    last = state.get(source_name)
    if not last:
        return True
    try:
        last_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(last)
        if last_dt.tzinfo is None:
            last_dt = last_dt.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    except ValueError:
        return True
    min_minutes = int(source_cfg.get("min_interval_minutes", 0))
    return (datetime.now(timezone.utc) - last_dt).total_seconds() / 60 >= min_minutes


def save_raw_snapshot(run_id: str, source_name: str, records) -> None:
    raw_dir = ROOT / "data" / "raw"
    raw_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    payload = [r.model_dump(mode="json") for r in records]
    (raw_dir / f"{run_id}_{source_name}.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, default=str), encoding="utf-8"
    )


def export_top_matches(engine, path: Path) -> int:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with Session(engine) as session:
        jobs = session.scalars(
            select(JobORM).where(JobORM.stale.is_(False)).order_by(JobORM.overall_score.desc(), JobORM.posted_at.desc())
        ).all()
    fields = [
        "opportunity_type", "priority", "eligibility_status", "verdict", "score", "review_status",
        "title", "company", "country", "city", "location", "remote", "seniority",
        "employment_type", "posted_at", "deadline", "visa_status", "funding_status",
        "source_url", "application_status",
    ]
    with path.open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8-sig") as f:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=fields)
        writer.writeheader()
        for job in jobs:
            writer.writerow({
                "opportunity_type": job.opportunity_type or "job",
                "priority": job.priority,
                "eligibility_status": job.eligibility_status,
                "verdict": job.verdict,
                "score": job.overall_score,
                "review_status": job.review_status,
                "title": job.title,
                "company": job.company,
                "country": job.country,
                "city": job.city,
                "location": job.location,
                "remote": job.remote,
                "seniority": job.seniority,
                "employment_type": job.employment_type,
                "posted_at": job.posted_at.isoformat() if job.posted_at else "",
                "deadline": job.deadline.isoformat() if job.deadline else "",
                "visa_status": job.visa_status,
                "funding_status": job.funding_status,
                "source_url": job.canonical_url,
                "application_status": job.application_status,
            })
    return len(jobs)


def show_top_jobs(engine, limit: int = 15) -> None:
    with Session(engine) as session:
        jobs = session.scalars(
            select(JobORM)
            .options(selectinload(JobORM.sources))
            .where(JobORM.stale.is_(False))
            .order_by(JobORM.overall_score.desc(), JobORM.posted_at.desc())
            .limit(limit)
        ).all()
    table = Table(title="Top live matches from the dry run")
    table.add_column("Type")
    table.add_column("Priority")
    table.add_column("Eligibility")
    table.add_column("Score", justify="right")
    table.add_column("Title", overflow="fold")
    table.add_column("Company")
    table.add_column("Location")
    table.add_column("Source")
    for job in jobs:
        source = job.sources[0].source_name if job.sources else ""
        table.add_row(job.opportunity_type or "job", job.priority or "", job.eligibility_status or "", str(job.overall_score), job.title, job.company, job.location, source)
    console.print(table)


def dry_run(
    max_jobs: int | None = None,
    ignore_poll_interval: bool = False,
    selected_sources: list[str] | None = None,
) -> int:
    settings = load_yaml(ROOT / "config" / "settings.yaml")
    sources_cfg = load_yaml(ROOT / "config" / "sources.yaml").get("sources", {})
    profile = load_json(ROOT / "config" / "profile.json")
    log_path = setup_logging(settings)

    configured_max = int(settings.get("collection", {}).get("max_jobs_per_dry_run", 100))
    max_jobs = min(max_jobs or configured_max, configured_max)
    db_path = ROOT / settings.get("storage", {}).get("sqlite_path", "data/jobs.db")
    engine = get_engine(str(db_path))
    init_db(engine)

    requested = {x.strip().lower() for x in (selected_sources or []) if x.strip()}
    enabled = [
        (name, cfg) for name, cfg in sources_cfg.items()
        if cfg.get("enabled", True) and (not requested or name.lower() in requested)
    ]
    if requested:
        unknown = sorted(requested - {name.lower() for name in sources_cfg})
        if unknown:
            console.print(f"[yellow]Unknown source(s) ignored: {', '.join(unknown)}[/yellow]")
    if not enabled:
        console.print("[red]No enabled collectors match the selected source scope.[/red]")
        return 3

    run_id = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
    poll_state = load_poll_state()
    records = []
    errors: dict[str, str] = {}
    per_source_default = max(1, max_jobs // max(1, len(enabled)))

    console.print(f"\n[bold]TTEA dry run[/bold] - max {max_jobs} raw listings")
    logging.info("Starting dry run with %s selected/enabled sources", len(enabled))

    for source_name, cfg in enabled:
        if len(records) >= max_jobs:
            break
        source_limit = min(int(cfg.get("max_jobs", per_source_default)), per_source_default, max_jobs - len(records))
        if not ignore_poll_interval and not poll_allowed(source_name, cfg, poll_state):
            msg = f"Skipped: minimum poll interval has not elapsed ({cfg.get('min_interval_minutes', 0)} min)."
            console.print(f"[yellow][{source_name.upper()}][/yellow] {msg}")
            logging.info("[%s] %s", source_name.upper(), msg)
            continue

        collector_cls = COLLECTORS.get(source_name)
        if not collector_cls:
            errors[source_name] = "No collector implementation"
            continue
        collector = collector_cls(cfg, settings)
        try:
            console.print(f"[cyan][{source_name.upper()}][/cyan] collecting up to {source_limit} jobs...")
            fetched = collector.fetch(source_limit)
            save_raw_snapshot(run_id, source_name, fetched)
            records.extend(fetched)
            poll_state[source_name] = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
            console.print(f"[green][{source_name.upper()}][/green] {len(fetched)} jobs fetched")
            logging.info("[%s] %s jobs fetched", source_name.upper(), len(fetched))
        except Exception as exc:
            errors[source_name] = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
            console.print(f"[red][{source_name.upper()}][/red] failed: {exc}")
            logging.exception("[%s] collector failed", source_name.upper())
        finally:
            collector.close()

    save_poll_state(poll_state)
    counts = {"inserted": 0, "merged": 0, "seen_again": 0}
    stale_count = 0
    stale_after = int(settings.get("collection", {}).get("stale_after_days", 45))

    with Session(engine) as session:
        for record in records[:max_jobs]:
            scores = score_job(record, profile, settings)
            stale = is_stale(record, stale_after)
            if stale:
                stale_count += 1
            _, action = upsert_job(session, record, scores, stale)
            counts[action] = counts.get(action, 0) + 1
        session.commit()

    export_path = ROOT / settings.get("storage", {}).get("export_top_matches_csv", "data/exports/top_matches.csv")
    exported = export_top_matches(engine, export_path)
    show_top_jobs(engine)

    console.print("\n[bold]Dry-run summary[/bold]")
    console.print(f"Raw jobs fetched:   {len(records[:max_jobs])}")
    console.print(f"New canonical jobs: {counts.get('inserted', 0)}")
    console.print(f"Duplicates merged:  {counts.get('merged', 0)}")
    console.print(f"Already seen URLs:  {counts.get('seen_again', 0)}")
    console.print(f"Marked stale:       {stale_count}")
    console.print(f"Collector failures: {len(errors)}")
    console.print(f"Live jobs exported: {exported}")
    console.print(f"SQLite database:    {db_path}")
    console.print(f"CSV export:         {export_path}")
    console.print(f"Log file:           {log_path}")

    if errors:
        console.print("\n[yellow]Some collectors failed, but the pipeline continued:[/yellow]")
        for name, err in errors.items():
            console.print(f"  - {name}: {err}")
    if not records:
        console.print("\n[red]No listings were fetched from any selected source.[/red]")
        return 2
    console.print("\n[green bold]DRY RUN PASSED[/green bold] - live data reached SQLite and the ranking export.")
    return 0


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="TTEA personal job-market collector")
    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command")
    dry = sub.add_parser("dry-run", help="Fetch a live batch and run the full pipeline")
    dry.add_argument("--limit", type=int, default=None)
    dry.add_argument("--ignore-poll-interval", action="store_true")
    dry.add_argument("--sources", type=str, default="", help="Comma-separated collector names; empty means all enabled")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.command in (None, "dry-run"):
        selected = [x.strip() for x in getattr(args, "sources", "").split(",") if x.strip()]
        return dry_run(
            max_jobs=getattr(args, "limit", None),
            ignore_poll_interval=getattr(args, "ignore_poll_interval", False),
            selected_sources=selected,
        )
    return 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

def _contains_phrase(text: str, phrase: str) -> bool:
    """Token/phrase matching; 'ai' must not match inside 'italien'."""
    t = norm_text(text)
    p = norm_text(phrase)
    if not p:
        return False
    return re.search(rf"(?<![a-z0-9]){re.escape(p)}(?![a-z0-9])", t) is not None


def _title_seniority(title: str) -> str:
    # Title is authoritative. French supervisory titles are included explicitly.
    t = f" {norm_text(title)} "
    ordered = [
        ("director", [r"\bdirector\b", r"\bdirecteur\b", r"\bdirectrice\b", r"\bhead of\b", r"\bchief\b", r"\bvice president\b", r"\bvp\b"]),
        ("principal", [r"\bprincipal\b"]),
        ("staff", [r"\bstaff\b"]),
        ("senior", [r"\bsenior\b", r"\bsr\b", r"\bconfirme\b", r"\bconfirmee\b"]),
        ("lead", [r"\bteam lead\b", r"\bteam leader\b", r"\btech lead\b", r"\blead\b", r"\bchef d equipe\b", r"\bchef equipe\b", r"\bsuperviseur\b", r"\bsupervisor\b"]),
        ("manager", [r"\bmanager\b", r"\bresponsable\b"]),
        ("mid-level", [r"\bmid level\b", r"\bmid-level\b", r"\bintermediate\b"]),
        ("intern", [r"\bintern\b", r"\binternship\b", r"\bstage\b", r"\bstagiaire\b"]),
        ("graduate", [r"\bgraduate\b", r"\bfresh graduate\b", r"\bnew grad\b", r"\bjeune diplome\b"]),
        ("junior", [r"\bjunior\b", r"\bjr\b"]),
        ("entry-level", [r"\bentry level\b", r"\bentry-level\b", r"\bdebutant\b", r"\bdebutante\b"]),
        ("associate", [r"\bassociate\b"]),
    ]
    for label, patterns in ordered:
        if any(re.search(pattern, t, re.I) for pattern in patterns):
            return label
    return ""


def _effective_seniority(job: JobRecord) -> str:
    """Prefer title evidence and distrust stale derived seniority metadata.

    Earlier collectors sometimes persisted a seniority inferred from broad
    description text. That value can outlive later parser fixes. A high-impact
    seniority value is therefore accepted only when current text supports it.
    """
    title_level = _title_seniority(job.title)
    if title_level:
        return title_level

    stored = norm_text(job.seniority or "")
    description_level = detect_seniority(job.description or "")

    if stored and description_level and stored == description_level:
        return stored

    # Experience requirements are scored separately, so an unspecified title
    # is safer than inventing Intern/Senior from unrelated boilerplate.
    return ""


def _title_role_relevance(title: str) -> tuple[str, list[str]]:
    non_target = [signal for signal in NON_TARGET_TITLE_SIGNALS if _contains_phrase(title, signal)]
    if non_target:
        return "non-target", non_target[:3]
    target = [signal for signal in TARGET_TITLE_SIGNALS if _contains_phrase(title, signal)]
    if target:
        return "target", target[:5]
    t = f" {norm_text(title)} "
    if any(term in t for term in [" engineer ", " ingenieur ", " ingÃ©nieur ", " analyst ", " support ", " specialist ", " consultant ", " research ", " technicien "]):
        return "adjacent", []
    return "unclear", []


def extract_required_languages(job: JobRecord, profile: dict) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    title = norm_text(job.title)
    description = norm_text(job.description)
    sentences = [norm_text(x) for x in re.split(r"[\n\r.!?;:]+", job.description or "") if norm_text(x)]
    result: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

    for canonical, aliases in LANGUAGE_ALIASES.items():
        alias_hit = next((a for a in aliases if _contains_phrase(title, a)), None)
        evidence = ""
        mandatory = False
        optional = False

        if alias_hit:
            mandatory = True
            evidence = f"Title mentions {canonical}"
        else:
            for sentence in sentences:
                found = next((a for a in aliases if _contains_phrase(sentence, a)), None)
                if not found:
                    continue
                optional_here = any(signal in sentence for signal in OPTIONAL_LANGUAGE_SIGNALS)
                mandatory_here = any(signal in sentence for signal in MANDATORY_LANGUAGE_SIGNALS)
                if optional_here and not mandatory_here:
                    optional = True
                    evidence = sentence[:220]
                    continue
                if mandatory_here:
                    mandatory = True
                    evidence = sentence[:220]
                    break

        if mandatory or optional:
            result.append({
                "language": canonical,
                "mandatory": bool(mandatory),
                "optional": bool(optional and not mandatory),
                "evidence": evidence,
            })

    return result


def extract_requirements(job: JobRecord, profile: dict) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], dict[str, str]]:
    text = f"{job.title} {job.description}"
    min_years = job.min_years
    max_years = job.max_years
    if min_years is None and max_years is None:
        min_years, max_years = extract_years(text)

    languages = extract_required_languages(job, profile)
    detected_terms: list[str] = []
    for keyword in profile.get("target_keywords", []) + profile.get("skills", []):
        if _contains_phrase(text, keyword):
            detected_terms.append(str(keyword))
    detected_terms = list(dict.fromkeys(detected_terms))[:18]

    education_terms = []
    t = norm_text(text)
    for label, patterns in {
        "Bac+3 / Licence / Bachelor": ["bac+3", "licence", "bachelor"],
        "Bac+5 / Master": ["bac+5", "master", "mastÃ¨re", "mastere"],
        "Engineering degree": ["engineering degree", "diplome d ingenieur", "diplÃ´me d ingÃ©nieur", "ingenieur"],
        "Doctoral / PhD": ["phd", "doctoral", "doctorate", "doctorat"],
    }.items():
        if any(norm_text(p) in t for p in patterns):
            education_terms.append(label)

    requirements = {
        "languages": languages,
        "experience": {"min_years": min_years, "max_years": max_years},
        "education_signals": education_terms,
        "technical_terms": detected_terms,
        "employment_type": job.employment_type or detect_job_type(text),
        "seniority": _effective_seniority(job),
    }

    confidence = {
        "languages": "high" if languages else "medium",
        "experience": "high" if min_years is not None else "medium",
        "salary": "high" if job.salary_min is not None and job.salary_currency else ("medium" if job.salary_min is not None else "low"),
        "posted_at": "high" if job.posted_at is not None else "low",
        "company": "low" if norm_text(job.company) in {"", "unknown", "entreprise anonyme"} else "medium",
        "location": "high" if job.location else "low",
    }
    return requirements, confidence


def _technical_score(job: JobRecord, profile: dict) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
    title_hits: list[str] = []
    body_hits: list[str] = []
    for keyword in profile.get("target_keywords", []) + profile.get("skills", []):
        if not norm_text(keyword):
            continue
        if _contains_phrase(job.title, keyword):
            title_hits.append(str(keyword))
        elif _contains_phrase(job.description, keyword):
            body_hits.append(str(keyword))

    unique_title = list(dict.fromkeys(title_hits))
    unique_body = list(dict.fromkeys(body_hits))
    score = min(100, len(unique_title) * 18 + len(unique_body) * 5)
    role_class, title_signals = _title_role_relevance(job.title)
    details: list[str] = []

    if (job.opportunity_type or "job").lower() == "phd":
        # Research titles often do not repeat implementation keywords in the title.
        score = max(score, 45 if unique_body else 35)
        details.append("MATCH: Doctoral/research opportunity uses the research-fit pathway")
    elif role_class == "non-target":
        score = min(score, 15)
        details.append("BLOCKER: Title is outside configured role families")
    elif role_class == "unclear":
        score = min(score, 40)
        details.append("VERIFY: Title has no clear configured role-family signal")
    elif role_class == "adjacent":
        score = max(score, 25)
        score = min(score, 65)
        details.append("VERIFY: Title is adjacent to the target market")
    else:
        score = max(score, 40)
        details.append("MATCH: Target title signal: " + ", ".join(title_signals))

    hits = (unique_title + unique_body)[:8]
    if hits:
        details.append("MATCH: Technical terms: " + ", ".join(hits))
    elif role_class not in {"non-target", "target"}:
        details.append("VERIFY: No strong target-skill keyword match detected")
    return score, details


COUNTRY_ALIASES = {
    "Germany": ["germany", "deutschland", "berlin", "munich", "mÃ¼nchen", "hamburg", "stuttgart", "frankfurt", "dresden"],
    "Netherlands": ["netherlands", "nederland", "amsterdam", "eindhoven", "enschede", "rotterdam", "delft", "maassluis", "venlo"],
    "Switzerland": ["switzerland", "schweiz", "suisse", "zurich", "zÃ¼rich", "lausanne", "geneva", "genÃ¨ve", "epfl", "eth"],
    "Denmark": ["denmark", "danmark", "copenhagen", "kÃ¸benhavn", "aalborg", "aarhus"],
    "Sweden": ["sweden", "sverige", "stockholm", "gothenburg", "gÃ¶teborg", "lund", "uppsala"],
    "Norway": ["norway", "norge", "oslo", "trondheim", "bergen"],
    "Belgium": ["belgium", "belgique", "brussels", "bruxelles", "leuven", "ghent", "gent"],
    "France": ["france", "paris", "lyon", "grenoble", "toulouse", "lille", "strasbourg"],
    "Austria": ["austria", "Ã¶sterreich", "vienna", "wien", "graz", "linz"],
    "Canada": ["canada", "toronto", "montreal", "montrÃ©al", "vancouver", "ottawa", "waterloo"],
    "USA": ["united states", "usa", "u.s.", "new york", "california", "boston", "seattle", "texas"],
    "UK": ["united kingdom", "uk", "england", "scotland", "london", "cambridge", "oxford", "manchester"],
    "Australia": ["australia", "sydney", "melbourne", "brisbane", "perth"],
    "Tunisia": ["tunisia", "tunisie", "tunis", "ariana", "ben arous", "manouba", "sousse", "sfax"],
}


def _country_from_job(job: JobRecord) -> str:
    if job.country:
        return job.country.strip()
    text = norm_text(f"{job.location} {job.remote_region}")
    for country, aliases in COUNTRY_ALIASES.items():
        if any(_contains_phrase(text, alias) for alias in aliases):
            return country
    return ""


def _country_tier(country: str, profile: dict) -> int | None:
    geo = profile.get("geography", {})
    n = norm_text(country)
    tiers = geo.get("international_priority_tiers", {})
    for label, countries in tiers.items():
        if n in {norm_text(x) for x in countries}:
            m = re.search(r"(\d+)", str(label))
            return int(m.group(1)) if m else None
    return None


def _hard_work_authorization_block(job: JobRecord) -> str | None:
    if job.visa_sponsorship is False or job.international_candidates is False:
        return "Employer metadata indicates no sponsorship/international eligibility"
    text = norm_text(f"{job.title} {job.description}")
    blockers = [
        "no visa sponsorship", "visa sponsorship is not available", "without sponsorship",
        "must be authorized to work", "must have the right to work", "existing right to work",
        "eu citizenship required", "eu citizen required", "european union citizen",
        "security clearance required", "us citizen required", "u.s. citizen required",
    ]
    for signal in blockers:
        if signal in text:
            return f"Work-authorization restriction detected: {signal}"
    return None


def _positive_mobility_signal(job: JobRecord) -> str | None:
    if job.visa_sponsorship is True:
        return "Employer explicitly indicates visa sponsorship"
    if job.relocation_support is True:
        return "Employer explicitly indicates relocation support"
    text = norm_text(f"{job.title} {job.description}")
    positives = [
        "visa sponsorship", "work permit support", "relocation package", "relocation support",
        "international applicants", "international candidates", "global mobility",
    ]
    for signal in positives:
        if signal in text:
            return f"Mobility signal detected: {signal}"
    return None


def _education_score(job: JobRecord, profile: dict, requirements: dict) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
    completed = [x for x in profile.get("education", []) if x.get("completed")]
    has_master = any("master" in norm_text(x.get("degree_level")) or "mastere" in norm_text(x.get("degree_level")) for x in completed)
    has_bachelor = bool(completed)
    signals = requirements.get("education_signals", [])
    text = norm_text(f"{job.education_requirement} {job.description}")

    if (job.opportunity_type or "job").lower() == "phd":
        if has_master:
            return 100, ["MATCH: Completed Master's degree supports doctoral eligibility"]
        return 20, ["BLOCKER: Doctoral opportunity normally requires a completed Master's-equivalent degree"]

    if any(x in text for x in ["phd required", "doctorate required"]):
        return 0, ["BLOCKER: Role explicitly requires a PhD/doctorate"]
    if signals:
        if has_master or has_bachelor:
            return 100, ["MATCH: Education level is compatible with detected degree requirement"]
        return 35, ["VERIFY: Degree requirement detected but profile education could not be confirmed"]
    return 80, ["VERIFY: No explicit education requirement detected"]


def _domain_score(job: JobRecord, profile: dict) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
    text = norm_text(f"{job.title} {job.description}")
    primary = [
        "robot", "ros", "ros2", "embedded", "firmware", "medical device", "biomedical",
        "mechatronic", "control", "automation", "autonomous", "sensor", "computer vision", "r&d",
    ]
    secondary = ["software", "python", "c++", "systems", "integration", "iot", "machine learning", "ai"]
    primary_hits = [x for x in primary if _contains_phrase(text, x)]
    secondary_hits = [x for x in secondary if _contains_phrase(text, x)]
    role_class, _ = _title_role_relevance(job.title)
    if role_class == "non-target" and (job.opportunity_type or "job").lower() == "job":
        return 5, ["BLOCKER: Opportunity domain is outside the configured career families"]
    if primary_hits:
        return min(100, 70 + 8 * min(4, len(primary_hits))), ["MATCH: Priority engineering domain: " + ", ".join(primary_hits[:5])]
    if (job.opportunity_type or "job").lower() == "phd" and secondary_hits:
        return 75, ["MATCH: Research domain overlaps software/intelligent-systems profile"]
    if secondary_hits:
        return 65, ["MATCH: Secondary software/AI domain overlap: " + ", ".join(secondary_hits[:4])]
    return 35, ["VERIFY: Domain fit is unclear"]


def _language_score(job: JobRecord, profile: dict, requirements: dict) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
    spoken = {norm_text(name) for name in profile.get("languages", {}).keys()}
    mandatory = [x for x in requirements.get("languages", []) if x.get("mandatory")]
    unsupported = [x["language"] for x in mandatory if norm_text(x.get("language")) not in spoken]
    if unsupported:
        return 0, ["BLOCKER: Mandatory language not in profile: " + ", ".join(unsupported)]
    if mandatory:
        return 100, ["MATCH: Mandatory language requirement is represented in profile"]
    optional_unsupported = [
        x["language"] for x in requirements.get("languages", [])
        if x.get("optional") and norm_text(x.get("language")) not in spoken
    ]
    if optional_unsupported:
        return 80, ["VERIFY: Optional language not in profile: " + ", ".join(optional_unsupported)]
    return 90, ["MATCH: No unsupported mandatory language detected"]


def _visa_score(job: JobRecord, profile: dict) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
    geo = profile.get("geography", {})
    country = _country_from_job(job)
    if job.remote:
        text = norm_text(f"{job.location} {job.remote_region}")
        if any(_contains_phrase(text, x) for x in ["worldwide", "anywhere", "global", "all countries"]):
            return 100, ["MATCH: Remote opportunity appears globally accessible"]
        if any(_contains_phrase(text, x) for x in ["emea", "africa", "mena", "middle east and africa"]):
            return 95, ["MATCH: Remote opportunity covers Africa/EMEA"]
        if any(_contains_phrase(text, x) for x in ["europe", "eu only", "europe only", "eu residents"]):
            return 35, ["VERIFY: Europe/EU remote eligibility requires confirmation for a Tunisia-based applicant"]
        for region_name, signals in REGION_RESTRICTED_SIGNALS.items():
            if any(_contains_phrase(text, signal) for signal in signals):
                return 5, [f"BLOCKER: Remote opportunity appears restricted to {region_name.upper()}"]
        return 70, ["VERIFY: Remote geographic eligibility is not explicit"]

    if norm_text(country) == "tunisia":
        return 100, ["MATCH: No international work permit is required for Tunisia"]

    hard = _hard_work_authorization_block(job)
    if hard:
        return 0, ["BLOCKER: " + hard]
    positive = _positive_mobility_signal(job)
    if positive:
        return 100, ["MATCH: " + positive]

    if geo.get("relocation") and geo.get("international_onsite"):
        tier = _country_tier(country, profile)
        if tier in {1, 2, 3}:
            return 60, [f"VERIFY: Target-country role (Tier {tier}); sponsorship/work-permit support is not stated"]
        if country:
            return 50, [f"VERIFY: International on-site role in {country}; work-permit feasibility is unknown"]
        return 45, ["VERIFY: International location/work-permit feasibility is unknown"]
    return 10, ["BLOCKER: International on-site relocation is disabled in profile"]


def _location_score(job: JobRecord, profile: dict) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
    geo = profile.get("geography", {})
    location = norm_text(job.location)
    region = norm_text(job.remote_region)
    text = norm_text(f"{job.location} {job.remote_region}")
    allowed = [norm_text(x) for x in geo.get("allowed_local_locations", [])]

    if job.remote:
        if any(_contains_phrase(text, x) for x in ["worldwide", "anywhere", "global", "all countries"]):
            return 100, ["MATCH: Remote role appears globally available"]
        if any(_contains_phrase(text, x) for x in ["emea", "africa", "middle east and africa", "mena"]):
            return 95, ["MATCH: Remote role targets EMEA/Africa"]
        if any(_contains_phrase(text, x) for x in ["europe", "european union", "eu only", "eu residents", "europe only"]):
            return 55, ["VERIFY: Europe/EU-restricted remote role; Tunisia eligibility needs verification"]
        for region_name, signals in REGION_RESTRICTED_SIGNALS.items():
            if any(_contains_phrase(text, signal) for signal in signals):
                return 15, [f"BLOCKER: Remote role appears restricted to {region_name.upper()}, not Tunisia"]
        if not location and not region:
            return 75, ["VERIFY: Remote role has unspecified geographic eligibility"]
        return 60, [f"VERIFY: Remote role has a geographic restriction: {job.location or job.remote_region or 'unknown'}"]

    country = _country_from_job(job)
    if norm_text(country) == "tunisia":
        if any(x and _contains_phrase(location, x) for x in allowed) or location == "tunis":
            return 90, ["MATCH: Tunisia role is in a historically preferred Greater Tunis location"]
        if location in {"tunisia", "tunisie"}:
            return 70, ["VERIFY: Tunisian city is not specified"]
        return 55, [f"VERIFY: Tunisia role is outside the historically preferred Greater Tunis area: {job.location}"]

    tier = _country_tier(country, profile)
    if tier == 1:
        return 100, [f"MATCH: Tier 1 international target market: {country}"]
    if tier == 2:
        return 90, [f"MATCH: Tier 2 international target market: {country}"]
    if tier == 3:
        return 75, [f"MATCH: Tier 3 selective international market: {country}"]
    if country:
        return 60, [f"VERIFY: International market outside configured priority tiers: {country}"]
    if not location:
        return 55, ["VERIFY: Location is unknown"]
    return 55, [f"VERIFY: Country could not be normalized from location: {job.location}"]


def _experience_score(job: JobRecord, requirements: dict, profile: dict) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
    seniority = _effective_seniority(job)
    min_years = requirements.get("experience", {}).get("min_years")
    seniority_cfg = profile.get("seniority", {})
    strong_max = int(seniority_cfg.get("max_required_years", 3))
    stretch_max = int(seniority_cfg.get("stretch_max_required_years", 5))

    if (job.opportunity_type or "job").lower() == "phd":
        return 100, ["MATCH: Professional years are not used as a doctoral seniority barrier"]

    if min_years is not None:
        if min_years <= strong_max:
            return 100, [f"MATCH: Experience requirement ({min_years} year(s)) is within early-career target"]
        if min_years <= stretch_max:
            score = 45 if min_years == strong_max + 1 else 25
            return score, [f"VERIFY: {min_years}+ years is a stretch application for the current profile"]
        return 0, [f"BLOCKER: Requires about {min_years}+ years; above configured stretch ceiling ({stretch_max})"]

    if seniority in {"graduate", "entry-level", "junior", "associate"}:
        return 100, [f"MATCH: Seniority '{seniority}' matches early-career target"]
    if seniority in {"mid-level"}:
        return 45, ["VERIFY: Mid-level title is a stretch"]
    if seniority in {"senior", "lead", "manager", "director", "principal", "staff"}:
        return 10, [f"BLOCKER: Seniority '{seniority}' is above target"]
    if seniority == "intern":
        return 20, ["BLOCKER: Internship seniority is excluded by current job-type policy"]
    return 75, ["VERIFY: No explicit experience requirement found"]


def _salary_score(job: JobRecord, profile: dict) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
    if (job.opportunity_type or "job").lower() == "phd":
        funding = norm_text(job.funding_status)
        if "self funded" in funding or "self-funded" in funding:
            return 0, ["BLOCKER: Doctoral opportunity appears self-funded"]
        if any(x in funding for x in ["salaried", "fully funded", "funded", "employment contract"]):
            return 100, ["MATCH: Doctoral opportunity is explicitly funded/salaried"]
        if job.salary_min is not None:
            return 95, ["MATCH: Doctoral compensation is explicitly stated"]
        if job.tuition_covered is True:
            return 85, ["MATCH: Doctoral tuition is explicitly covered"]
        return 45, ["VERIFY: PhD funding/compensation is not yet confirmed"]

    if job.salary_min is None:
        return 50, []
    # V0.5.0 intentionally avoids exchange-rate assumptions. Precise economic
    # normalization is reserved for V0.5.1; explicit salary data still earns
    # a stronger evidence score than an unknown salary.
    return 80, ["MATCH: Compensation is explicitly stated; V0.5.1 will normalize purchasing/economic value"]


def _eligibility_score(
    job: JobRecord,
    profile: dict,
    requirements: dict,
    education: int,
    visa: int,
    language: int,
    experience: int,
    salary: int,
) -> tuple[int, str, list[str]]:
    reasons: list[str] = []
    job_type = canonical_job_type(
        job.employment_type or detect_job_type(f"{job.title} {job.description}"),
        job.remote,
    )
    seniority = _effective_seniority(job)

    if (job.opportunity_type or "job").lower() == "job":
        configured_types = profile.get("job_types", {})
        type_key = {
            "full-time": "full_time", "part-time": "part_time", "internship": "internship",
            "apprenticeship": "apprenticeship", "civp": "civp", "freelance": "freelance",
            "fixed-term": "fixed_term", "contract": "remote_contract" if job.remote else "fixed_term",
        }.get(job_type)
        if seniority == "intern" and configured_types.get("internship") is False:
            reasons.append("BLOCKER: Internship seniority is excluded by profile")
        if type_key and configured_types.get(type_key) is False:
            reasons.append(f"BLOCKER: Job type '{job_type}' is excluded by profile")

    if education == 0:
        reasons.append("BLOCKER: Education requirement is not met")
    if language == 0:
        reasons.append("BLOCKER: Mandatory language requirement is not met")
    if visa <= 5:
        reasons.append("BLOCKER: Work authorization / geographic eligibility conflict")
    if experience == 0:
        reasons.append("BLOCKER: Experience requirement is above configured stretch ceiling")
    if seniority in {"senior", "lead", "manager", "director", "principal", "staff"}:
        reasons.append(f"BLOCKER: Seniority '{seniority}' is outside the early-career target")
    if salary == 0 and (job.opportunity_type or "job").lower() == "phd":
        reasons.append("BLOCKER: PhD appears not to be funded")

    role_class, _ = _title_role_relevance(job.title)
    if role_class == "non-target" and (job.opportunity_type or "job").lower() == "job":
        reasons.append("BLOCKER: Job title belongs to a non-target role family")

    if reasons:
        return 0, "blocked", reasons

    verification = []
    if visa < 80:
        verification.append("VERIFY: Visa/work-permit feasibility requires confirmation")
    if education < 80:
        verification.append("VERIFY: Education compatibility requires confirmation")
    if experience < 60:
        verification.append("VERIFY: Experience requirement is stretch-level")
    if (job.opportunity_type or "job").lower() == "phd" and salary < 70:
        verification.append("VERIFY: PhD funding must be confirmed before application")
    if job.is_open is False:
        return 0, "blocked", ["BLOCKER: Opportunity is marked closed"]

    if verification:
        return 60, "needs_verification", verification
    return 100, "eligible", ["MATCH: No hard eligibility conflict detected"]


def _priority(overall: int) -> str:
    if overall >= 80:
        return "A"
    if overall >= 65:
        return "B"
    if overall >= 50:
        return "C"
    if overall >= 35:
        return "stretch"
    return "discard"


def score_job(job: JobRecord, profile: dict, settings: dict) -> ScoreResult:
    job.opportunity_type = (job.opportunity_type or "job").lower().strip()
    if job.opportunity_type not in {"job", "phd"}:
        job.opportunity_type = "job"

    job.seniority = _effective_seniority(job)
    if job.opportunity_type == "job":
        job.employment_type = canonical_job_type(
            job.employment_type or detect_job_type(f"{job.title} {job.description}"),
            job.remote,
        )
    elif not job.employment_type:
        job.employment_type = "doctoral"

    if job.min_years is None and job.max_years is None and job.opportunity_type == "job":
        job.min_years, job.max_years = extract_years(f"{job.title} {job.description}")

    requirements, confidence = extract_requirements(job, profile)
    requirements["opportunity_type"] = job.opportunity_type
    requirements["country"] = _country_from_job(job)

    technical, e1 = _technical_score(job, profile)
    experience, e2 = _experience_score(job, requirements, profile)
    education, e3 = _education_score(job, profile, requirements)
    domain, e4 = _domain_score(job, profile)
    visa, e5 = _visa_score(job, profile)
    salary, e6 = _salary_score(job, profile)
    location, e7 = _location_score(job, profile)
    language, e8 = _language_score(job, profile, requirements)
    eligibility, eligibility_status, e9 = _eligibility_score(
        job, profile, requirements, education, visa, language, experience, salary
    )

    weights = settings.get("matching", {}).get("tarek_fit_weights") or settings.get("matching", {}).get("overall_weights", {})
    defaults = {
        "technical": 0.30,
        "experience": 0.20,
        "education": 0.10,
        "domain": 0.10,
        "visa": 0.10,
        "salary": 0.10,
        "location": 0.05,
        "language": 0.05,
    }
    values = {
        "technical": technical,
        "experience": experience,
        "education": education,
        "domain": domain,
        "visa": visa,
        "salary": salary,
        "location": location,
        "language": language,
    }
    overall = round(sum(values[key] * float(weights.get(key, defaults[key])) for key in defaults))
    overall = max(0, min(100, overall))
    priority = _priority(overall)

    explanations = e1 + e2 + e3 + e4 + e5 + e6 + e7 + e8 + e9
    verification_reasons = [x for x in explanations if x.startswith("VERIFY:")]

    if eligibility_status == "blocked":
        verdict = "blocked"
    elif eligibility_status == "eligible" and not verification_reasons and overall >= 50:
        verdict = "actionable"
    elif eligibility_status == "eligible" and overall >= 65:
        verdict = "actionable"
    else:
        verdict = "needs_verification"

    return ScoreResult(
        technical=technical,
        eligibility=eligibility,
        location=location,
        experience=experience,
        education=education,
        domain=domain,
        visa=visa,
        salary=salary,
        language=language,
        overall=overall,
        priority=priority,
        eligibility_status=eligibility_status,
        verdict=verdict,
        explanations=explanations,
        requirements=requirements,
        confidence=confidence,
    )


def is_stale(job: JobRecord, stale_after_days: int) -> bool:
    if not job.posted_at:
        return False
    dt = job.posted_at
    if dt.tzinfo is None:
        dt = dt.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    age = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - dt
    return age.days > stale_after_days
