from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime
from typing import Any

from pydantic import BaseModel, Field


class JobRecord(BaseModel):
    # V0.5 keeps the historical JobRecord name for collector compatibility,
    # while allowing the record to represent either a job or a funded PhD.
    opportunity_type: str = "job"

    title: str
    company: str = "Unknown"
    location: str = ""
    country: str = ""
    city: str = ""
    description: str = ""
    employment_type: str = ""
    work_mode: str = ""
    remote: bool = False
    remote_region: str = ""
    seniority: str = ""
    min_years: int | None = None
    max_years: int | None = None

    salary_min: float | None = None
    salary_max: float | None = None
    salary_currency: str | None = None
    salary_period: str | None = None

    posted_at: datetime | None = None
    deadline: datetime | None = None
    last_verified_at: datetime | None = None
    is_open: bool = True

    education_requirement: str = ""
    visa_status: str = "unknown"
    visa_sponsorship: bool | None = None
    international_candidates: bool | None = None
    relocation_support: bool | None = None

    funding_status: str = ""
    tuition_covered: bool | None = None
    housing_support: bool | None = None
    insurance_support: bool | None = None
    travel_support: bool | None = None
    benefits: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)

    source_name: str
    source_url: str
    external_id: str | None = None
    raw_payload: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)


class ScoreResult(BaseModel):
    # All component scores are percentages (0-100). Overall is the weighted
    # TarekFit score on a 0-100 scale.
    technical: int
    eligibility: int
    location: int
    experience: int
    education: int = 0
    domain: int = 0
    visa: int = 0
    salary: int = 0
    language: int = 0
    overall: int

    priority: str = "discard"
    eligibility_status: str = "needs_verification"
    verdict: str = "needs_verification"

    explanations: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
    requirements: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)
    confidence: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)

from __future__ import annotations

import html
import re
import unicodedata
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Iterable

from bs4 import BeautifulSoup
from dateutil import parser as date_parser

FRENCH_MONTHS = {
    "janvier": "January", "fÃ©vrier": "February", "fevrier": "February",
    "mars": "March", "avril": "April", "mai": "May", "juin": "June",
    "juillet": "July", "aoÃ»t": "August", "aout": "August",
    "septembre": "September", "octobre": "October",
    "novembre": "November", "dÃ©cembre": "December", "decembre": "December",
}

SALARY_CONTEXT = [
    "salary", "salaire", "pay", "compensation", "remuneration", "rÃ©munÃ©ration",
    "mensuel", "monthly", "annuel", "annual", "yearly", "hourly", "par mois",
    "par an", "per month", "per year", "per hour",
]


def clean_text(value: str | None) -> str:
    if not value:
        return ""
    value = str(value)
    stripped = value.strip()
    # Most values in the matcher are plain text tokens, titles, locations,
    # URLs, or already-clean page text. Avoid invoking BeautifulSoup unless
    # the string actually looks like markup.
    if "<" not in stripped or ">" not in stripped:
        return re.sub(r"\s+", " ", html.unescape(stripped)).strip()
    text = BeautifulSoup(stripped, "html.parser").get_text(" ", strip=True)
    return re.sub(r"\s+", " ", html.unescape(text)).strip()


def norm_text(value: str | None) -> str:
    value = clean_text(value).lower()
    value = unicodedata.normalize("NFKD", value)
    value = "".join(ch for ch in value if not unicodedata.combining(ch))
    value = re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9+#.]+", " ", value)
    return re.sub(r"\s+", " ", value).strip()


def parse_date(value) -> datetime | None:
    """Parse source timestamps without changing ISO month/day order.

    ISO timestamps are always year-month-day. The older implementation sent
    every string through dateutil with dayfirst=True, so 2026-08-11 could
    become 2026-11-08. French and slash-formatted local dates still use
    day-first parsing after ISO formats have been handled explicitly.
    """
    if value in (None, ""):
        return None

    if isinstance(value, datetime):
        return value if value.tzinfo else value.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)

    if isinstance(value, (int, float)):
        try:
            # Millisecond timestamps occasionally appear in APIs.
            timestamp = float(value)
            if timestamp > 10_000_000_000:
                timestamp /= 1000.0
            return datetime.fromtimestamp(timestamp, tz=timezone.utc)
        except (ValueError, OSError, OverflowError):
            return None

    text = clean_text(str(value)).strip()
    if not text:
        return None

    # ISO / RFC3339: never apply dayfirst=True to these.
    iso_candidate = text.replace("Z", "+00:00")
    if re.match(r"^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}(?:[T\s]|$)", iso_candidate):
        try:
            parsed = datetime.fromisoformat(iso_candidate)
            return parsed if parsed.tzinfo else parsed.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
        except ValueError:
            try:
                parsed = date_parser.isoparse(iso_candidate)
                return parsed if parsed.tzinfo else parsed.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
            except (ValueError, TypeError, OverflowError):
                pass

    lowered = text.lower()
    for fr, en in FRENCH_MONTHS.items():
        lowered = re.sub(rf"\b{re.escape(fr)}\b", en, lowered, flags=re.I)

    try:
        parsed = date_parser.parse(lowered, dayfirst=True, fuzzy=True)
        return parsed if parsed.tzinfo else parsed.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    except (ValueError, TypeError, OverflowError):
        return None


def extract_years(text: str) -> tuple[int | None, int | None]:
    # Preserve range separators before normalising punctuation away.
    raw = clean_text(text).lower()
    raw = unicodedata.normalize("NFKD", raw)
    raw = "".join(ch for ch in raw if not unicodedata.combining(ch))
    raw = raw.replace("\u2013", "-").replace("\u2014", "-")

    range_match = re.search(
        r"\b(?:entre\s+)?(\d+)\s*(?:-|to|a|et|and)\s*(\d+)\s*(?:years?|ans?)\b",
        raw,
    )
    if range_match:
        low, high = int(range_match.group(1)), int(range_match.group(2))
        if 0 <= low <= 20 and 0 <= high <= 20:
            return min(low, high), max(low, high)

    normalised = norm_text(raw)
    matches = [int(x) for x in re.findall(r"\b(\d+)\+?\s*(?:years?|ans?)\b", normalised)]
    sensible = [x for x in matches if 0 <= x <= 20]
    if sensible:
        value = min(sensible)
        return value, value
    return None, None


def detect_seniority(text: str) -> str:
    t = f" {norm_text(text)} "
    ordered = [
        ("director", [r"\bdirector\b", r"\bdirecteur\b", r"\bdirectrice\b", r"\bhead of\b", r"\bchief\b", r"\bvice president\b", r"\bvp\b"]),
        ("principal", [r"\bprincipal\b"]),
        ("staff", [r"\bstaff\b"]),
        ("senior", [r"\bsenior\b", r"\bsr\b", r"\bconfirme\b", r"\bconfirmee\b"]),
        ("lead", [r"\btech lead\b", r"\bteam lead\b", r"\blead engineer\b", r"\blead developer\b", r"\bchef d equipe\b", r"\bchef equipe\b", r"\bsuperviseur\b", r"\bsupervisor\b"]),
        ("manager", [r"\bmanager\b", r"\bresponsable\b"]),
        ("mid-level", [r"\bmid level\b", r"\bmid-level\b", r"\bintermediate\b"]),
        ("intern", [r"\bintern\b", r"\binternship\b", r"\bstage\b", r"\bstagiaire\b"]),
        ("graduate", [r"\bgraduate\b", r"\bfresh graduate\b", r"\bnew grad\b", r"\bjeune diplome\b"]),
        ("junior", [r"\bjunior\b", r"\bjr\b"]),
        ("entry-level", [r"\bentry level\b", r"\bentry-level\b", r"\bdebutant\b", r"\bdebutante\b"]),
        ("associate", [r"\bassociate\b"]),
    ]
    for label, patterns in ordered:
        if any(re.search(pattern, t, re.I) for pattern in patterns):
            return label
    return ""


def canonical_job_type(value: str | None, remote: bool = False) -> str:
    """Return one canonical employment-type spelling used by the matcher."""
    if not value:
        return ""

    token = norm_text(str(value)).replace("_", " ").replace("-", " ")
    token = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", token).strip()

    aliases = {
        "full time": "full-time",
        "fulltime": "full-time",
        "temps plein": "full-time",
        "plein temps": "full-time",
        "cdi": "full-time",
        "part time": "part-time",
        "parttime": "part-time",
        "temps partiel": "part-time",
        "intern": "internship",
        "internship": "internship",
        "stage": "internship",
        "stagiaire": "internship",
        "apprenticeship": "apprenticeship",
        "alternance": "apprenticeship",
        "civp": "civp",
        "sivp": "civp",
        "freelance": "freelance",
        "fixed term": "fixed-term",
        "cdd": "fixed-term",
        "contract": "contract",
        "contractor": "contract",
        "remote contract": "contract",
    }

    if token in aliases:
        return aliases[token]

    detected = detect_job_type(token)
    if detected:
        return detected

    return str(value).strip().lower()


def detect_job_type(text: str) -> str:
    t = norm_text(text)
    pairs = [
        ("internship", ["internship", "stage", "stagiaire", "pfe"]),
        ("apprenticeship", ["apprenticeship", "alternance", "apprenti"]),
        ("civp", ["civp", "sivp"]),
        ("part-time", ["part time", "part-time", "temps partiel"]),
        ("freelance", ["freelance", "independant", "independent contractor"]),
        ("fixed-term", ["cdd", "fixed term", "fixed-term"]),
        ("contract", ["contract", "contractor"]),
        ("full-time", ["full time", "full-time", "temps plein", "cdi", "plein temps"]),
    ]
    for label, terms in pairs:
        if any(term in t for term in terms):
            return label
    return ""


def parse_salary_text(text: str) -> tuple[float | None, float | None, str | None, str | None]:
    """Parse compensation without confusing dates, decimal commas or grouping.

    Supported examples include:
      900 - 1200 TND / Mois
      $31.2k - $52k per year
      $31,2k - $52k
      $1,200 - $1,800 per month
      1 500 - 2 000 TND
    """
    if not text:
        return None, None, None, None

    normalized = (
        clean_text(text)
        .replace("\u202f", " ")
        .replace("\u00a0", " ")
    )
    low = norm_text(normalized)

    currency = None
    if re.search(r"\b(?:TND|DT)\b", normalized, re.I):
        currency = "TND"
    elif "$" in normalized or re.search(r"\bUSD\b", normalized, re.I):
        currency = "USD"
    elif "â‚¬" in normalized or re.search(r"\bEUR\b", normalized, re.I):
        currency = "EUR"
    elif "Â£" in normalized or re.search(r"\bGBP\b", normalized, re.I):
        currency = "GBP"

    has_pay_context = currency is not None or any(
        term in low for term in SALARY_CONTEXT
    )
    if not has_pay_context:
        return None, None, None, None

    def numeric_value(raw: str, suffix: str) -> float | None:
        token = (
            raw.strip()
            .replace("\u202f", "")
            .replace("\u00a0", "")
            .replace(" ", "")
        )
        if not token:
            return None

        suffix = suffix.lower()

        # With k/m suffixes, punctuation is naturally a decimal separator:
        # 31,2k == 31.2k and 31.2k == 31.2k.
        if suffix:
            if "," in token and "." in token:
                # Whichever separator appears last is the decimal separator.
                if token.rfind(",") > token.rfind("."):
                    token = token.replace(".", "").replace(",", ".")
                else:
                    token = token.replace(",", "")
            elif "," in token:
                token = token.replace(",", ".")

        else:
            if "," in token and "." in token:
                # Last separator is decimal; earlier one is grouping.
                if token.rfind(",") > token.rfind("."):
                    token = token.replace(".", "").replace(",", ".")
                else:
                    token = token.replace(",", "")
            elif "," in token:
                head, tail = token.rsplit(",", 1)
                if len(tail) == 3 and head.replace(",", "").isdigit():
                    token = token.replace(",", "")
                else:
                    token = token.replace(",", ".")
            elif "." in token:
                head, tail = token.rsplit(".", 1)
                if len(tail) == 3 and head.replace(".", "").isdigit():
                    token = token.replace(".", "")

        try:
            value = float(token)
        except ValueError:
            return None

        if suffix == "k":
            value *= 1_000.0
        elif suffix == "m":
            value *= 1_000_000.0

        return value

    numbers: list[float] = []

    # Prefer grouped/decimal forms first so "1 500" is one number, not 1 + 500.
    number_pattern = (
        r"(?<!\d)"
        r"("
        r"\d{1,3}(?:[ \u00a0\u202f]\d{3})+"
        r"|"
        r"\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?"
        r")"
        r"\s*([kKmM]?)"
        r"(?![A-Za-z])"
    )

    for raw, suffix in re.findall(number_pattern, normalized):
        value = numeric_value(raw, suffix)
        if value is None:
            continue

        # Bare 4-digit years must never become compensation.
        if not suffix and 1900 <= value <= 2100:
            continue

        if 1 <= value <= 100_000_000:
            numbers.append(value)

    period = None
    if any(
        x in low
        for x in [
            "/year",
            "per year",
            "yearly",
            "annual",
            "annuel",
            "par an",
        ]
    ):
        period = "yearly"
    elif any(
        x in low
        for x in [
            "/month",
            "per month",
            "monthly",
            "mensuel",
            "par mois",
            "/ mois",
            "mois",
        ]
    ):
        period = "monthly"
    elif any(
        x in low
        for x in [
            "/hour",
            "per hour",
            "hourly",
            "heure",
        ]
    ):
        period = "hourly"

    if not numbers:
        return None, None, currency, period

    if len(numbers) == 1:
        return numbers[0], numbers[0], currency, period

    a, b = numbers[0], numbers[1]
    return min(a, b), max(a, b), currency, period


def first_match(text: str, candidates: Iterable[str]) -> str:
    t = norm_text(text)
    for candidate in candidates:
        if norm_text(candidate) in t:
            return candidate
    return ""

def _load_yaml(path: Path) -> dict:
    if not path.exists():
        return {}
    with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        return yaml.safe_load(f) or {}


def _save_yaml(path: Path, data: dict) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    tmp = path.with_suffix(path.suffix + ".tmp")
    tmp.write_text(yaml.safe_dump(data, allow_unicode=True, sort_keys=False), encoding="utf-8")
    tmp.replace(path)


def _load_json(path: Path, default):
    if not path.exists():
        return default
    try:
        return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except Exception:
        return default


def _save_json(path: Path, data) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    tmp = path.with_suffix(path.suffix + ".tmp")
    tmp.write_text(json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, default=str), encoding="utf-8")
    tmp.replace(path)


def _json_field(value: str | None, default):
    try:
        return json.loads(value or "")
    except Exception:
        return default


settings = _load_yaml(SETTINGS_PATH)
db_path = ROOT / settings.get("storage", {}).get("sqlite_path", "data/jobs.db")
engine = get_engine(str(db_path))
init_db(engine)

app = FastAPI(title="TTEA Dashboard API", version="0.5.0")
app.mount("/assets", StaticFiles(directory=UI_DIR), name="assets")

    review_status: str | None = None
    application_status: str | None = None
    notes: str | None = None
    cv_version: str | None = None
    cover_letter_version: str | None = None

    interview_stage: str | None = None

    ignore_poll_interval: bool = False
    sources: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)


class ProfileUpdate(BaseModel):
    profile: dict[str, Any]
    matching: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)
    collection: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)


def _iso(value) -> str | None:
    if value is None:
        return None
    if isinstance(value, str):
        return value
    try:
        return value.isoformat()
    except Exception:
        return str(value)


def _reason_groups(explanations: list[str]) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
    groups = {"blockers": [], "matches": [], "verification": [], "rules": []}
    for raw in explanations:
        if raw.startswith("BLOCKER:"):
            groups["blockers"].append(raw.split(":", 1)[1].strip())
        elif raw.startswith("MATCH:"):
            groups["matches"].append(raw.split(":", 1)[1].strip())
        elif raw.startswith("VERIFY:"):
            groups["verification"].append(raw.split(":", 1)[1].strip())
        elif raw.startswith("RULE:"):
            groups["rules"].append(raw.split(":", 1)[1].strip())
        else:
            groups["verification"].append(raw)
    return groups


def _job_payload(job: JobORM, include_description: bool = False) -> dict:
    explanations = _json_field(job.score_explanation_json, [])
    requirements = _json_field(job.requirements_json, {})
    required_languages = _json_field(job.required_languages_json, [])
    confidence = _json_field(job.extraction_confidence_json, {})
    reason_groups = _reason_groups(explanations)

    payload = {
        "id": job.id,
        "opportunity_type": job.opportunity_type or "job",
        "title": job.title,
        "company": job.company,
        "location": job.location,
        "country": job.country,
        "city": job.city,
        "work_mode": job.work_mode,
        "employment_type": job.employment_type,
        "remote": bool(job.remote),
        "remote_region": job.remote_region,
        "seniority": job.seniority,
        "min_years": job.min_years,
        "max_years": job.max_years,
        "salary_min": job.salary_min,
        "salary_max": job.salary_max,
        "salary_currency": job.salary_currency,
        "salary_period": job.salary_period,
        "posted_at": _iso(job.posted_at),
        "deadline": _iso(job.deadline),
        "last_verified_at": _iso(job.last_verified_at),
        "is_open": bool(job.is_open),
        "stale": bool(job.stale),
        "priority": job.priority or "discard",
        "eligibility_status": job.eligibility_status or "needs_verification",
        "verdict": job.verdict or "needs_verification",
        "review_status": job.review_status or "unreviewed",
        "scores": {
            "technical": job.technical_score,
            "eligibility": job.eligibility_score,
            "location": job.location_score,
            "experience": job.experience_score,
            "education": job.education_score,
            "domain": job.domain_score,
            "visa": job.visa_score,
            "salary": job.salary_score,
            "language": job.language_score,
            "overall": job.overall_score,
        },
        "score_explanations": explanations,
        "reason_groups": reason_groups,
        "requirements": requirements,
        "required_languages": required_languages,
        "extraction_confidence": confidence,
        "application_status": job.application_status or "not_started",
        "application_date": _iso(job.application_date),
        "cv_version": job.cv_version,
        "cover_letter_version": job.cover_letter_version,
        "response": job.response,
        "interview_stage": job.interview_stage,
        "notes": job.notes,
        "education_requirement": job.education_requirement,
        "visa_status": job.visa_status,
        "visa_sponsorship": job.visa_sponsorship,
        "international_candidates": job.international_candidates,
        "relocation_support": job.relocation_support,
        "funding": {
            "status": job.funding_status,
            "tuition_covered": job.tuition_covered,
            "housing_support": job.housing_support,
            "insurance_support": job.insurance_support,
            "travel_support": job.travel_support,
            "benefits": _json_field(job.benefits_json, {}),
        },
        "canonical_url": job.canonical_url,
        "first_seen_at": _iso(job.first_seen_at),
        "last_seen_at": _iso(job.last_seen_at),
        "updated_at": _iso(job.updated_at),
        "sources": [
            {
                "name": source.source_name,
                "url": source.source_url,
                "external_id": source.external_id,
                "first_seen_at": _iso(source.first_seen_at),
                "last_seen_at": _iso(source.last_seen_at),
            }
            for source in (job.sources or [])
        ],
    }
    if include_description:
        payload["description"] = job.description or ""
    return payload


def _status_counts(session: Session) -> dict[str, int]:
    rows = session.execute(
        select(JobORM.application_status, func.count(JobORM.id)).group_by(JobORM.application_status)
    ).all()
    return {status or "not_started": int(count) for status, count in rows}


def _review_counts(session: Session) -> dict[str, int]:
    rows = session.execute(select(JobORM.review_status, func.count(JobORM.id)).group_by(JobORM.review_status)).all()
    return {status or "unreviewed": int(count) for status, count in rows}


def _source_poll_info() -> list[dict]:
    cfg = _load_yaml(SOURCES_PATH).get("sources", {})
    state = _load_json(POLL_STATE_PATH, {})
    history = _load_json(RUN_HISTORY_PATH, [])
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

    with Session(engine) as session:
        counts = dict(session.execute(
            select(JobSourceORM.source_name, func.count(JobSourceORM.id)).group_by(JobSourceORM.source_name)
        ).all())
        live_counts = dict(session.execute(
            select(JobSourceORM.source_name, func.count(JobSourceORM.id))
            .join(JobORM, JobORM.id == JobSourceORM.job_id)
            .where(JobORM.stale.is_(False))
            .group_by(JobSourceORM.source_name)
        ).all())

    result = []
    for name, source_cfg in cfg.items():
        last_raw = state.get(name)
        last_dt = None
        if last_raw:
            try:
                last_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(last_raw)
                if last_dt.tzinfo is None:
                    last_dt = last_dt.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
            except ValueError:
                last_dt = None
        min_minutes = int(source_cfg.get("min_interval_minutes", 0))
        next_dt = last_dt + timedelta(minutes=min_minutes) if last_dt else None
        eligible = next_dt is None or next_dt <= now
        seconds_remaining = max(0, int((next_dt - now).total_seconds())) if next_dt and not eligible else 0

        last_success = None
        last_error = None
        for run in history:
            source_run = (run.get("sources") or {}).get(name, {})
            status = source_run.get("status")
            if status == "success" and last_success is None:
                last_success = run.get("finished_at") or run.get("started_at")
            if status == "failed" and last_error is None:
                last_error = source_run.get("error") or "Collector failed"

        result.append({
            "name": name,
            "enabled": bool(source_cfg.get("enabled", True)),
            "kind": source_cfg.get("kind", ""),
            "note": source_cfg.get("note", ""),
            "jobs_seen": int(counts.get(name, 0)),
            "live_jobs": int(live_counts.get(name, 0)),
            "last_polled_at": _iso(last_dt),
            "last_success_at": last_success or _iso(last_dt),
            "last_error": last_error,
            "next_eligible_at": _iso(next_dt),
            "eligible_now": eligible,
            "seconds_remaining": seconds_remaining,
            "min_interval_minutes": min_minutes,
            "max_jobs": int(source_cfg.get("max_jobs", 0)),
        })
    return result


def _source_registry_info() -> list[dict]:
    registry = _load_yaml(SOURCE_REGISTRY_PATH).get("sources", {})
    active = {item["name"]: item for item in _source_poll_info()}
    result = []
    for name, meta in registry.items():
        row = {
            "name": name,
            "market": meta.get("market", ""),
            "method": meta.get("method", ""),
            "state": meta.get("state", "planned"),
            "priority": meta.get("priority", "normal"),
            "note": meta.get("note", ""),
            "enabled": False,
            "jobs_seen": 0,
            "live_jobs": 0,
            "last_polled_at": None,
            "last_success_at": None,
            "last_error": None,
            "next_eligible_at": None,
            "eligible_now": False,
            "min_interval_minutes": None,
        }
        if name in active:
            row.update(active[name])
            row["market"] = meta.get("market", row.get("market", ""))
            row["method"] = meta.get("method", row.get("kind", ""))
            row["state"] = "active" if row.get("enabled") else "disabled"
            row["priority"] = meta.get("priority", "high")
            row["note"] = meta.get("note") or row.get("note", "")
        result.append(row)
    # Any configured collector missing from the registry must still appear.
    for name, row in active.items():
        if name not in registry:
            result.append({**row, "market": "", "method": row.get("kind", ""), "state": "active", "priority": "high"})
    order = {"active": 0, "in_development": 1, "planned": 2, "auth_required": 3, "browser_required": 4, "blocked": 5, "disabled": 6}
    return sorted(result, key=lambda x: (order.get(x.get("state"), 9), x.get("market", ""), x.get("name", "")))


def _stored_record(job: JobORM) -> JobRecord:
    source = job.sources[0] if job.sources else None
    return JobRecord(
        opportunity_type=job.opportunity_type or "job",
        title=job.title,
        company=job.company,
        location=job.location,
        country=job.country,
        city=job.city,
        description=job.description,
        employment_type=job.employment_type,
        work_mode=job.work_mode,
        remote=job.remote,
        remote_region=job.remote_region,
        seniority=job.seniority,
        min_years=job.min_years,
        max_years=job.max_years,
        salary_min=job.salary_min,
        salary_max=job.salary_max,
        salary_currency=job.salary_currency,
        salary_period=job.salary_period,
        posted_at=job.posted_at,
        deadline=job.deadline,
        last_verified_at=job.last_verified_at,
        is_open=job.is_open,
        education_requirement=job.education_requirement,
        visa_status=job.visa_status or "unknown",
        visa_sponsorship=job.visa_sponsorship,
        international_candidates=job.international_candidates,
        relocation_support=job.relocation_support,
        funding_status=job.funding_status,
        tuition_covered=job.tuition_covered,
        housing_support=job.housing_support,
        insurance_support=job.insurance_support,
        travel_support=job.travel_support,
        benefits=_json_field(job.benefits_json, {}),
        source_name=source.source_name if source else "stored",
        source_url=source.source_url if source else (job.canonical_url or f"stored:{job.id}"),
    )


def _rescore_all(profile_data: dict, settings_data: dict) -> int:
    changed = 0
    with Session(engine) as session:
        jobs = session.scalars(select(JobORM).options(selectinload(JobORM.sources))).all()
        for job in jobs:
            record = _stored_record(job)
            scores = score_job(record, profile_data, settings_data)
            job.seniority = record.seniority
            job.employment_type = record.employment_type
            job.min_years = record.min_years
            job.max_years = record.max_years
            job.technical_score = scores.technical
            job.eligibility_score = scores.eligibility
            job.location_score = scores.location
            job.experience_score = scores.experience
            job.education_score = scores.education
            job.domain_score = scores.domain
            job.visa_score = scores.visa
            job.salary_score = scores.salary
            job.language_score = scores.language
            job.overall_score = scores.overall
            job.priority = scores.priority
            job.eligibility_status = scores.eligibility_status
            job.verdict = scores.verdict
            job.score_explanation_json = json.dumps(scores.explanations, ensure_ascii=False)
            job.requirements_json = json.dumps(scores.requirements, ensure_ascii=False, default=str)
            job.required_languages_json = json.dumps(scores.requirements.get("languages", []), ensure_ascii=False, default=str)
            job.extraction_confidence_json = json.dumps(scores.confidence, ensure_ascii=False, default=str)
            job.updated_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
            changed += 1
        session.commit()
    return changed

@app.get("/api/overview")
def overview():
    history = _load_json(RUN_HISTORY_PATH, [])
    last_run = history[0] if history else None
    new_last_run = int((last_run or {}).get("summary", {}).get("new_jobs", 0) or 0)

    with Session(engine) as session:
        total = session.scalar(select(func.count(JobORM.id))) or 0
        job_total = session.scalar(select(func.count(JobORM.id)).where(JobORM.opportunity_type == "job")) or 0
        phd_total = session.scalar(select(func.count(JobORM.id)).where(JobORM.opportunity_type == "phd")) or 0
        live = session.scalar(select(func.count(JobORM.id)).where(JobORM.stale.is_(False))) or 0
        stale = session.scalar(select(func.count(JobORM.id)).where(JobORM.stale.is_(True))) or 0
        strong = session.scalar(select(func.count(JobORM.id)).where(JobORM.stale.is_(False), JobORM.overall_score >= 65)) or 0
        eligible_50 = session.scalar(select(func.count(JobORM.id)).where(JobORM.stale.is_(False), JobORM.overall_score >= 50, JobORM.eligibility_score >= 50)) or 0
        status_counts = _status_counts(session)
        review_counts = _review_counts(session)

    return {
        "metrics": {
            "total_jobs": int(total),
            "total_opportunities": int(total),
            "job_opportunities": int(job_total),
            "phd_opportunities": int(phd_total),
            "live_jobs": int(live),
            "stale_jobs": int(stale),
            "new_last_run": new_last_run,
            # Kept for backward API compatibility; V0.4 does not render these on Overview.
            "strong_matches": int(strong),
            "eligible_50_plus": int(eligible_50),
        },
        "status_counts": status_counts,
        "review_counts": review_counts,
        "sources": _source_poll_info(),
        "last_run": last_run,
    }


@app.get("/api/jobs")
def list_jobs(
    q: str = "",
    opportunity_type: Literal["all", "job", "phd"] = "all",
    min_score: int = Query(default=0, ge=0, le=100),
    status: str = "all",
    review: str = "all",
    verdict: str = "all",
    source: str = "all",
    remote: Literal["all", "yes", "no"] = "all",
    location_bucket: Literal["all", "local", "remote_accessible", "remote_restricted"] = "all",
    freshness: Literal["live", "stale", "all"] = "live",
    sort: Literal["score", "newest", "first_seen", "updated"] = "score",
    page: int = Query(default=1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(default=25, ge=1, le=100),
):
    filters = [JobORM.overall_score >= min_score]
    if opportunity_type != "all":
        filters.append(JobORM.opportunity_type == opportunity_type)
    if q.strip():
        needle = f"%{q.strip().lower()}%"
        filters.append(or_(
            func.lower(JobORM.title).like(needle),
            func.lower(JobORM.company).like(needle),
            func.lower(JobORM.location).like(needle),
            func.lower(JobORM.description).like(needle),
        ))
    if status != "all":
        filters.append(JobORM.application_status == status)
    if review != "all":
        filters.append(JobORM.review_status == review)
    if verdict == "reviewable":
        filters.append(JobORM.verdict != "blocked")
    elif verdict != "all":
        filters.append(JobORM.verdict == verdict)
    if source != "all":
        filters.append(JobORM.sources.any(JobSourceORM.source_name == source))
    if remote == "yes":
        filters.append(JobORM.remote.is_(True))
    elif remote == "no":
        filters.append(JobORM.remote.is_(False))
    if location_bucket == "local":
        filters.extend([JobORM.remote.is_(False), JobORM.location_score >= 60])
    elif location_bucket == "remote_accessible":
        filters.extend([JobORM.remote.is_(True), JobORM.location_score >= 60])
    elif location_bucket == "remote_restricted":
        filters.extend([JobORM.remote.is_(True), JobORM.location_score < 60])
    if freshness == "live":
        filters.append(JobORM.stale.is_(False))
    elif freshness == "stale":
        filters.append(JobORM.stale.is_(True))

    if sort == "newest":
        order = [JobORM.posted_at.desc(), JobORM.overall_score.desc()]
    elif sort == "first_seen":
        order = [JobORM.first_seen_at.desc()]
    elif sort == "updated":
        order = [JobORM.updated_at.desc()]
    else:
        # Verdict is a gate, score is the within-gate ranking.
        verdict_order = case(
            (JobORM.verdict == "actionable", 0),
            (JobORM.verdict == "needs_verification", 1),
            else_=2,
        )
        order = [verdict_order.asc(), JobORM.overall_score.desc(), JobORM.posted_at.desc()]

    with Session(engine) as session:
        total = session.scalar(select(func.count(JobORM.id)).where(*filters)) or 0
        jobs = session.scalars(
            select(JobORM)
            .options(selectinload(JobORM.sources))
            .where(*filters)
            .order_by(*order)
            .offset((page - 1) * page_size)
            .limit(page_size)
        ).all()
    return {
        "items": [_job_payload(j) for j in jobs],
        "page": page,
        "page_size": page_size,
        "total": int(total),
        "pages": max(1, (int(total) + page_size - 1) // page_size),
    }


@app.get("/api/jobs/{job_id}")
def get_job(job_id: int):
    with Session(engine) as session:
        job = session.scalar(select(JobORM).options(selectinload(JobORM.sources)).where(JobORM.id == job_id))
        if not job:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Job not found")
        return _job_payload(job, include_description=True)


@app.patch("/api/jobs/{job_id}")
def update_job(job_id: int, payload: JobUpdate):
    with Session(engine) as session:
        job = session.scalar(select(JobORM).options(selectinload(JobORM.sources)).where(JobORM.id == job_id))
        if not job:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Job not found")

        if payload.review_status is not None:
            if payload.review_status not in REVIEW_STATUSES:
                raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid review status")
            job.review_status = payload.review_status
            job.updated_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

        if payload.application_status is not None:
            if payload.application_status not in APPLICATION_STATUSES:
                raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid application status")
            previous = job.application_status
            job.application_status = payload.application_status
            if payload.application_status == "applied" and job.application_date is None:
                job.application_date = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
            if previous != payload.application_status:
                job.updated_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

        if payload.notes is not None:
            job.notes = payload.notes.strip() or None
            job.updated_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        if payload.cv_version is not None:
            job.cv_version = payload.cv_version.strip() or None
            job.updated_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        if payload.cover_letter_version is not None:
            job.cover_letter_version = payload.cover_letter_version.strip() or None
            job.updated_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        if payload.response is not None:
            job.response = payload.response.strip() or None
            job.updated_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        if payload.interview_stage is not None:
            job.interview_stage = payload.interview_stage.strip() or None
            job.updated_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

        session.commit()
        session.refresh(job)
        return _job_payload(job, include_description=True)


@app.get("/api/applications")
def applications():
    with Session(engine) as session:
        jobs = session.scalars(
            select(JobORM)
            .options(selectinload(JobORM.sources))
            .where(or_(JobORM.review_status == "shortlisted", JobORM.application_status != "not_started"))
            .order_by(JobORM.updated_at.desc())
        ).all()
        counts = _status_counts(session)
    return {
        "items": [_job_payload(j) for j in jobs],
        "status_counts": counts,
        "statuses": APPLICATION_STATUSES,
    }


@app.get("/api/sources")
def sources():
    return {"items": _source_registry_info()}


@app.get("/api/profile")
def profile():
    return {
        "profile": _load_json(PROFILE_PATH, {}),
        "settings": _load_yaml(SETTINGS_PATH),
        "review_statuses": REVIEW_STATUSES,
        "application_statuses": APPLICATION_STATUSES,
        "verdicts": VERDICTS,
    }


@app.patch("/api/profile")
def update_profile(payload: ProfileUpdate):
    global settings
    profile_data = payload.profile
    if not isinstance(profile_data.get("languages", {}), dict):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="profile.languages must be an object")

    new_settings = _load_yaml(SETTINGS_PATH)
    matching = new_settings.setdefault("matching", {})
    collection = new_settings.setdefault("collection", {})
    for key, value in payload.matching.items():
        matching[key] = value
    for key, value in payload.collection.items():
        collection[key] = value

    weights = matching.get("tarek_fit_weights", {}) or matching.get("overall_weights", {})
    if weights:
        keys = ["technical", "experience", "education", "domain", "visa", "salary", "location", "language"]
        total = sum(float(weights.get(k, 0)) for k in keys)
        if abs(total - 1.0) > 0.02:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="TarekFit weights must add up to 1.0")

    _save_json(PROFILE_PATH, profile_data)
    _save_yaml(SETTINGS_PATH, new_settings)
    settings = new_settings
    changed = _rescore_all(profile_data, new_settings)
    return {"ok": True, "rescored_jobs": changed, "profile": profile_data, "settings": new_settings}


def _read_run_state() -> dict:
    with RUN_LOCK:
        return _load_json(RUN_STATE_PATH, {})


def _write_run_state(state: dict) -> None:
    with RUN_LOCK:
        _save_json(RUN_STATE_PATH, state)


def _append_history(state: dict) -> None:
    with RUN_LOCK:
        history = _load_json(RUN_HISTORY_PATH, [])
        history.insert(0, {
            "id": state.get("id"),
            "status": state.get("status"),
            "started_at": state.get("started_at"),
            "finished_at": state.get("finished_at"),
            "limit": state.get("limit"),
            "summary": state.get("summary", {}),
            "sources": state.get("sources", {}),
        })
        _save_json(RUN_HISTORY_PATH, history[:100])


def _parse_run_line(state: dict, line: str) -> None:
    state["last_line"] = line
    logs = state.setdefault("logs", [])
    logs.append(line)
    if len(logs) > 300:
        del logs[:-300]

    start_match = re.search(r"\[([A-Z0-9_-]+)\]\s+collecting up to\s+(\d+)", line, re.I)
    if start_match:
        name = start_match.group(1).lower()
        source = state.setdefault("sources", {}).setdefault(name, {})
        source.update({"status": "running", "requested": int(start_match.group(2))})
        state["phase"] = f"Collecting {name}"

    fetched_match = re.search(r"\[([A-Z0-9_-]+)\]\s+(\d+)\s+jobs fetched", line, re.I)
    if fetched_match:
        name = fetched_match.group(1).lower()
        source = state.setdefault("sources", {}).setdefault(name, {})
        source.update({"status": "success", "fetched": int(fetched_match.group(2))})
        state["phase"] = f"Completed {name}"

    failed_match = re.search(r"\[([A-Z0-9_-]+)\]\s+failed:\s*(.*)", line, re.I)
    if failed_match:
        name = failed_match.group(1).lower()
        source = state.setdefault("sources", {}).setdefault(name, {})
        source.update({"status": "failed", "error": failed_match.group(2).strip()})

    skipped_match = re.search(r"\[([A-Z0-9_-]+)\]\s+Skipped:", line, re.I)
    if skipped_match:
        name = skipped_match.group(1).lower()
        source = state.setdefault("sources", {}).setdefault(name, {})
        source.update({"status": "skipped"})

    summary_patterns = {
        "raw_jobs": r"Raw jobs fetched:\s+(\d+)",
        "new_jobs": r"New canonical jobs:\s+(\d+)",
        "duplicates": r"Duplicates merged:\s+(\d+)",
        "seen_again": r"Already seen URLs:\s+(\d+)",
        "stale": r"Marked stale:\s+(\d+)",
        "collector_failures": r"Collector failures:\s+(\d+)",
        "live_exported": r"Live jobs exported:\s+(\d+)",
    }
    summary = state.setdefault("summary", {})
    for key, pattern in summary_patterns.items():
        m = re.search(pattern, line, re.I)
        if m:
            summary[key] = int(m.group(1))
    if "Dry-run summary" in line:
        state["phase"] = "Saving and summarizing"


def _run_collection_worker(run_id: str, limit: int, ignore_poll_interval: bool, selected_sources: list[str]) -> None:
    global RUN_PROCESS
    state = _read_run_state()
    _write_run_state(state)
    cmd = [sys.executable, "-m", "src.main", "dry-run", "--limit", str(limit)]
    if ignore_poll_interval:
        cmd.append("--ignore-poll-interval")
    if selected_sources:
        cmd.extend(["--sources", ",".join(selected_sources)])

    try:
        RUN_PROCESS = subprocess.Popen(
            cmd,
            cwd=str(ROOT),
            stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
            stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
            text=True,
            encoding="utf-8",
            errors="replace",
            bufsize=1,
        )
        if RUN_PROCESS.stdout is not None:
            for raw_line in RUN_PROCESS.stdout:
                line = raw_line.rstrip("\r\n")
                if not line:
                    continue
                state = _read_run_state()
                _parse_run_line(state, line)
                _write_run_state(state)
        return_code = RUN_PROCESS.wait()
        state = _read_run_state()
        state["return_code"] = return_code
        if state.get("stop_requested"):
            state["status"] = "stopped"
            state["phase"] = "Stopped by user"
        else:
            state["status"] = "success" if return_code == 0 else "failed"
            state["phase"] = "Completed" if return_code == 0 else "Failed"
        state["finished_at"] = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
        _write_run_state(state)
        _append_history(state)
    except Exception as exc:
        state = _read_run_state()
        state["status"] = "failed"
        state["phase"] = "Failed"
        state["finished_at"] = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
        state["error"] = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
        state.setdefault("logs", []).append(state["error"])
        _write_run_state(state)
        _append_history(state)
    finally:
        RUN_PROCESS = None


@app.get("/api/run/status")
def run_status():
    return _read_run_state()


@app.get("/api/run/history")
def run_history():
    return {"items": _load_json(RUN_HISTORY_PATH, [])}


@app.post("/api/run")
def start_run(payload: RunRequest):
    global RUN_THREAD
    current = _read_run_state()
    if current.get("status") == "running":
        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="A collection run is already in progress")

    active = _source_poll_info()
    selected_set = {x.lower() for x in payload.sources}
    enabled = [s for s in active if s["enabled"] and (not selected_set or s["name"].lower() in selected_set)]
    if not enabled:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="No enabled collectors match the requested source scope")
    if not payload.ignore_poll_interval and not any(s["eligible_now"] for s in enabled):
        next_times = [s["next_eligible_at"] for s in enabled if s["next_eligible_at"]]
        raise HTTPException(status_code=429, detail={
            "message": "All selected sources are still inside their minimum polling interval.",
            "next_eligible_at": min(next_times) if next_times else None,
        })

    selected_names = [s["name"] for s in enabled]
    run_id = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("ui_%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
    state = {
        "id": run_id,
        "status": "running",
        "phase": "Starting",
        "started_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        "finished_at": None,
        "limit": payload.limit,
        "ignore_poll_interval": payload.ignore_poll_interval,
        "selected_sources": selected_names,
        "summary": {},
        "sources": {name: {"status": "queued", "fetched": 0} for name in selected_names},
        "logs": [],
        "last_line": "",
        "stop_requested": False,
    }
    _write_run_state(state)
    RUN_THREAD = threading.Thread(
        target=_run_collection_worker,
        args=(run_id, payload.limit, payload.ignore_poll_interval, selected_names),
        daemon=True,
        name=f"ttea-{run_id}",
    )
    RUN_THREAD.start()
    return state


@app.post("/api/run/stop")
def stop_run():
    global RUN_PROCESS
    state = _read_run_state()
    if state.get("status") != "running":
        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="No collection run is active")
    state["stop_requested"] = True
    state["phase"] = "Stopping"
    state.setdefault("logs", []).append("Stop requested from dashboard")
    _write_run_state(state)
    if RUN_PROCESS is not None and RUN_PROCESS.poll() is None:
        RUN_PROCESS.terminate()
    return state

import json
from pathlib import Path

import yaml

from src.matcher import score_job
from src.models import JobRecord

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]


def load():
    profile = json.loads((ROOT / "config" / "profile.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    settings = yaml.safe_load((ROOT / "config" / "settings.yaml").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    return profile, settings


def test_good_local_junior_role_scores_well():
    profile, settings = load()
    job = JobRecord(
        title="Junior Embedded Software Engineer",
        company="Example",
        location="Ariana",
        description="Python C++ embedded systems testing. 1 year experience.",
        employment_type="full-time",
        remote=False,
        source_name="test",
        source_url="https://example.com/1",
    )
    result = score_job(job, profile, settings)
    assert result.technical >= 50
    assert result.location >= 85
    assert result.experience == 100
    assert result.overall >= 65
    assert result.eligibility_status == "eligible"


def test_internship_is_blocked_but_fit_is_not_artificially_capped():
    profile, settings = load()
    job = JobRecord(
        title="Software Engineering Intern",
        company="Example",
        location="Tunis",
        description="Python internship",
        employment_type="internship",
        source_name="test",
        source_url="https://example.com/2",
    )
    result = score_job(job, profile, settings)
    assert result.eligibility == 0
    assert result.eligibility_status == "blocked"
    assert result.verdict == "blocked"
    assert result.overall >= 0  # fit remains independent from the hard gate


def test_senior_title_is_blocked_by_eligibility_not_score_cap():
    profile, settings = load()
    job = JobRecord(
        title="Sr Customer Success Manager",
        company="Example",
        location="USA",
        description="Works with associate teams and software customers. 5 years experience.",
        remote=True,
        source_name="test",
        source_url="https://example.com/3",
    )
    result = score_job(job, profile, settings)
    assert job.seniority == "senior"
    assert result.experience <= 25
    assert result.visa <= 15
    assert result.verdict == "blocked"


def test_manager_title_is_blocked():
    profile, settings = load()
    job = JobRecord(
        title="Enterprise Customer Success Manager",
        company="Example",
        location="Anywhere",
        description="Technical SaaS software support with associate stakeholders.",
        remote=True,
        source_name="test",
        source_url="https://example.com/4",
    )
    result = score_job(job, profile, settings)
    assert job.seniority == "manager"
    assert result.verdict == "blocked"


def test_tier1_international_onsite_role_is_now_targeted():
    profile, settings = load()
    job = JobRecord(
        title="Junior Embedded Software Engineer",
        company="Example",
        location="Berlin, Germany",
        description="C++ embedded Linux ROS2. 1 year experience.",
        remote=False,
        source_name="test",
        source_url="https://example.com/5",
    )
    result = score_job(job, profile, settings)
    assert result.location == 100
    assert 40 <= result.visa < 100  # unknown sponsorship is a verification state
    assert result.eligibility_status == "needs_verification"
    assert result.overall >= 65

from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
HTML = (ROOT / 'ui' / 'index.html').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
JS = (ROOT / 'ui' / 'app.js').read_text(encoding='utf-8')


def section(section_id: str) -> str:
    start = HTML.index(f'id="view-{section_id}"')
    next_pos = HTML.find('<section class="view"', start + 1)
    if next_pos == -1:
        next_pos = HTML.find('</main>', start)
    return HTML[start:next_pos]

    assert 'COLLECTION LEDGER' in overview

    assert 'APPLICATION FUNNEL' not in overview
    assert 'SOURCE COVERAGE' not in overview

import json
from pathlib import Path

import yaml

from src.matcher import score_job
from src.models import JobRecord
from src.normalize import detect_seniority, extract_years, parse_salary_text

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
PROFILE = json.loads((ROOT / 'config' / 'profile.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
SETTINGS = yaml.safe_load((ROOT / 'config' / 'settings.yaml').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))


def make_job(title, description='', **kwargs):
    return JobRecord(
        title=title,
        description=description,
        company=kwargs.pop('company', 'Test'),
        location=kwargs.pop('location', 'Tunis'),
        source_name='test',
        source_url='https://example.com/' + str(abs(hash(title))),
        **kwargs,
    )


def test_year_is_not_salary_without_pay_context():
    assert parse_salary_text('Tunis 14 aoÃƒÂ»t 2026')[:2] == (None, None)


def test_year_is_excluded_from_real_salary_range():
    low, high, currency, period = parse_salary_text('CDI 1400 - 1800 TND Tunis 16 aoÃƒÂ»t 2026')
    assert (low, high, currency) == (1400.0, 1800.0, 'TND')


def test_keejob_monthly_salary_format():
    low, high, currency, period = parse_salary_text('Salaire proposÃƒÂ© 800 - 1200 TND / Mois')
    assert (low, high, currency, period) == (800.0, 1200.0, 'TND', 'monthly')

    job = make_job('SDA italien ou allemand et anglais', 'technicien support informatique')
    result = score_job(job, PROFILE, SETTINGS)
    assert not any('Technical terms: ai' in reason for reason in result.explanations)

    job = make_job('SDA italien ou allemand et anglais', 'technicien support informatique')
    result = score_job(job, PROFILE, SETTINGS)
    assert result.eligibility == 0
    assert result.verdict == 'blocked'
    assert any('Mandatory language not in profile' in reason for reason in result.explanations)

    result = score_job(job, PROFILE, SETTINGS)
    assert result.eligibility > 0


def test_french_supervisory_titles_are_detected():
    assert detect_seniority("chef d'equipe plasturgie") == 'lead'
    assert detect_seniority('Superviseur support IT') == 'lead'
    assert detect_seniority('Responsable infrastructure') == 'manager'

def test_senior_french_role_is_not_actionable():

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from pathlib import Path

import yaml
from bs4 import BeautifulSoup

from src.collectors.keejob import KeejobCollector
from src.matcher import score_job
from src.models import JobRecord
from src.normalize import clean_text, extract_years

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
PROFILE = json.loads((ROOT / "config" / "profile.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
SETTINGS = yaml.safe_load((ROOT / "config" / "settings.yaml").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


def test_clean_text_plain_tokens_are_safe():
    assert clean_text("Italian") == "Italian"
    assert clean_text("fran\u00e7ais") == "fran\u00e7ais"
    assert clean_text("https://example.com/a/b") == "https://example.com/a/b"


def test_year_ranges_still_work():
    assert extract_years("3-5 years experience") == (3, 5)
    assert extract_years("Entre 5 et 10 ans") == (5, 10)


def test_keejob_company_from_enterprise_section():
    html = """
    <html><body>
      <h1>Ing\u00e9nieur infrastructure</h1>
      <h2>Entreprise</h2>
      <h3>TUNISIE LEASING ET FACTORING</h3>
      <div>Secteur: banque / finance / assurances</div>
    </body></html>
    """
    soup = BeautifulSoup(html, "html.parser")
    assert (
        KeejobCollector._company_from_page(soup, "Ing\u00e9nieur infrastructure")
        == "TUNISIE LEASING ET FACTORING"
    )


def test_keejob_publication_uses_main_header_date():
    html = """
    <html><body>
      <h1>Technicien Maintenance Smartphone</h1>
      <div>FLASH PHONE Ariana Ville, Ariana Publi\u00e9e le 11 ao\u00fbt 2026</div>
      <h3>Date de publication</h3>
      <div>11 ao\u00fbt 2026</div>
      <div>Expiration 16 novembre 2026</div>
    </body></html>
    """
    soup = BeautifulSoup(html, "html.parser")
    lines = [x.strip() for x in soup.stripped_strings]
    raw, parsed = KeejobCollector._publication_from_page(soup, lines)
    assert parsed is not None
    assert (parsed.year, parsed.month, parsed.day) == (2026, 8, 11)


def test_future_publication_is_rejected():
    future = datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(days=30)
    text = future.strftime("%d %B %Y")
    soup = BeautifulSoup(
        f"<html><body><div>Published on {text}</div><h3>Date de publication</h3><div>{text}</div></body></html>",
        "html.parser",
    )
    lines = [x.strip() for x in soup.stripped_strings]
    _, parsed = KeejobCollector._publication_from_page(soup, lines)
    assert parsed is None


def test_normal_full_time_job_can_be_actionable_without_salary():
    job = JobRecord(
        title="Ing\u00e9nieur infrastructure",
        company="TUNISIE LEASING ET FACTORING",
        location="El Menzah, Tunis, Tunisie",
        description=(
            "Administration Linux reseaux sauvegarde documentation technique. "
            "Master Bac+5. Experience professionnelle d'au moins deux ans."
        ),
        employment_type="full-time",
        remote=False,
        min_years=2,
        max_years=5,
        source_name="test",
        source_url="https://example.com/job",
    )
    result = score_job(job, PROFILE, SETTINGS)
    assert result.verdict == "actionable"


def test_part_time_salary_is_not_a_matching_filter():
    job = JobRecord(
        title="Junior Developer Part Time",
        company="Test",
        location="Tunis, Tunisie",
        description="Python developer entry level.",
        employment_type="part-time",
        remote=False,
        source_name="test",
        source_url="https://example.com/job",
    )
    result = score_job(job, PROFILE, SETTINGS)
    assert result.verdict == "actionable"
    assert not any("salary" in x.lower() for x in result.explanations)

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path

import yaml

from src.matcher import score_job
from src.models import JobRecord
from src.normalize import (
    canonical_job_type,
    parse_date,
    parse_salary_text,
)

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
PROFILE = json.loads(
    (ROOT / "config" / "profile.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
)
SETTINGS = yaml.safe_load(
    (ROOT / "config" / "settings.yaml").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
)


def test_iso_date_does_not_swap_month_and_day():
    dt = parse_date("2026-08-11T20:18:02")
    assert (dt.year, dt.month, dt.day) == (2026, 8, 11)


def test_iso_date_only_does_not_swap_month_and_day():
    dt = parse_date("2026-08-11")
    assert (dt.year, dt.month, dt.day) == (2026, 8, 11)


def test_local_slash_date_remains_day_first():
    dt = parse_date("11/08/2026")
    assert (dt.year, dt.month, dt.day) == (2026, 8, 11)


def test_unix_milliseconds_are_supported():
    seconds = 1786479482
    dt_s = parse_date(seconds)
    dt_ms = parse_date(seconds * 1000)
    assert dt_s == dt_ms


def test_canonical_full_time_aliases():
    assert canonical_job_type("full_time") == "full-time"
    assert canonical_job_type("full-time") == "full-time"
    assert canonical_job_type("FULL TIME") == "full-time"


def test_remote_office_assistant_is_not_an_internship():
    job = JobRecord(
        title="Remote Office Assistant",
        company="Coalition Technologies",
        location="Worldwide",
        description=(
            "Full-time remote office assistant. Entry-level bookkeeping, "
            "billing, reporting and data entry. International team members."
        ),
        employment_type="full_time",
        remote=True,
        remote_region="Worldwide",
        seniority="intern",
        source_name="test",
        source_url="https://example.com/office-assistant",
    )
    result = score_job(job, PROFILE, SETTINGS)
    assert job.seniority != "intern"
    assert job.employment_type == "full-time"
    assert not any("Job type 'full_time'" in x for x in result.explanations)
    assert result.verdict == "blocked"


def test_k_salary_format():
    low, high, currency, period = parse_salary_text(
        "Salary $31.2k - $52k per year"
    )
    assert low == 31200
    assert high == 52000
    assert currency == "USD"
    assert period == "yearly"


def test_comma_decimal_k_salary_format():
    low, high, currency, _ = parse_salary_text(
        "Salary $31,2k - $52k"
    )
    assert low == 31200
    assert high == 52000
    assert currency == "USD"

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path

import yaml

from src.matcher import score_job
from src.models import JobRecord

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
PROFILE = json.loads(
    (ROOT / "config" / "profile.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
)
SETTINGS = yaml.safe_load(
    (ROOT / "config" / "settings.yaml").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
)


def make_part_time(salary=None):
    return JobRecord(
        title="Junior Python Developer",
        company="Test Company",
        location="Tunis, Tunisie",
        description="Python developer. Entry level. REST API. Git.",
        employment_type="part-time",
        remote=False,
        salary_min=salary,
        salary_max=salary,
        salary_currency="TND" if salary is not None else None,
        salary_period="monthly" if salary is not None else None,
        source_name="test",
        source_url="https://example.com/job",
    )


def test_unknown_salary_does_not_create_verification_rule():
    result = score_job(make_part_time(None), PROFILE, SETTINGS)
    assert not any("salary" in reason.lower() for reason in result.explanations)


def test_salary_is_a_soft_economic_signal_not_an_eligibility_block():
    no_salary = score_job(make_part_time(None), PROFILE, SETTINGS)
    low_salary = score_job(make_part_time(500), PROFILE, SETTINGS)

    assert low_salary.eligibility == no_salary.eligibility
    assert low_salary.salary > no_salary.salary
    assert low_salary.verdict == no_salary.verdict


def test_profile_salary_preference_is_not_needed_for_matching():
    profile = json.loads(json.dumps(PROFILE))
    profile.pop("salary_preferences", None)
    result = score_job(make_part_time(None), profile, SETTINGS)
    assert result.eligibility > 0

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path

import yaml
from sqlalchemy import inspect

from src.database import get_engine, init_db
from src.matcher import score_job
from src.models import JobRecord

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
PROFILE = json.loads((ROOT / "config" / "profile.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
SETTINGS = yaml.safe_load((ROOT / "config" / "settings.yaml").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


def test_weights_are_exactly_tarekfit_v2():
    weights = SETTINGS["matching"]["tarek_fit_weights"]
    assert weights == {
        "technical": 0.30,
        "experience": 0.20,
        "education": 0.10,
        "domain": 0.10,
        "visa": 0.10,
        "salary": 0.10,
        "location": 0.05,
        "language": 0.05,
    }
    assert abs(sum(weights.values()) - 1.0) < 1e-9


def test_schema_migration_is_idempotent(tmp_path):
    engine = get_engine(str(tmp_path / "jobs.db"))
    init_db(engine)
    init_db(engine)
    cols = {x["name"] for x in inspect(engine).get_columns("jobs")}
    for name in [
        "opportunity_type", "country", "deadline", "visa_status", "funding_status",
        "education_score", "domain_score", "visa_score", "salary_score", "language_score",
        "priority", "eligibility_status", "cover_letter_version", "interview_stage",
    ]:
        assert name in cols


def test_funded_robotics_phd_scores_as_strong_opportunity():
    job = JobRecord(
        opportunity_type="phd",
        title="PhD Researcher in Assistive Robotics and Shared Autonomy",
        company="Example University",
        location="Eindhoven, Netherlands",
        description=(
            "Fully funded doctoral researcher position in ROS2, C++, robot control, "
            "human-robot interaction, sensors and medical rehabilitation robotics. "
            "International candidates are welcome and visa sponsorship is provided."
        ),
        employment_type="doctoral",
        funding_status="fully funded salaried employment",
        tuition_covered=True,
        visa_sponsorship=True,
        international_candidates=True,
        salary_min=3200,
        salary_currency="EUR",
        salary_period="monthly",
        source_name="test",
        source_url="https://example.edu/phd/1",
    )
    result = score_job(job, PROFILE, SETTINGS)
    assert result.education == 100
    assert result.visa == 100
    assert result.salary >= 95
    assert result.domain >= 85
    assert result.overall >= 75
    assert result.eligibility_status == "eligible"
    assert result.priority in {"A", "B"}


def test_self_funded_phd_is_blocked_but_fit_remains_visible():
    job = JobRecord(
        opportunity_type="phd",
        title="PhD in Medical Robotics",
        company="Example University",
        location="Munich, Germany",
        description="Research in ROS2 robotic manipulation and medical devices.",
        funding_status="self-funded",
        source_name="test",
        source_url="https://example.edu/phd/2",
    )
    result = score_job(job, PROFILE, SETTINGS)
    assert result.technical > 0
    assert result.domain > 0
    assert result.salary == 0
    assert result.eligibility_status == "blocked"
    assert result.verdict == "blocked"


def test_priority_and_eligibility_are_independent():
    job = JobRecord(
        title="Junior Robotics Software Engineer",
        company="Example",
        location="Zurich, Switzerland",
        description="ROS2 C++ Python SLAM robot control. Must have the right to work in Switzerland without sponsorship.",
        min_years=1,
        source_name="test",
        source_url="https://example.com/blocked-high-fit",
    )
    result = score_job(job, PROFILE, SETTINGS)
    assert result.overall >= 65
    assert result.priority in {"A", "B"}
    assert result.eligibility_status == "blocked"
    assert result.verdict == "blocked"
