const esc = (v='') => String(v).replaceAll('&','&amp;').replaceAll('<','&lt;').replaceAll('>','&gt;').replaceAll('"','&quot;').replaceAll("'",'&#039;');
const pretty = (v='') => String(v).replaceAll('_',' ').replace(/\b\w/g,c=>c.toUpperCase());
function fmtDate(v){ if(!v)return 'UNKNOWN'; const d=new Date(v); if(Number.isNaN(d.getTime()))return 'UNKNOWN'; return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(undefined,{year:'numeric',month:'short',day:'2-digit'}).format(d).toUpperCase(); }
function fmtDateTime(v){ if(!v)return 'NEVER'; const d=new Date(v); if(Number.isNaN(d.getTime()))return 'NEVER'; return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(undefined,{year:'numeric',month:'short',day:'2-digit',hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'}).format(d).toUpperCase(); }
function badgeClass(v){ if(['actionable','success','active','ready','offer','shortlisted','applied'].includes(v))return 'good'; if(['needs_verification','in_development','running','application_preparing','interview','technical_test','hr_interview'].includes(v))return 'info'; if(['blocked','failed','rejected','dismissed','withdrawn','expired'].includes(v))return 'bad'; if(['planned','cooldown','browser_required','auth_required','skipped','stopped'].includes(v))return 'warn'; return 'neutral'; }
function scoreClass(v){ return Number(v)>=65?'score-high':Number(v)>=45?'score-mid':'score-low'; }
/* V0.4.5.1 RUNTIME NULL-SAFETY */
function byId(id){return document.getElementById(id);}
function fieldValue(id,fallback=''){const e=byId(id);return e&&('value' in e)?e.value:fallback;}


async function api(path,options={}){ const r=await fetch(path,{headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json',...(options.headers||{})},...options}); if(!r.ok){let d=`HTTP ${r.status}`;try{const b=await r.json();d=typeof b.detail==='string'?b.detail:(b.detail?.message||JSON.stringify(b.detail||b));}catch{} throw new Error(d);} return r.json(); }
function toast(msg,error=false){const e=document.getElementById('toast');e.textContent=`${error?'ERROR':'NOTICE'} :: ${msg}`;e.classList.remove('hidden');clearTimeout(toast.timer);toast.timer=setTimeout(()=>e.classList.add('hidden'),3500);}
function handleError(e){toast(e?.message||String(e),true);}
function setView(v){state.view=v;document.querySelectorAll('.view').forEach(x=>x.classList.toggle('active',x.id===`view-${v}`));document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item').forEach(x=>x.classList.toggle('active',x.dataset.view===v));const m=viewMeta[v];document.getElementById('viewNumber').textContent=m[0];document.getElementById('viewTitle').textContent=m[1];document.getElementById('viewSubtitle').textContent=m[2];refreshCurrent().catch(handleError);}
function snapshot(i,label,value,note){return `<div class="snapshot-cell"><div class="snapshot-index">${i}</div><div class="snapshot-value">${esc(value)}</div><div class="snapshot-label">${esc(label)}</div><div class="snapshot-note">${esc(note)}</div></div>`;}
function header(cols){return `<div class="register-row header">${cols.map(c=>`<div class="register-cell">${esc(c)}</div>`).join('')}</div>`;}

async function loadOverview(){const [o,h]=await Promise.all([api('/api/overview'),api('/api/run/history')]);const m=o.metrics||{};document.getElementById('overviewSnapshot').innerHTML=[snapshot('01','TOTAL CANONICAL RECORDS',m.total_jobs??0,'ALL JOBS INCLUDING ARCHIVE'),snapshot('02','LIVE MARKET RECORDS',m.live_jobs??0,'CURRENT NON-STALE LISTINGS'),snapshot('03','STALE ARCHIVE',m.stale_jobs??0,'PRESERVED HISTORICAL LISTINGS'),snapshot('04','NEW LAST COLLECTION',m.new_last_run??0,'NEW CANONICAL RECORDS FROM LATEST RUN')].join('');renderRunLedger(h.items||[]);}
function renderRunLedger(items){const e=document.getElementById('overviewRunLedger');if(!items.length){e.innerHTML='<div class="empty">NO COLLECTION HISTORY RECORDED.</div>';return;}e.innerHTML=header(['STARTED','RESULT','RAW','NEW','MERGED','STALE','FAIL','LIMIT'])+items.map(r=>{const s=r.summary||{};return `<div class="register-row data"><div class="register-cell">${esc(fmtDateTime(r.started_at))}</div><div class="register-cell"><span class="status-stamp ${badgeClass(r.status)}">${esc(r.status||'unknown')}</span></div><div class="register-cell">${s.raw_jobs??'--'}</div><div class="register-cell">${s.new_jobs??'--'}</div><div class="register-cell">${s.duplicates??'--'}</div><div class="register-cell">${s.stale??'--'}</div><div class="register-cell">${s.collector_failures??'--'}</div><div class="register-cell">${r.limit??'--'}</div></div>`;}).join('');}

function applyJobMode(mode){state.jobsMode=mode;document.querySelectorAll('.mode-button').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',b.dataset.jobMode===mode));if(mode==='review'){fieldValue('jobsReview','unreviewed')='unreviewed';fieldValue('jobsVerdict','reviewable')='reviewable';}else{fieldValue('jobsReview','unreviewed')='all';fieldValue('jobsVerdict','reviewable')='all';}state.jobsPage=1;loadJobs().catch(handleError);}
function populateSourceFilter(){
  const active=state.sources.filter(s=>s.state==='active');
  const jobsSource=byId('jobsSource');
  if(jobsSource){
    const current=jobsSource.value;
    jobsSource.innerHTML='<option value="all">ALL ACTIVE SOURCES</option>'+
      active.map(s=>`<option value="${esc(s.name)}">${esc(s.name.toUpperCase())}</option>`).join('');
    if([...jobsSource.options].some(o=>o.value===current))jobsSource.value=current;
  }
  const runScope=byId('runScope');
  if(runScope){
    const currentScope=runScope.value;
    runScope.innerHTML='<option value="all">ALL READY SOURCES</option>'+
      active.filter(s=>s.enabled).map(s=>`<option value="${esc(s.name)}">${esc(s.name.toUpperCase())}</option>`).join('');
    if([...runScope.options].some(o=>o.value===currentScope))runScope.value=currentScope;
  }
}
async function fetchSources(){const d=await api('/api/sources');state.sources=d.items||[];populateSourceFilter();return state.sources;}
function jobsFreshness(){return state.jobsMode==='archive'?'stale':'live';}
async function loadJobs(){const p=new URLSearchParams({q:fieldValue('jobsSearch',''),min_score:fieldValue('jobsMinScore','0'),review:fieldValue('jobsReview','unreviewed'),verdict:fieldValue('jobsVerdict','reviewable'),source:fieldValue('jobsSource','all'),location_bucket:fieldValue('jobsLocation','all'),freshness:jobsFreshness(),sort:fieldValue('jobsSort','verdict'),page:state.jobsPage,page_size:state.jobsPageSize});const d=await api(`/api/jobs?${p}`);state.jobsPages=d.pages;state.jobsPage=d.page;document.getElementById('jobsCount').textContent=`${d.total} MATCHING RECORD${d.total===1?'':'S'}`;document.getElementById('jobsPage').textContent=`PAGE ${d.page} / ${d.pages}`;document.getElementById('jobsPrev').disabled=d.page<=1;document.getElementById('jobsNext').disabled=d.page>=d.pages;const e=document.getElementById('jobsTable');if(!d.items.length){e.innerHTML='<div class="empty">NO JOBS MATCH THE CURRENT PARAMETERS.</div>';return;}e.innerHTML=header(['VERDICT','SCORE','VACANCY','COMPANY','LOCATION','POSTED','DECISION'])+d.items.map(j=>{const src=(j.sources||[])[0]?.name||'UNKNOWN';const sub=`${(j.employment_type||'UNSPECIFIED').toUpperCase()} / ${src.toUpperCase()}${j.stale?' / ARCHIVED':''}`;return `<div class="register-row data job-data-row" data-job-id="${j.id}"><div class="register-cell"><span class="status-stamp ${badgeClass(j.verdict)}">${esc(pretty(j.verdict).toUpperCase())}</span></div><div class="register-cell"><span class="score-stamp ${scoreClass(j.scores.overall)}">${j.scores.overall}</span></div><div class="register-cell"><div><div class="job-title">${esc(j.title)}</div><div class="job-subline">${esc(sub)}</div></div></div><div class="register-cell company-cell">${esc(j.company)}</div><div class="register-cell location-cell">${esc(j.location||'UNKNOWN')}</div><div class="register-cell date-cell">${esc(fmtDate(j.posted_at))}</div><div class="register-cell quick-actions">${j.review_status==='shortlisted'?'<span class="status-stamp good">SHORTLISTED</span>':j.review_status==='dismissed'?'<span class="status-stamp bad">DISMISSED</span>':`<button class="mini-action" data-action="shortlisted" data-id="${j.id}">SHORTLIST</button><button class="mini-action" data-action="dismissed" data-id="${j.id}">DISMISS</button>`}</div></div>`;}).join('');wireJobRows(e);}
function wireJobRows(root){root.querySelectorAll('.job-data-row').forEach(r=>r.addEventListener('click',()=>openJob(Number(r.dataset.jobId)).catch(handleError)));root.querySelectorAll('.mini-action').forEach(b=>b.addEventListener('click',ev=>{ev.stopPropagation();quickReview(Number(b.dataset.id),b.dataset.action).catch(handleError);}));}
async function quickReview(id,status){await api(`/api/jobs/${id}`,{method:'PATCH',body:JSON.stringify({review_status:status})});toast(status==='shortlisted'?'JOB SHORTLISTED':'JOB DISMISSED');await loadJobs();}
function scoreBar(label,v){return `<div class="score-bar-row"><span>${esc(label)}</span><div class="progress-track"><div class="progress-fill" style="width:${Number(v)||0}%"></div></div><strong>${Number(v)||0}</strong></div>`;}
function requirementRow(label,value,confidence=''){return `<div class="requirement-row"><div>${esc(label)}</div><strong>${esc(value||'NOT DETECTED')}</strong>${confidence?`<span class="confidence-stamp ${esc(confidence)}">${esc(confidence.toUpperCase())}</span>`:''}</div>`;}
async function openJob(id){state.currentJobId=id;const j=await api(`/api/jobs/${id}`);document.getElementById('jobModalTitle').textContent=j.title;document.getElementById('jobModalCompany').textContent=`${j.company} / ${j.location||'UNKNOWN LOCATION'}`;const rg=j.reason_groups||{};const req=j.requirements||{};const conf=j.extraction_confidence||{};const langs=(req.languages||[]).filter(x=>x.mandatory).map(x=>x.language).join(', ')||'NONE DETECTED';const exp=req.experience?.min_years!=null?(req.experience.min_years===req.experience.max_years?`${req.experience.min_years} YEAR(S)`: `${req.experience.min_years}-${req.experience.max_years} YEARS`):'NOT DETECTED';const edu=(req.education_signals||[]).join(', ')||'NOT DETECTED';const tech=(req.technical_terms||[]).join(', ')||'NOT DETECTED';const appOpts=state.applicationStatuses.map(s=>`<option value="${s}" ${s===j.application_status?'selected':''}>${esc(pretty(s).toUpperCase())}</option>`).join('');const sources=(j.sources||[]).map(s=>`<a class="badge info open-link" href="${esc(s.url)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">${esc(s.name.toUpperCase())} [OPEN]</a>`).join(' ');document.getElementById('jobModalBody').innerHTML=`<div class="decision-banner ${badgeClass(j.verdict)}"><div><div class="section-label">DECISION SUPPORT</div><div class="decision-verdict">${esc(pretty(j.verdict).toUpperCase())}</div></div><div class="decision-score">${j.scores.overall}/100</div></div>${(rg.blockers||[]).length?`<h3>BLOCKERS</h3><div class="decision-list blockers">${rg.blockers.map(x=>`<div>! ${esc(x)}</div>`).join('')}</div>`:''}${(rg.verification||[]).length?`<h3>NEEDS VERIFICATION</h3><div class="decision-list verify">${rg.verification.map(x=>`<div>? ${esc(x)}</div>`).join('')}</div>`:''}${(rg.matches||[]).length?`<h3>POSITIVE MATCHES</h3><div class="decision-list matches">${rg.matches.map(x=>`<div>+ ${esc(x)}</div>`).join('')}</div>`:''}<h3>EXTRACTED REQUIREMENTS / FACTS</h3><div class="requirements-ledger">${requirementRow('MANDATORY LANGUAGES',langs,conf.languages)}${requirementRow('EXPERIENCE',exp,conf.experience)}${requirementRow('EDUCATION SIGNALS',edu)}${requirementRow('TECHNICAL TERMS',tech)}${requirementRow('POSTING DATE',fmtDate(j.posted_at),conf.posted_at)}${requirementRow('WORK MODE',j.remote?'REMOTE':'ON-SITE')}${requirementRow('SENIORITY',(j.seniority||'UNSPECIFIED').toUpperCase())}</div><h3>SCORE BREAKDOWN</h3><div class="job-detail-top"><div class="big-score ${scoreClass(j.scores.overall)}">${j.scores.overall}</div><div class="score-bars">${scoreBar('TECHNICAL',j.scores.technical)}${scoreBar('ELIGIBILITY',j.scores.eligibility)}${scoreBar('LOCATION',j.scores.location)}${scoreBar('EXPERIENCE',j.scores.experience)}</div></div><h3>SOURCE / EVIDENCE</h3><div class="tag-list">${sources||'NO SOURCE METADATA'}</div><h3>CAPTURED DESCRIPTION</h3><div class="description">${esc(j.description||'NO DESCRIPTION CAPTURED.')}</div><h3>TRACKING RECORD</h3><div class="editor-grid"><label><span>REVIEW DECISION</span><select id="editReviewStatus">${state.reviewStatuses.map(s=>`<option value="${s}" ${s===j.review_status?'selected':''}>${esc(pretty(s).toUpperCase())}</option>`).join('')}</select></label><label><span>APPLICATION STATUS</span><select id="editJobStatus">${appOpts}</select></label><label><span>CV VERSION</span><input id="editCvVersion" value="${esc(j.cv_version||'')}" placeholder="EX: SOFTWARE-V3" /></label><label class="full"><span>NOTES / NEXT ACTION</span><textarea id="editJobNotes">${esc(j.notes||'')}</textarea></label></div><div class="modal-actions"><a href="${esc(j.canonical_url)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener" class="button open-link">OPEN LISTING</a><button class="button" id="dismissJobButton">DISMISS</button><button class="button" id="shortlistJobButton">SHORTLIST</button><button class="button primary" id="saveJobButton">SAVE RECORD</button></div>`;document.getElementById('jobModal').classList.remove('hidden');document.getElementById('saveJobButton').onclick=()=>saveJob().catch(handleError);document.getElementById('shortlistJobButton').onclick=()=>modalReview('shortlisted').catch(handleError);document.getElementById('dismissJobButton').onclick=()=>modalReview('dismissed').catch(handleError);}
async function modalReview(s){document.getElementById('editReviewStatus').value=s;await saveJob();}
async function saveJob(){if(!state.currentJobId)return;await api(`/api/jobs/${state.currentJobId}`,{method:'PATCH',body:JSON.stringify({review_status:document.getElementById('editReviewStatus').value,application_status:document.getElementById('editJobStatus').value,cv_version:document.getElementById('editCvVersion').value,notes:document.getElementById('editJobNotes').value})});toast('JOB RECORD UPDATED');document.getElementById('jobModal').classList.add('hidden');await refreshCurrent();}

async function loadApplications(){const d=await api('/api/applications');const e=document.getElementById('applicationBoard');if(!d.items.length){e.innerHTML='<div class="application-empty"><strong>NO ACTIVE APPLICATIONS</strong><p>Shortlist an opportunity from JOBS to begin tracking it.</p><button class="button primary" id="openReviewQueue">OPEN REVIEW QUEUE</button></div>';document.getElementById('openReviewQueue').onclick=()=>{applyJobMode('review');setView('jobs');};return;}const groups=[['SHORTLISTED',j=>j.review_status==='shortlisted'&&j.application_status==='not_started'],['PREPARING',j=>['application_preparing','ready_to_apply'].includes(j.application_status)],['APPLIED',j=>j.application_status==='applied'],['INTERVIEW',j=>['interview','technical_test','hr_interview'].includes(j.application_status)],['OUTCOME',j=>['offer','rejected','withdrawn','expired'].includes(j.application_status)]];e.innerHTML=groups.map(([label,test])=>{const jobs=d.items.filter(test);return `<div class="kanban-column"><div class="kanban-head"><span class="kanban-title">${label}</span><span class="badge neutral">${jobs.length}</span></div>${jobs.length?jobs.map(j=>`<button class="kanban-card" data-app-job="${j.id}"><div class="kanban-card-title">${esc(j.title)}</div><div class="kanban-card-meta">${esc(j.company)}</div>${j.application_date?`<div class="kanban-card-meta">APPLIED ${esc(fmtDate(j.application_date))}</div>`:''}${j.cv_version?`<div class="kanban-card-meta">CV: ${esc(j.cv_version)}</div>`:''}${j.notes?`<div class="kanban-card-meta next-action">${esc(j.notes)}</div>`:''}</button>`).join(''):'<div class="empty">EMPTY</div>'}</div>`;}).join('');e.querySelectorAll('[data-app-job]').forEach(x=>x.onclick=()=>openJob(Number(x.dataset.appJob)).catch(handleError));}

async function loadSources(){const s=await fetchSources();const active=s.filter(x=>x.state==='active').length;const planned=s.filter(x=>x.state!=='active'&&x.state!=='disabled').length;document.getElementById('sourceCoverageSummary').textContent=`${active} ACTIVE / ${planned} PLANNED OR PENDING / ${s.length} REGISTERED`;const e=document.getElementById('sourcesTable');e.innerHTML=header(['SOURCE','MARKET','METHOD','PRIORITY','STATE','LIVE','SEEN','LAST SUCCESS','NEXT POLL','LAST ERROR / NOTE'])+s.map(x=>`<div class="register-row data"><div class="register-cell"><strong>${esc(x.name.toUpperCase())}</strong></div><div class="register-cell">${esc((x.market||'--').toUpperCase())}</div><div class="register-cell">${esc((x.method||x.kind||'--').toUpperCase())}</div><div class="register-cell"><span class="status-stamp ${x.priority==='high'?'bad':x.priority==='low'?'neutral':'warn'}">${esc((x.priority||'normal').toUpperCase())}</span></div><div class="register-cell"><span class="status-stamp ${badgeClass(x.state)}">${esc((x.state||'planned').replaceAll('_',' ').toUpperCase())}</span></div><div class="register-cell">${x.live_jobs??'--'}</div><div class="register-cell">${x.jobs_seen??'--'}</div><div class="register-cell">${esc(fmtDateTime(x.last_success_at))}</div><div class="register-cell">${x.state==='active'?(x.eligible_now?'NOW':esc(fmtDateTime(x.next_eligible_at))):'--'}</div><div class="register-cell">${esc(x.last_error||x.note||'--')}</div></div>`).join('');updateRunEligibility();}

function updateRunEligibility(){const active=state.sources.filter(s=>s.state==='active'&&s.enabled);const scope=fieldValue('runScope','all');const selected=scope==='all'?active:active.filter(s=>s.name===scope);const ready=selected.filter(s=>s.eligible_now);const running=document.getElementById('runStatusBadge').textContent.toLowerCase()==='running';document.getElementById('runEligibilityText').textContent=ready.length?`${ready.length}/${selected.length} SELECTED SOURCE(S) AVAILABLE NOW`:`SELECTED SOURCE(S) IN COOLDOWN`;document.getElementById('runButtonPipeline').disabled=running||!ready.length;document.getElementById('stopRunButton').disabled=!running;}
async function startCollection(){const scope=fieldValue('runScope','all');const sources=scope==='all'?[]:[scope];await api('/api/run',{method:'POST',body:JSON.stringify({limit:Number(fieldValue('runLimit','100')||100),ignore_poll_interval:false,sources})});toast('COLLECTION STARTED');beginRunPolling();await loadPipeline();}
async function stopCollection(){await api('/api/run/stop',{method:'POST',body:'{}'});toast('STOP REQUESTED');}
function elapsed(start,end){if(!start)return '--';const a=new Date(start),b=end?new Date(end):new Date();const sec=Math.max(0,Math.floor((b-a)/1000));return `${Math.floor(sec/60)}m ${sec%60}s`;}
function renderRun(r){const status=r.status||'idle';const b=document.getElementById('runStatusBadge');b.textContent=status.toUpperCase();b.className=`status-stamp ${badgeClass(status)}`;document.getElementById('runPhase').textContent=(r.phase||'IDLE').toUpperCase();document.getElementById('runMeta').innerHTML=[['RUN ID',r.id||'--'],['STARTED',fmtDateTime(r.started_at)],['ELAPSED',elapsed(r.started_at,r.finished_at)],['SCOPE',(r.selected_sources||[]).join(', ').toUpperCase()||'ALL READY']].map(([l,v])=>`<div class="run-meta-item"><span>${esc(l)}</span><strong>${esc(v)}</strong></div>`).join('');const entries=Object.entries(r.sources||{});document.getElementById('runSourceProgress').innerHTML=entries.length?entries.map(([n,s])=>{const st=s.status||'queued';const pct=['success','failed','skipped'].includes(st)?100:st==='running'?55:8;return `<div class="source-progress-row"><span class="source-name">${esc(n)}</span><div class="progress-track"><div class="progress-fill" style="width:${pct}%"></div></div><span class="status-stamp ${badgeClass(st)}">${esc(st.toUpperCase())}${Number.isFinite(s.fetched)?` / ${s.fetched}`:''}</span></div>`;}).join(''):'<div class="empty">NO ACTIVE SOURCE PROCESS.</div>';const logs=(r.logs||[]).slice(-180);const log=document.getElementById('runLogs');log.textContent=logs.length?logs.join('\n'):'NO UI-MANAGED RUN YET.';log.scrollTop=log.scrollHeight;updateRunEligibility();if(status==='running')document.getElementById('stopRunButton').disabled=false;}
async function loadPipeline(){await fetchSources();const r=await api('/api/run/status');renderRun(r);if(r.status==='running')beginRunPolling();}
function beginRunPolling(){clearInterval(state.runTimer);state.runTimer=setInterval(async()=>{try{const r=await api('/api/run/status');renderRun(r);if(r.status!=='running'){clearInterval(state.runTimer);state.runTimer=null;await fetchSources();updateRunEligibility();toast(`RUN ${String(r.status||'finished').toUpperCase()}`,r.status==='failed');}}catch{}},1000);}

async function loadProfile(){const d=await api('/api/profile');state.profile=d.profile;state.settings=d.settings;state.reviewStatuses=d.review_statuses||['unreviewed','shortlisted','dismissed'];state.applicationStatuses=d.application_statuses||[];const p=d.profile||{},s=d.settings||{},w=s.matching?.overall_weights||{},caps=s.matching?.ranking_caps||{};document.getElementById('profileSummary').innerHTML=`<div class="profile-block"><h3>IDENTITY / RESIDENCE</h3><div class="profile-record"><strong>${esc((p.name||'').toUpperCase())}</strong>${esc((p.country_of_residence||'').toUpperCase())}</div></div><div class="profile-block"><h3>LANGUAGES</h3><div class="tag-list">${Object.entries(p.languages||{}).map(([l,v])=>`<span class="badge info">${esc(l)} ${esc(v)}</span>`).join('')}</div><div class="helper rule-helper">MANDATORY LANGUAGES NOT LISTED HERE ARE AUTOMATICALLY BLOCKING.</div></div><div class="profile-block"><h3>JOB TYPES</h3>${renderJobTypeRules(p)}</div><div class="profile-block"><h3>SENIORITY</h3><div class="rule"><span>ALLOWED</span><strong>${esc((p.seniority?.allowed||[]).join(', '))}</strong></div><div class="rule"><span>DISALLOWED</span><strong>${esc((p.seniority?.disallowed||[]).join(', '))}</strong></div></div><div class="profile-block"><h3>SKILLS / TARGET TERMS</h3><div class="tag-list">${(p.skills||[]).map(x=>`<span class="badge neutral">${esc(x)}</span>`).join('')}</div></div>`;document.getElementById('settingsSummary').innerHTML=`<div class="profile-block"><h3>GEOGRAPHY</h3><div class="rule-list"><div class="rule"><span>LOCAL COUNTRY</span><strong>${esc(p.geography?.local_country||'--')}</strong></div><div class="rule"><span>INTERNATIONAL ON-SITE</span><strong>${p.geography?.international_onsite?'ALLOW':'REJECT'}</strong></div><div class="rule"><span>WORLDWIDE REMOTE</span><strong>${p.geography?.remote_worldwide?'ALLOW':'REJECT'}</strong></div><div class="rule"><span>RELOCATION</span><strong>${p.geography?.relocation?'ALLOW':'REJECT'}</strong></div></div></div><div class="profile-block"><h3>SCORE POLICY</h3><div class="rule-list"><div class="rule"><span>SHORTLIST THRESHOLD</span><strong>${s.matching?.shortlist_threshold??65}/100</strong></div><div class="rule"><span>TECHNICAL</span><strong>${Math.round((w.technical||0)*100)}%</strong></div><div class="rule"><span>ELIGIBILITY</span><strong>${Math.round((w.eligibility||0)*100)}%</strong></div><div class="rule"><span>LOCATION</span><strong>${Math.round((w.location||0)*100)}%</strong></div><div class="rule"><span>EXPERIENCE</span><strong>${Math.round((w.experience||0)*100)}%</strong></div></div></div><div class="profile-block"><h3>HARD RANKING GATES</h3><div class="rule-list">${Object.entries(caps).map(([k,v])=>`<div class="rule"><span>${esc(pretty(k).toUpperCase())}</span><strong>MAX ${esc(v)}</strong></div>`).join('')}</div></div><div class="profile-block"><h3>GREATER TUNIS ACCEPTED LOCATIONS</h3><div class="tag-list">${(p.geography?.allowed_local_locations||[]).map(x=>`<span class="badge neutral">${esc(x)}</span>`).join('')}</div></div>`;}
function renderObjectRules(obj){return `<div class="rule-list">${Object.entries(obj).map(([k,v])=>{const shown=typeof v==='boolean'?(v?'ACCEPT':'REJECT'):String(v).toUpperCase();return `<div class="rule"><span>${esc(pretty(k).toUpperCase())}</span><strong>${esc(shown)}</strong></div>`;}).join('')}</div>`;}
function renderJobTypeRules(p){return `<div class="rule-list">${Object.entries(p.job_types||{}).map(([k,v])=>`<div class="rule"><span>${esc(pretty(k).toUpperCase())}</span><strong>${v?'ACCEPT':'REJECT'}</strong></div>`).join('')}</div>`;}

function openProfileEditor(){
  const p=structuredClone(state.profile||{}),s=state.settings||{},w=s.matching?.overall_weights||{};
  const jobTypes=Object.entries(p.job_types||{}).map(([k,v])=>`${k}:${v?'accept':'reject'}`).join('\n');
  const yesNo=(value)=>`<option value="false" ${!value?'selected':''}>REJECT / NO</option><option value="true" ${value?'selected':''}>ALLOW / YES</option>`;
  document.getElementById('profileModalBody').innerHTML=`
    <div class="profile-editor-grid">
      <label class="full"><span>LANGUAGES (ONE PER LINE: LANGUAGE:LEVEL)</span><textarea id="pfLanguages">${esc(Object.entries(p.languages||{}).map(([k,v])=>`${k}:${v}`).join('\n'))}</textarea></label>
      <label class="full"><span>JOB TYPES (ONE PER LINE: KEY:ACCEPT OR KEY:REJECT)</span><textarea id="pfJobTypes">${esc(jobTypes)}</textarea></label>
      <label><span>ALLOWED SENIORITIES (ONE PER LINE)</span><textarea id="pfAllowedSeniorities">${esc((p.seniority?.allowed||[]).join('\n'))}</textarea></label>
      <label><span>DISALLOWED SENIORITIES (ONE PER LINE)</span><textarea id="pfDisallowedSeniorities">${esc((p.seniority?.disallowed||[]).join('\n'))}</textarea></label>
      <label><span>MAX REQUIRED EXPERIENCE YEARS</span><input id="pfMaxYears" type="number" min="0" max="20" value="${p.seniority?.max_required_years??2}" /></label>
      <label><span>WORLDWIDE REMOTE</span><select id="pfRemoteWorldwide">${yesNo(Boolean(p.geography?.remote_worldwide))}</select></label>
      <label><span>INTERNATIONAL ON-SITE</span><select id="pfInternationalOnsite">${yesNo(Boolean(p.geography?.international_onsite))}</select></label>
      <label><span>RELOCATION</span><select id="pfRelocation">${yesNo(Boolean(p.geography?.relocation))}</select></label>
      <label class="full"><span>GREATER TUNIS LOCATIONS (ONE PER LINE)</span><textarea id="pfLocations">${esc((p.geography?.allowed_local_locations||[]).join('\n'))}</textarea></label>
      <label class="full"><span>SKILLS (ONE PER LINE)</span><textarea id="pfSkills">${esc((p.skills||[]).join('\n'))}</textarea></label>
      <label class="full"><span>TARGET KEYWORDS (ONE PER LINE)</span><textarea id="pfKeywords">${esc((p.target_keywords||[]).join('\n'))}</textarea></label>
      <label><span>SHORTLIST THRESHOLD</span><input id="pfThreshold" type="number" min="0" max="100" value="${s.matching?.shortlist_threshold??65}" /></label>
      <label><span>TECHNICAL WEIGHT %</span><input id="pfTech" type="number" value="${Math.round((w.technical||.45)*100)}" /></label>
      <label><span>ELIGIBILITY WEIGHT %</span><input id="pfElig" type="number" value="${Math.round((w.eligibility||.2)*100)}" /></label>
      <label><span>LOCATION WEIGHT %</span><input id="pfLoc" type="number" value="${Math.round((w.location||.2)*100)}" /></label>
      <label><span>EXPERIENCE WEIGHT %</span><input id="pfExp" type="number" value="${Math.round((w.experience||.15)*100)}" /></label>
    </div>
    <div class="modal-actions"><button class="button primary" id="saveProfileButton">SAVE + RESCORE ALL JOBS</button></div>`;
  document.getElementById('profileModal').classList.remove('hidden');
  document.getElementById('saveProfileButton').onclick=()=>saveProfile().catch(handleError);
}
function lines(id){return document.getElementById(id).value.split(/\r?\n/).map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean);}
async function saveProfile(){
  const p=structuredClone(state.profile||{});
  p.languages=Object.fromEntries(lines('pfLanguages').map(x=>{const i=x.indexOf(':');return i>0?[x.slice(0,i).trim(),x.slice(i+1).trim()]:[x,''];}));
  p.job_types=Object.fromEntries(lines('pfJobTypes').map(x=>{const i=x.indexOf(':');const key=(i>0?x.slice(0,i):x).trim();const val=(i>0?x.slice(i+1):'accept').trim().toLowerCase();return [key,!['reject','no','false','0'].includes(val)];}));
  p.seniority=p.seniority||{};
  p.seniority.allowed=lines('pfAllowedSeniorities');
  p.seniority.disallowed=lines('pfDisallowedSeniorities');
  p.seniority.max_required_years=Number(document.getElementById('pfMaxYears').value||2);
  p.skills=lines('pfSkills');
  p.target_keywords=lines('pfKeywords');
  p.geography=p.geography||{};
  p.geography.allowed_local_locations=lines('pfLocations');
  p.geography.remote_worldwide=document.getElementById('pfRemoteWorldwide').value==='true';
  p.geography.international_onsite=document.getElementById('pfInternationalOnsite').value==='true';
  p.geography.relocation=document.getElementById('pfRelocation').value==='true';
  const weights={technical:Number(document.getElementById('pfTech').value)/100,eligibility:Number(document.getElementById('pfElig').value)/100,location:Number(document.getElementById('pfLoc').value)/100,experience:Number(document.getElementById('pfExp').value)/100};
  const d=await api('/api/profile',{method:'PATCH',body:JSON.stringify({profile:p,matching:{shortlist_threshold:Number(document.getElementById('pfThreshold').value),overall_weights:weights},collection:{}})});
  toast(`PROFILE SAVED / ${d.rescored_jobs} JOBS RESCORED`);
  document.getElementById('profileModal').classList.add('hidden');
  await loadProfile();
}


async function refreshCurrent(){if(state.view==='overview')return loadOverview();if(state.view==='jobs')return loadJobs();if(state.view==='applications')return loadApplications();if(state.view==='sources')return loadSources();if(state.view==='pipeline')return loadPipeline();if(state.view==='profile')return loadProfile();}
function scheduleJobs(){clearTimeout(state.jobsDebounce);state.jobsDebounce=setTimeout(()=>{state.jobsPage=1;loadJobs().catch(handleError);},220);}
async function bootstrap(){
  try{
    const [h,p,s]=await Promise.all([api('/api/health'),api('/api/profile'),api('/api/sources')]);
    state.bootstrapRetries=0;
    const dot=byId('connectionDot');
    const connection=byId('connectionText');
    const version=byId('appVersion');
    if(dot){dot.classList.remove('offline');dot.classList.add('online');}
    if(connection)connection.textContent='LOCAL API / ONLINE';
    if(version)version.textContent=`VERSION ${h.version}`;
    state.profile=p.profile;
    state.settings=p.settings;
    state.reviewStatuses=p.review_statuses||[];
    state.applicationStatuses=p.application_statuses||[];
    state.sources=s.items||[];
    populateSourceFilter();
    await loadOverview();
    const r=await api('/api/run/status');
    renderRun(r);
    if(r.status==='running')beginRunPolling();
  }catch(e){
    const dot=byId('connectionDot');
    const connection=byId('connectionText');
    if(dot){dot.classList.remove('online');dot.classList.add('offline');}
    if(connection)connection.textContent='LOCAL API / OFFLINE';
    state.bootstrapRetries=(state.bootstrapRetries||0)+1;
    if(state.bootstrapRetries<=12){setTimeout(()=>bootstrap(),500);}
    else{handleError(e);}
  }
}

document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item').forEach(x=>x.onclick=()=>setView(x.dataset.view));document.getElementById('refreshButton').onclick=()=>refreshCurrent().catch(handleError);document.querySelectorAll('.mode-button').forEach(x=>x.onclick=()=>applyJobMode(x.dataset.jobMode));if(byId('jobsSearch'))byId('jobsSearch').oninput=scheduleJobs;['jobsVerdict','jobsReview','jobsLocation','jobsSource','jobsMinScore','jobsSort'].forEach(id=>{const e=byId(id);if(e)e.onchange=scheduleJobs;});document.getElementById('jobsPrev').onclick=()=>{if(state.jobsPage>1){state.jobsPage--;loadJobs().catch(handleError);}};document.getElementById('jobsNext').onclick=()=>{if(state.jobsPage<state.jobsPages){state.jobsPage++;loadJobs().catch(handleError);}};document.getElementById('runButtonPipeline').onclick=()=>startCollection().catch(handleError);document.getElementById('stopRunButton').onclick=()=>stopCollection().catch(handleError);if(byId('runScope'))byId('runScope').onchange=updateRunEligibility;document.getElementById('editProfileButton').onclick=openProfileEditor;document.getElementById('closeJobModal').onclick=()=>document.getElementById('jobModal').classList.add('hidden');document.getElementById('closeProfileModal').onclick=()=>document.getElementById('profileModal').classList.add('hidden');['jobModal','profileModal'].forEach(id=>document.getElementById(id).onclick=e=>{if(e.target.id===id)e.currentTarget.classList.add('hidden');});document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Escape'){document.getElementById('jobModal').classList.add('hidden');document.getElementById('profileModal').classList.add('hidden');}});
bootstrap();
